## LY.CAREER.EL.27 出师征伐：世克应爻师勇兵强可剿伐，应伤世象贼多凶炽莫撄锋；世克应则决胜，世旺动带福神克应克鬼势如破竹，世旺克而无吉神助反被日辰动爻克世虽胜未全功，世衰应旺只可御敌；世化子孙应化官鬼宜假途灭敌，子孙伏世下冲应克应暗渡陈仓；应克世彼赢按兵守候，应旺动克世或鬼煞同克彼合谋夹战；应上加兄弟诡谲、玄武偷营、白虎猖獗

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻克应爻，师勇兵强，可以剿伐；应爻伤世象，贼多势凶，不要触其锋。世克应爻，我必定取胜；世爻旺相发动而带福神克应、克官鬼的，势如破竹，一战就奏凯；世爻旺相克应但没有吉神发动帮助，反而被日辰、动爻克世的，虽然能取胜却没有获得全功；世衰应旺，对方虽然受克，也只可以抵御；（这样）没有得到奇谋善策，不能大规模专门征伐。世变子孙、应变官鬼的，宜用借路灭敌的计策，又不可再战邀功；子孙伏在世爻下面，而冲应、克应的，必须暗渡陈仓。应克世，必定对方赢，暂且按兵守候；应旺而克世的，来势必定不可轻视；应旺相发动克世，或者同旺相的官鬼、凶煞一起来克的，对方必定合谋夹击，机关难测。应爻上如果加兄弟，（对方）非常诡诈；加玄武，必定偷营；加白虎，猖獗突击得很厉害。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self controls the Other, the troops are brave and strong and one can campaign; if the Other injures the Self image, the bandits are many and fierce and one must not touch their edge. If the Self controls the Other, I surely win; if the Self is thriving and moving and carries the fortune spirit, Output, and controls the Other and controls Pressure, the force is like splitting bamboo and one wins in a single battle; if the Self is thriving and controls but no auspicious spirit moves to help, and instead the Day or a moving line controls the Self, though one can win one does not gain complete success; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, though they are controlled, one can only fend them off, and without marvellous plans and good strategies one cannot make large expeditions. If the Self turns into Output and the Other into Pressure, it is fit to destroy the enemy by borrowing a road, and one must not fight again to seek credit; if Output lies hidden under the Self and clashes with or controls the Other, one must secretly cross at Chencang. If the Other controls the Self, they surely win, and for the moment one holds the troops and waits; if the Other is thriving and controls the Self, its force is surely not to be made light of; if the Other is thriving and moving and controls the Self, or thriving Pressure and baleful spirits come to control together, they will surely plot together and attack from both sides, and their stratagems are hard to fathom. If a Peer line is added on the Other, they are extremely crafty; with the Dark Tortoise, they will surely raid the camp; with the White Tiger, they are fiercely and violently aggressive.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 347 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 347*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世克应爻，师勇兵强剿伐。 | 世爻克应爻，师勇兵强，可以剿伐； | If the Self controls the Other, the troops are brave and strong and one can campaign; |
| 应伤世象，贼多凶炽莫撄锋。 | 应爻伤世象，贼多势凶，不要触其锋。 | if the Other injures the Self image, the bandits are many and fierce and one must not touch their edge. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 348–350 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 348–350*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如世克应爻者，则我决胜。 | 世克应爻，我必定取胜。 | If the Self controls the Other, I surely win. |
| 世若旺动而带福神克应，克鬼者，则席卷长驱、势如破竹，一战而奏凯矣！ | 世爻旺相发动而带福神克应、克官鬼的，势如破竹，一战就奏凯。 | If the Self is thriving and moving and carries the fortune spirit, Output, and controls the Other and controls Pressure, the force is like splitting bamboo and one wins in a single battle. |
| 世若旺克而不带吉神动助，反被日辰动爻克世者，虽能取胜未获全功。 | 世爻旺相克应但没有吉神发动帮助，反而被日辰、动爻克世的，虽然能取胜却没有获得全功。 | If the Self is thriving and controls but no auspicious spirit moves to help, and instead the Day or a moving line controls the Self, though one can win one does not gain complete success. |
| 若世衰应旺，彼虽受克，只可御敌而已。 | 世衰应旺，对方虽然受克，也只可以抵御。 | If the Self is weak and the Other thriving, though they are controlled, one can only fend them off. |
| 未得奇谋善策大专征伐。 | （这样）没有得到奇谋善策，不能大规模专门征伐。 | Without marvellous plans and good strategies one cannot make large expeditions. |
| 若世化子孙应化官鬼者，宜以假途灭敌，又不可复战邀功，若子孙伏于世下而冲应克应者，必须暗渡陈仓。 | 世变子孙、应变官鬼的，宜用借路灭敌的计策，又不可再战邀功；子孙伏在世爻下面，而冲应、克应的，必须暗渡陈仓。 | If the Self turns into Output and the Other into Pressure, it is fit to destroy the enemy by borrowing a road, and one must not fight again to seek credit; if Output lies hidden under the Self and clashes with or controls the Other, one must secretly cross at Chencang. |
| 如应克世者，必定彼赢，暂且按兵守候。 | 应克世，必定对方赢，暂且按兵守候。 | If the Other controls the Self, they surely win, and for the moment one holds the troops and waits. |
| 应若旺而克世者，其势决识。 | 应旺而克世的，来势必定不可轻视（原文「决识」）。 | If the Other is thriving and controls the Self, its force is surely not to be made light of (the text reads 'jue shi'). |
| 应旺而动克世或同旺相鬼煞来克者，彼必合谋而夹战，机关匡测。 | 应旺相发动克世，或者同旺相的官鬼、凶煞一起来克的，对方必定合谋夹击，机关难测。 | If the Other is thriving and moving and controls the Self, or thriving Pressure and baleful spirits come to control together, they will surely plot together and attack from both sides, and their stratagems are hard to fathom. |
| 应上若加兄弟非常诡谲，加玄武必定偷营，加白虎，猖獗突甚。 | 应爻上如果加兄弟，（对方）非常诡诈；加玄武，必定偷营；加白虎，猖獗突击得很厉害。 | If a Peer line is added on the Other, they are extremely crafty; with the Dark Tortoise, they will surely raid the camp; with the White Tiger, they are fiercely and violently aggressive. |

> **按**:「席卷长驱」「势如破竹」是势头猛烈、一路取胜；「假途灭敌」（假道伐虢）是借路灭掉敌人，「暗渡陈仓」是明里修栈道、暗里绕道偷袭，这两个典故书里自己写出来了。「其势决识」文字有误，疑是「其势炽盛」一类，译文按「不可轻视」译并标出原文；「机关匡测」的「匡」是「叵」（不可）。第四、五句「世衰应旺……只可御敌而已，未得奇谋善策大专征伐」连读：世弱应强时只能防御。
>
> **Note:** 'Xi juan chang qu' and 'shi ru po zhu' mean a fierce momentum and winning all the way; 'jia tu mie di' (borrowing the road to destroy Guo) is destroying the enemy by borrowing a passage, and 'an du Chencang' is openly repairing the plank road while secretly going round to raid, both allusions the book writes out itself. 'Qi shi jue shi' is defective, presumably something like 'its force is blazing', and the translation reads 'not to be made light of' and marks the original; the 'kuang' of 'jiguan kuang ce' is 'po', cannot. The fourth and fifth sentences, 'if the Self is weak and the Other thriving ... one can only fend them off, and without marvellous plans and good strategies one cannot make large expeditions', read on together: when the Self is weak and the Other strong one can only defend.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.26、LY.CAREER.EL.28、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.YY.55、LY.CAREER.HJC.86、LY.CAREER.HJC.93、LY.CAREER.HJC.94

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.28 出师征伐：杀不克身值交重无大害，杀如伤世虽居安静且休征；杀即大煞（劫煞亡神小白虎大白虎，官鬼亦作杀神）；诸煞旺动不克身世无大害，不旺不动而所克害身世必生祸，旺动克身世祸骤来；杀并日辰冲克静世名暗动，有阴谋窃劫之患；大煞劫煞亡神小白虎大白虎的起例

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:煞不克身，即使重叠发动也没有大害；煞如果伤世，虽然在安静中也暂且不要出征。「杀」就是大煞，有劫煞，也有亡神，又有小白虎、大白虎，官鬼也当作杀神。如果各煞或旺相或发动、但不克身爻、世爻，必定没有大害；如果不旺不动，而所克的（爻）有害于身爻、世爻，必定生祸，要谨慎提防；如果各煞或旺相或发动来克身、世，祸会骤然来到，绝非小事。如果煞和日辰一起冲克安静的世爻，叫作暗动，必有阴谋窃劫的祸患；想察觉什么时候来害，须推官鬼和煞生旺的时期。所以经书说「福来而不知，祸来而不觉」，正是说这个。大煞、劫煞的起例已经注在前面；亡神煞的起例说：正月起亥顺行十二位；小白虎，就是庚、辛日起白虎；大白虎，是正月起申顺行十二位。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a baleful spirit does not control the Body, even if it moves in double there is no great harm; if a baleful spirit injures the Self, even if it is still, for the time being do not set out. 'Sha' is the great baleful spirit, and there are Robbery, the Lost Spirit, the Lesser White Tiger and the Greater White Tiger, and Pressure too is counted as a baleful spirit. If the various baleful spirits are thriving or moving but do not control the Body and Self, there is surely no great harm; if they are neither thriving nor moving but what they control does harm to the Body and Self, disaster surely arises and one must guard carefully; if the baleful spirits are thriving or moving and come to control the Body and Self, the disaster comes suddenly and is no small matter. If a baleful spirit joins the Day in clashing with and controlling a still Self, it is called secret motion, and there will surely be the danger of plots and plunder; to find out when the harm will come, work out the period when Pressure and the baleful spirit are born or thriving. So the classic says 'fortune comes and one does not know, disaster comes and one does not feel', which says exactly this. The setting out of the great baleful spirit and Robbery is noted earlier; the setting out of the Lost Spirit says: from the first month start at Hai and go through the twelve places; the Lesser White Tiger is the White Tiger starting on Geng and Xin days; the Greater White Tiger starts at Shen in the first month and goes through the twelve places.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 351 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 351*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 杀不克身，值交重无大害。 | 煞不克身，逢重叠发动也没有大害； | If a baleful spirit does not control the Body, even if it moves in double there is no great harm; |
| 杀如伤世，虽居安静且休征。 | 煞如果伤世，虽然在安静中也暂且不要出征。 | if a baleful spirit injures the Self, even if it is still, for the time being do not set out. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 352 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 352*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫杀者，大煞也，有劫煞、也有亡神、又有小白虎、大白虎，官鬼亦作杀神也。 | 「杀」就是大煞，有劫煞，也有亡神，又有小白虎、大白虎，官鬼也当作杀神。 | 'Sha' is the great baleful spirit, and there are Robbery, the Lost Spirit, the Lesser White Tiger and the Greater White Tiger, and Pressure too is counted as a baleful spirit. |
| 若诸杀或旺或动不克身世者，决无大害，若不旺不动而所克，害于身世者，必然生祸，谨慎提防。 | 如果各煞或旺相或发动、但不克身爻、世爻，必定没有大害；如果不旺不动，而所克的（爻）有害于身爻、世爻，必定生祸，要谨慎提防。 | If the various baleful spirits are thriving or moving but do not control the Body and Self, there is surely no great harm; if they are neither thriving nor moving but what they control does harm to the Body and Self, disaster surely arises and one must guard carefully. |
| 若诸杀或旺或动来克身世者，其祸骤来决非小可。 | 如果各煞或旺相或发动来克身、世，祸会骤然来到，绝非小事。 | If the baleful spirits are thriving or moving and come to control the Body and Self, the disaster comes suddenly and is no small matter. |
| 若杀并日辰冲克静世者，名为暗动，必有阴谋窃劫之患，欲察何时来害，须推鬼煞生旺之期。 | 如果煞和日辰一起冲克安静的世爻，叫作暗动，必有阴谋窃劫的祸患；想察觉什么时候来害，须推官鬼和煞生旺的时期。 | If a baleful spirit joins the Day in clashing with and controlling a still Self, it is called secret motion, and there will surely be the danger of plots and plunder; to find out when the harm will come, work out the period when Pressure and the baleful spirit are born or thriving. |
| 故经云福来而不知，祸来而不觉，正此之谓也。 | 所以经书说「福来而不知，祸来而不觉」，正是说这个。 | So the classic says 'fortune comes and one does not know, disaster comes and one does not feel', which says exactly this. |
| 大煞、劫煞，例注在前，亡神杀例云：正月起亥顺行十二位是也。 | 大煞、劫煞的起例已经注在前面；亡神煞的起例说：正月起亥顺行十二位。 | The setting out of the great baleful spirit and Robbery is noted earlier; the setting out of the Lost Spirit says: from the first month start at Hai and go through the twelve places. |
| 小白虎，即庚辛日起白虎是也。 | 小白虎，就是庚、辛日起白虎（的位置）。 | The Lesser White Tiger is the White Tiger starting on Geng and Xin days. |
| 大白虎者，正月起申顺行十二位是也。 | 大白虎，是正月起申顺行十二位。 | The Greater White Tiger starts at Shen in the first month and goes through the twelve places. |

> **按**:「身世」是身爻和世爻。「暗动」是静爻被日辰冲动（见术语表）。「小白虎」「大白虎」是两种神煞，书里写了起例：小白虎庚辛日起白虎（日干在庚、辛时白虎位置的算法），大白虎正月起申顺行。第二个「若诸杀」那一句里「不旺不动而所克」的主语省略，译文按文意写成「所克的（爻）」。这一条与易隐征战占「彼己」里的「鬼暗动加大杀劫杀刑刃克世防刺客」（YY.56）取同一件事。
>
> **Note:** 'Shishen' is the Body and Self lines. 'Secret motion' is a still line clashed into motion by the Day (see the glossary). The 'Lesser White Tiger' and the 'Greater White Tiger' are two spirit-stars whose setting out the book writes: the Lesser starts on Geng and Xin days (the rule for the position of the White Tiger when the day stem is Geng or Xin), and the Greater starts at Shen in the first month. The subject of 'neither thriving nor moving but what it controls' in the second sentence is left out, and the translation follows the sense as 'what it controls'. This entry takes up the same matter as 'if Pressure moves in secret with a great baleful spirit, Robbery, punishment and the blade and controls the Self, guard against assassins' in the Yi Yin 'them and us' section (YY.56).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.27、LY.CAREER.EL.29、LY.CAREER.YY.56、LY.CAREER.HJC.109

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.29 出师征伐：世陷我军有难、应空贼寇罹危；世为主将宜雄强对垒不宜空陷；值旬空月破不可妄自争先，先则有害、迟则有益；世居阳象兵贵神速速行大利

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻陷落，我军有难；应爻落空，贼寇陷于危险。世是主将，要靠雄强来对垒折冲，哪里适宜落空陷落？如果值旬空，或者临月破的宫位，不可妄自争先，争先就有害，迟缓就有益。单爻属阳，发动成为老阳，主过去的缘由；又说阳主迅速，所以世居阳象，用兵贵在神速，迅速行动大利，迟缓就大害。这是大道理，所以不再琐碎细说。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self sinks, my army is in trouble; if the Other is Void, the bandits fall into danger. The Self is the commander, and must face the enemy by strength and stoutness; how can it be fit for it to be Void or sunk? If it meets decade Void or sits in a Month-break palace, one must not recklessly press ahead, for pressing ahead brings harm and delay brings benefit. A single line that is yang becomes old yang when it moves and governs the reasons of the past; again it is said that yang governs speed, so when the Self sits on a yang image, in using troops swiftness is prized, and acting swiftly is greatly favourable and delay greatly harmful. This is a great principle, so it is not detailed trivially.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 353 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 353*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世陷，我军有难。 | 世爻陷落，我军有难； | If the Self sinks, my army is in trouble; |
| 应空，贼寇罹危。 | 应爻落空，贼寇陷于危险。 | if the Other is Void, the bandits fall into danger. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 354 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 354*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡世为主将，惟伏雄强对垒折衡，岂宜空陷于？ | 凡是世爻为主将，要靠雄强来对垒折冲，哪里适宜落空陷落？ | Whenever the Self is the commander, he must face the enemy by strength and stoutness; how can it be fit for it to be Void or sunk? |
| 若值旬空，或临月破宫，不可妄自争先，先则有害，犀则有益。 | 如果值旬空，或者临月破的宫位，不可妄自争先，争先就有害，迟缓就有益（「犀」疑是「迟」）。 | If it meets decade Void or sits in a Month-break palace, one must not recklessly press ahead, for pressing ahead brings harm and delay brings benefit ('xi' is presumably 'chi'). |
| 单爻属阳，发为老阳，而主过去之由，又云阳主迅速，故世居阳象，兵贵神速，速行大利，犀则大害。 | 单爻属阳，发动成为老阳，主过去的缘由；又说阳主迅速，所以世居阳象，用兵贵在神速，迅速行动大利，迟缓就大害（「犀」疑是「迟」）。 | A single line that is yang becomes old yang when it moves and governs the reasons of the past; again it is said that yang governs speed, so when the Self sits on a yang image, in using troops swiftness is prized, and acting swiftly is greatly favourable and delay greatly harmful ('xi' is presumably 'chi'). |
| 此系大理，故不琐耳。 | 这是大道理，所以不再琐碎细说。 | This is a great principle, so it is not detailed trivially. |

> **按**:「犀」在第二、三句里两次出现，和「先」对着，应是「迟」（迟则有益、迟则大害）；译文按「迟缓」译并标出。「老阳」是阳爻发动的叫法（老阳变阴）。「折衡」即「折冲」，抵挡敌人。「惟伏」疑是「惟须」，译文按文意译。这一条与易隐征战占「世空，我有难；应空，彼必伤」（YY.54）取同一件事，但这里多了「不可争先」。
>
> **Note:** 'Xi' appears twice, in the second and third sentences, set against 'xian', ahead, and is presumably 'chi', slow (delay brings benefit, delay brings great harm); the translation reads 'delay' and marks it. 'Old yang' is the name for a yang line that moves (old yang changes into yin). 'Zhe heng' is 'zhe chong', turning back the enemy. 'Wei fu' is presumably 'wei xu', one must, and is translated by sense. This entry takes up the same matter as 'if the Self is Void I am in trouble; if the Other is Void they will surely be hurt' in the Yi Yin 'them and us' section (YY.54), but adds 'one must not press ahead'.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.28、LY.CAREER.EL.30、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.HJC.100

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.30 出师征伐：父乃旌旗舒卷凭动静，财为粮草盈虚决于兴衰；父旺相带青龙绣金华丽、加朱雀书彩鲜明、旺动高张大旗，衰墓逢白虎腐敝，空绝则少无；三军未发粮草先行，财旺兵粮丰盛，玄武兴防偷窃、兄弟兴抢夺，财衰墓粮不多，卦无财子化财必借贷邻邦，空绝则失无

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父爻是旌旗，舒展卷收要凭它的动静；财爻是粮草，盈满亏缺可以由它的兴衰来判断。营中的旌旗以父爻为用爻，旺相又带青龙，就绣金华丽；加朱雀，就色彩鲜明；旺相又发动，就大旗高张、导引飘飘。如果衰、墓而逢白虎，就腐朽破旧；（父爻）空而又绝，旗帜或少或没有，即使有也必然损坏；只要得到安静，世事就都安宁了。谚语说：三军未发，粮草先行，所以卦中的妻财当作车马的粮草：财爻旺相，是兵粮丰盛、马草有余；玄武兴起，要谨防偷窃；兄弟兴起，必定抢夺；如果财爻衰、墓，粮不多、草不肥；卦内没有财，如果有子孙变出财爻，必去邻邦借贷；这一爻如果空、绝，粮草就或者丢失或者没有。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Resource line is the banners, and whether they are unfurled or furled depends on its motion or stillness; Wealth is the grain and fodder, and fullness and lack can be decided by its flourishing or decline. The banners in the camp take the Resource line as the Subject line; if it is thriving and carries the Azure Dragon, they are embroidered with gold and splendid; with the Vermilion Bird added, the colours are bright and clear; thriving and moving, great banners are raised high and wave about to lead the way. If it is weak or in tomb and meets the White Tiger, they are rotten and worn; if Void and extinct as well, the banners are few or none, and even if there are some they are sure to be damaged; if it is only still, all will be at peace. The proverb says: before the three armies set out, grain and fodder go ahead, so the Wealth in the hexagram is taken as the grain and fodder of carts and horses: if the Wealth line is thriving, the troops' grain is plentiful and the horses' fodder has surplus; if the Dark Tortoise rises, guard against theft; if Peer rises, there will surely be plunder; if Wealth is weak or in tomb, the grain is not much and the fodder not fat; if the hexagram has no Wealth and Output turns into a Wealth line, one must go to a neighbouring state to borrow; if this line is Void or extinct, the grain and fodder are either lost or lacking.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 355 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 355*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父乃旌旗，舒卷当凭乎动静。 | 父爻是旌旗，舒展卷收要凭它的动静； | The Resource line is the banners, and whether they are unfurled or furled depends on its motion or stillness; |
| 财为粮草，盈虚可决于兴衰。 | 财爻是粮草，盈满亏缺可以由它的兴衰来判断。 | Wealth is the grain and fodder, and fullness and lack can be decided by its flourishing or decline. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 356 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 356*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 营中旌旗以父为用爻，旺相带表青龙，则绣金华丽。 | 营中的旌旗以父爻为用爻，旺相又带青龙，就绣金华丽。 | The banners in the camp take the Resource line as the Subject line; if it is thriving and carries the Azure Dragon, they are embroidered with gold and splendid. |
| 加朱雀则书彩鲜明，旺而又动，则高张大旗导引飘飘。 | 加朱雀，就色彩鲜明；旺相又发动，就大旗高张、导引飘飘。 | With the Vermilion Bird added, the colours are bright and clear; thriving and moving, great banners are raised high and wave about to lead the way. |
| 若衰墓而逢白虎，则腐壤敝旧。 | 如果衰、墓而逢白虎，就腐朽破旧。 | If it is weak or in tomb and meets the White Tiger, they are rotten and worn. |
| 空而又绝，或少或无纵有必然损破，但得安静，则世熊咸宁矣！ | （父爻）空而又绝，旗帜或少或没有，即使有也必然损坏；只要得到安静，世事就都安宁了。 | If it is Void and extinct as well, the banners are few or none, and even if there are some they are sure to be damaged; if it is only still, all will be at peace. |
| 谚云：三军未发，粮草先行。 | 谚语说：三军未发，粮草先行。 | The proverb says: before the three armies set out, grain and fodder go ahead. |
| 故卦中之妻财，作车马之粮草。 | 所以卦中的妻财当作车马的粮草。 | So the Wealth in the hexagram is taken as the grain and fodder of carts and horses. |
| 如财爻旺相，是兵粮丰盛，马草盈余。 | 如果财爻旺相，是兵粮丰盛、马草有余。 | If the Wealth line is thriving, the troops' grain is plentiful and the horses' fodder has surplus. |
| 玄武兴，谨防偷窃。 | 玄武兴起，要谨防偷窃。 | If the Dark Tortoise rises, guard against theft. |
| 兄弟兴，决然抢夺。 | 兄弟兴起，必定抢夺。 | If Peer rises, there will surely be plunder. |
| 若财爻衰墓，粮不多而草不肥。 | 如果财爻衰、墓，粮不多、草不肥。 | If the Wealth line is weak or in tomb, the grain is not much and the fodder not fat. |
| 卦内无财，若得子化财爻，必去粼邦借贷。 | 卦内没有财，如果有子孙变出财爻，必去邻邦借贷。 | If the hexagram has no Wealth and Output turns into a Wealth line, one must go to a neighbouring state to borrow. |
| 此象若空若绝，粮草或失或无。 | 这一爻如果空、绝，粮草就或者丢失或者没有。 | If this line is Void or extinct, the grain and fodder are either lost or lacking. |

> **按**:这一条和易隐征战占「六亲」里的旌旗（YY.58）、粮草（YY.59）取法相同，只是这里更详细地写了旌旗的色彩和粮草的盈虚。第四句「世熊咸宁」疑是「世态咸宁」，译作「世事就都安宁」；「粼邦」是「邻邦」。「导引飘飘」是大旗在前引导、飘扬。
>
> **Note:** This entry takes the same view as the banners (YY.58) and provisions (YY.59) in the Yi Yin war divination 'six kin' section, only describing in more detail the colours of the banners and the fullness of the provisions. In the fourth sentence 'shi xiong xian ning' is presumably 'shi tai xian ning', translated 'all will be at peace'; 'lin bang' is 'lin bang', neighbouring state. 'Dao yin piaopiao' is the great banners leading in front and fluttering.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.29、LY.CAREER.EL.31、LY.CAREER.YY.58、LY.CAREER.YY.59、LY.CAREER.HJC.87、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

## LY.CAREER.EL.31 出师征伐：子作先锋旺则强衰则弱，兄为伏寇现则有空则无；子孙旺相智勇两全、带吉神克应则冒险衡锋争先首捷，衰墓则柔懦无谋；兄弟好佞为埋伏之贼，冲动须防暗害、推生旺日时为期，绝无则不必虑

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙当先锋，旺相就强，衰弱就弱；兄弟是埋伏的贼，出现就有，落空就没有。子孙是现有的雄兵，这是当先锋的说法；子孙旺相，必定是智勇两全的将领，如果带吉神而能克应爻，就能冒险冲锋、争先首捷，唾手成功；子孙如果遇到衰、墓，就柔弱怯懦无谋，难以胜任。兄弟是奸佞之神，就是埋伏的贼，如果逢冲动，必定有埋伏的兵，须防暗害；想知道什么时候来犯，必推它生旺的日子、时辰为期；如果绝、没有，就不必忧虑了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output acts as the vanguard, strong if thriving and weak if declining; Peer is the hidden bandit, there if it appears and absent if Void. Output is the troops that are present, which is the argument for acting as the vanguard; if it is thriving, it is surely a general complete in both wit and courage, and if it carries an auspicious spirit and can control the Other, it can risk a charge, be first to press ahead and win the first success as easily as spitting on the palm; if Output meets weak or tomb, it is soft, timid and without plans and cannot fill the post. Peer is a crafty and flattering spirit, the bandit lying in ambush, and if it meets a clash into motion there are surely troops in ambush and one must guard against secret harm; to know when they will attack, work out the day or hour it is born or thriving as the term; if it is extinct or absent, there is no need to worry.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 357 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 357*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子作先锋，旺则强，而衰则弱。 | 子孙当先锋，旺相就强，衰弱就弱； | Output acts as the vanguard, strong if thriving and weak if declining; |
| 兄为伏寇，现则有，而空则无。 | 兄弟是埋伏的贼，出现就有，落空就没有。 | Peer is the hidden bandit, there if it appears and absent if Void. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 358 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 358*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙为现之雄兵，是当先锋之论。 | 子孙是现有的雄兵，这是当先锋的说法。 | Output is the troops that are present, which is the argument for acting as the vanguard. |
| 若逢旺相，必智勇两全之将，若带吉神而能克应者，则冒险衡锋争先首捷唾手可成功矣！ | 如果逢旺相，必定是智勇两全的将领；如果带吉神而能克应爻，就能冒险冲锋、争先首捷，唾手成功。 | If it meets thriving, it is surely a general complete in both wit and courage; if it carries an auspicious spirit and can control the Other, it can risk a charge, be first to press ahead and win the first success as easily as spitting on the palm. |
| 子孙倘遇衰墓，则柔懦无谋，难充斯任。 | 子孙如果遇到衰、墓，就柔弱怯懦无谋，难以胜任。 | If Output meets weak or tomb, it is soft, timid and without plans and cannot fill the post. |
| 兄弟乃好佞之神，即为埋伏之贼，若逢冲动，必在埋伏之兵，须防暗害，欲知何时有犯，必推生旺日时为期，若绝若无，则不必虑矣！ | 兄弟是奸佞之神，就是埋伏的贼，如果逢冲动，必定有埋伏的兵，须防暗害；想知道什么时候来犯，必推它生旺的日子、时辰为期；如果绝、没有，就不必忧虑了。 | Peer is a crafty and flattering spirit, the bandit lying in ambush, and if it meets a clash into motion there are surely troops in ambush and one must guard against secret harm; to know when they will attack, work out the day or hour it is born or thriving as the term; if it is extinct or absent, there is no need to worry. |

> **按**:这一条与易隐征战占「六亲」里的子（先锋）、兄（埋伏）（YY.60、YY.61）取法相同。「唾手」是不费力气。「衡锋」是「冲锋」。
>
> **Note:** This entry takes the same view as Output (vanguard) and Peer (ambush) in the 'six kin' section of the Yi Yin war divination (YY.60, YY.61). 'Tuo shou' is without effort. 'Heng feng' is 'chong feng', charging the front.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.30、LY.CAREER.EL.32、LY.CAREER.YY.60、LY.CAREER.YY.61、LY.CAREER.HJC.90、LY.CAREER.HJC.92

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.32 出师征伐：父母兴隆机关炯炯，妻财发动兵甲纷纷；子孙为兵之用、父母为子之忌，父母旺而兴则机关迭出诡策多端是为伤兵之兆，子旺父衰则将勇兵强；官鬼为彼之用、妻财为鬼之助，财爻一发鬼逞豪强，惟要财静则官鬼孤立自然太平

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父母兴旺，机谋炯炯；妻财发动，兵甲纷纷。子孙是兵的用神，父母是子孙的忌神：如果父母旺相而兴起，就机谋迭出、诡计多端，这是伤兵的征兆；如果子孙旺相、父母衰弱，就是将领勇敢、兵卒强壮，可以有所作为。官鬼是对方的用神，妻财是官鬼的助力：对方本来不兴兵，但财爻一发动，官鬼就逞强，所以士卒纵横、兵戈扰乱；只要财爻安静，官鬼就孤立无助，自然太平了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Resource flourishes, stratagems are bright and clear; if Wealth moves, arms and armour are everywhere in motion. Output is the Subject for the troops and Resource is the Adversary of Output: if Resource is thriving and rises, stratagems pour out and tricks are many, which is a sign of the troops being injured; if Output is thriving and Resource weak, the commander is brave and the troops strong, and one can act. Pressure is the Subject for the enemy and Wealth is the aid of Pressure: the enemy had not meant to raise troops, but as soon as the Wealth line moves Pressure shows its strength, so soldiers swarm and weapons are in disorder; if only Wealth is still, Pressure stands alone without aid and peace comes of itself.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 359 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 359*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母兴隆机关炯炯，妻财发动，兵甲纷纷。 | 父母兴旺，机谋炯炯；妻财发动，兵甲纷纷。 | If Resource flourishes, stratagems are bright and clear; if Wealth moves, arms and armour are everywhere in motion. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 360 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 360*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙为兵之用，父母为子之忌，若父母旺而兴，则机关迭出，诡策多端，是为伤兵之兆。 | 子孙是兵的用神，父母是子孙的忌神：如果父母旺相而兴起，就机谋迭出、诡计多端，这是伤兵的征兆。 | Output is the Subject for the troops and Resource is the Adversary of Output: if Resource is thriving and rises, stratagems pour out and tricks are many, which is a sign of the troops being injured. |
| 若子旺父衰，则将勇兵强可作可为也。 | 如果子孙旺相、父母衰弱，就是将领勇敢、兵卒强壮，可以有所作为。 | If Output is thriving and Resource weak, the commander is brave and the troops strong, and one can act. |
| 官鬼为彼之用，妻财为鬼之助。 | 官鬼是对方的用神，妻财是官鬼的助力。 | Pressure is the Subject for the enemy and Wealth is the aid of Pressure. |
| 彼本不兴兵，财爻一发，则鬼逞豪强，故士卒纵横兵戈扰攘，惟要财静，则官鬼孤立无助，自然太乎矣！ | 对方本来不兴兵，但财爻一发动，官鬼就逞强，所以士卒纵横、兵戈扰乱；只要财爻安静，官鬼就孤立无助，自然太平了！ | The enemy had not meant to raise troops, but as soon as the Wealth line moves Pressure shows its strength, so soldiers swarm and weapons are in disorder; if only Wealth is still, Pressure stands alone without aid and peace comes of itself! |

> **按**:「机关」是机谋、计策。「用神」是占问时被拿来看这件事成败的那个爻，「忌神」是克它的爻（见术语表）。「太乎」疑是「太平」，译作「太平」。这一条和易隐征战占「六亲」里的「财动助鬼」（YY.62）取同一个关系：妻财生官鬼。
>
> **Note:** 'Jiguan' is stratagems and schemes. The 'Subject' is the line taken as the matter asked about, and the 'Adversary' is the line that controls it (see the glossary). 'Tai hu' is presumably 'taiping', peace, and is translated 'peace'. This entry takes the same relation as 'Wealth moves to help Pressure' in the 'six kin' section of the Yi Yin war divination (YY.62): Wealth generates Pressure.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.31、LY.CAREER.EL.33、LY.CAREER.YY.62、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.33 出师征伐：兄象单兴恐有绝粮之患，父爻独发岂无伤卒之忧；兄弟专主奸险，卦中独发旺带白虎则行凶劫掠，衰监玄武必被阴谋抽窃，得日辰克制兄弟则不失脱；父母能克子孙单动则我兵大忌，旺带凶煞决被刑伤，休加吉神则彼势亦不可小视

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:兄象单独兴起，恐怕有断粮的忧患；父爻独自发动，难道没有伤兵卒的忧虑？兄弟专主奸险的事，如果在卦中独自发动，旺相又带白虎，对方就行凶劫掠；衰弱而临玄武，必被阴谋抽窃；如果得到日辰克制兄弟，即使见到有人行劫，也决不会失脱，就像伏着木牛流马一样稳固。父母能克子孙，如果在爻间单独发动，对我的兵大为忌讳，又旺相带凶煞，必定被刑伤；如果休囚又加吉神，对方的势头稍微（强）也不可小看啊。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Peer image rises alone, fear the danger of cut-off grain; if the Resource line moves alone, how can there be no worry of injured soldiers? Peer governs crafty and dangerous matters, and if it moves alone in the hexagram, thriving and carrying the White Tiger, the enemy commits violence and plunder; if weak and sitting on the Dark Tortoise, there will surely be secret plots and pilfering; if the Day controls Peer, even if one sees plundering there will surely be no loss, as firm as if wooden oxen and gliding horses were hidden away. Resource can control Output, and if it moves alone among the lines, it is a great taboo for my troops, and if thriving and carrying baleful spirits, they are surely punished and injured; if it is resting yet carries auspicious spirits, even a slight strength on the enemy's side must not be made light of.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 361 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 361*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄象单兴，恐有绝粮之患。 | 兄象单独兴起，恐怕有断粮的忧患； | If the Peer image rises alone, fear the danger of cut-off grain; |
| 父爻独发，岂无伤卒之忧。 | 父爻独自发动，难道没有伤兵卒的忧虑？ | if the Resource line moves alone, how can there be no worry of injured soldiers? |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 362–364 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 362–364*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄弟专主奸险之事，若在卦中独发，旺带白虎，则彼行凶声劫。 | 兄弟专主奸险的事，如果在卦中独自发动，旺相又带白虎，对方就行凶劫掠。 | Peer governs crafty and dangerous matters, and if it moves alone in the hexagram, thriving and carrying the White Tiger, the enemy commits violence and plunder. |
| 衰监玄武，必被阴谋抽窃若得日辰克制兄弟，虽见行劫决不失脱，乃伏木牛流马之固耳。 | 衰弱而临玄武，必被阴谋抽窃；如果得到日辰克制兄弟，即使见到有人行劫，也决不会失脱，就像伏着木牛流马一样稳固。 | If it is weak and sits on the Dark Tortoise, there will surely be secret plots and pilfering; if the Day controls Peer, even if one sees plundering there will surely be no loss, as firm as if wooden oxen and gliding horses were hidden away. |
| 父母能克子孙，若在爻间单动，则我兵大忌，又旺带凶煞，决被刑伤。 | 父母能克子孙，如果在爻间单独发动，对我的兵大为忌讳，又旺相带凶煞，必定被刑伤。 | Resource can control Output, and if it moves alone among the lines, it is a great taboo for my troops, and if it is thriving and carries baleful spirits, they are surely punished and injured. |
| 若休加吉神，则彼势稍稍亦非小视哉。 | 如果休囚又加吉神，对方的势头稍微（强）也不可小看啊。 | If it is resting yet carries auspicious spirits, even a slight strength on the enemy's side must not be made light of. |

> **按**:第二句在书里被断行隔开（「若得」在上一行末，「日辰克制兄弟」在下一行首），这里连起来读。「木牛流马」是诸葛亮造的运粮工具，这里比喻粮草稳固不失。「行劫声劫」书里写作「行凶声劫」，「声」疑是「抢」，译作「行凶劫掠」。这一条的前半和易隐征战占「兄为埋伏」（YY.61）、「兄动克财督饷侵渔」（YY.59）是同一类断语。
>
> **Note:** The second sentence is split by a line break in the book ('if one gets' at the end of one line and 'the Day controls Peer' at the start of the next) and is read here joined up. 'Wooden oxen and gliding horses' are the grain transport devices that Zhuge Liang made, an image here of provisions held firm and not lost. 'Xing xiong sheng jie' is written as such in the book, and 'sheng' is presumably 'qiang', to snatch; it is translated 'commits violence and plunder'. The first half of this entry is the same kind of judgment as 'Peer is the ambush' (YY.61) and 'a moving Peer controls Wealth and the supply official embezzles' (YY.59) in the Yi Yin war divination.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.32、LY.CAREER.EL.34、LY.CAREER.YY.59、LY.CAREER.YY.61

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.34 出师征伐：福化文书兵必骄而后悔无及，子之兄弟将虽弱而大获全功；父母为子之大忌，子变父则用变忌神，克伐如雪狮向阳初张威势日渐消，大有刑伤噬脐无及；兄弟为子之元神，子化兄则用化元神扶助，如猛虎添翼百战百胜大获全功

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:福神变成文书，兵必定骄傲，后悔也来不及；子孙变成兄弟，将领虽弱，也大获全功。父母是子孙的大忌，如果子孙变父母，就是用爻变成忌神，而克伐就像雪狮向着太阳，起初张扬威势，日渐消融，大有刑伤，噬脐莫及；兄弟是子孙的元神，子孙变兄弟，就是用爻化出元神来扶助，如同猛虎添翼，越发精神，百战百胜，大获全功。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the fortune spirit changes into the document, the troops are surely proud and regret will be too late; if Output changes into Peer, though the commander is weak he wins a great and complete success. Resource is the great Adversary of Output, and if Output turns into Resource, the Subject changes into the Adversary, and the control is like a snow lion facing the sun, at first putting on a show of might and day by day melting away, with much punishment and injury and the regret of biting one's own navel too late; Peer is the Support of Output, and if Output turns into Peer, the Subject changes into its Support to assist, like a fierce tiger adding wings, ever more spirited, winning a hundred battles in a hundred and gaining a great and complete success.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 365 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 365*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 福化文书，兵必骄而后悔无及。 | 福神变成文书，兵必定骄傲，后悔也来不及； | If the fortune spirit, Output, changes into the document, the troops are surely proud and regret will be too late; |
| 子之兄弟，将虽弱而大获全功。 | 子孙变成兄弟，将领虽弱，也大获全功。 | if Output changes into Peer, though the commander is weak he wins a great and complete success. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 366 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 366*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母为子之大忌，若子变父者，乃是用变忌神，而克伐犹雪狮之向阳，初张威势，日渐日苏，大有刑伤噬脐无及矣！ | 父母是子孙的大忌，如果子孙变父母，就是用爻变成忌神，而克伐就像雪狮向着太阳，起初张扬威势，日渐消融，大有刑伤，噬脐莫及！ | Resource is the great Adversary of Output, and if Output turns into Resource, the Subject changes into the Adversary, and the control is like a snow lion facing the sun, at first putting on a show of might and day by day melting away, with much punishment and injury and the regret of biting one's own navel too late! |
| 兄弟为子之元神，得子化兄者，乃是用化元神而扶助，如猛虎之添翼，越有精神百战百胜大获全功矣！ | 兄弟是子孙的元神，子孙变兄弟，就是用爻化出元神来扶助，如同猛虎添翼，越发精神，百战百胜，大获全功！ | Peer is the Support of Output, and if Output turns into Peer, the Subject changes into its Support to assist, like a fierce tiger adding wings, ever more spirited, winning a hundred battles in a hundred and gaining a great and complete success! |

> **按**:「福化文书」的福是福神（子孙），文书是父母；「元神」是生用神的爻（见术语表）。第一句解里的「日渐日苏」疑是「日渐日消」，译作「日渐消融」；「噬脐」是自咬肚脐够不着，比喻后悔也来不及。这里以子孙为兵将的用神，所以子孙变父母（忌神）凶、变兄弟（元神）吉；与易隐征战占「子化兄者将弱而功成，子化父者兵骄而致败」（YY.60）完全一致。
>
> **Note:** In 'fu hua wenshu' the 'fu' is the fortune spirit (Output) and the document is Resource; the 'Support' is the line that generates the Subject (see the glossary). 'Ri jian ri su' in the first explanation is presumably 'ri jian ri xiao', and is translated 'day by day melting away'; 'biting one's navel' is trying to bite one's own navel and not reaching it, an image of regret too late. Here Output is taken as the Subject for the commanders and troops, so Output turning into Resource (the Adversary) is ominous and into Peer (the Support) auspicious, which agrees exactly with 'Output turns into Peer, the commander is weak but succeeds; Output turns into Resource, the troops grow proud and bring defeat' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.60).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.33、LY.CAREER.EL.35、LY.CAREER.YY.60、LY.CAREER.HJC.93

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.35 出师征伐：福遇青龙必出忠良之将，子监白虎当差猛勇之军；子遇青龙必赤胆忠心之捷将，加禄马贵人必智勇谋略之士；子为兵卒临小白虎虽衰亦壮强之卒，临大白虎必骁勇猛烈之雄兵

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:福神遇到青龙，必定出忠良的将领；子孙监临白虎，应当差遣勇猛的军队。子孙遇到青龙，必是赤胆忠心的胜将，再加禄、驿马、贵人，必是智勇谋略之士；子孙是兵卒，如果临小白虎，虽然衰弱也是强壮的兵卒；如果临大白虎，必是骁勇猛烈的雄兵。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the fortune spirit meets the Azure Dragon, loyal and good commanders are sure to appear; if Output sits over the White Tiger, bold and fierce troops should be sent. If Output meets the Azure Dragon, he is a victorious general of true loyalty, and with Emolument, the Horse and the Noble added, he is a man of wit, courage and strategy; Output is the soldiers, and if it sits on the Lesser White Tiger, though weak they are still strong soldiers; if it sits on the Greater White Tiger, they are surely valiant and fierce picked troops.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 367 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 367*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 福遇青龙，必出忠良之将。 | 福神遇到青龙，必定出忠良的将领； | If the fortune spirit meets the Azure Dragon, loyal and good commanders are sure to appear; |
| 子监白虎，当差猛勇之军。 | 子孙监临白虎，应当差遣勇猛的军队。 | if Output sits over the White Tiger, bold and fierce troops should be sent. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 368 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 368*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若子遇青龙，必赤胆忠心之捷将，又加禄马贵人，必智勇谋略之士耳。 | 子孙遇到青龙，必是赤胆忠心的胜将，再加禄、驿马、贵人，必是智勇谋略之士。 | If Output meets the Azure Dragon, he is a victorious general of true loyalty, and with Emolument, the Horse and the Noble added, he is a man of wit, courage and strategy. |
| 子为兵卒，若临小白虎，虽然衰弱亦是壮强之卒。 | 子孙是兵卒，如果临小白虎，虽然衰弱也是强壮的兵卒。 | Output is the soldiers, and if it sits on the Lesser White Tiger, though weak they are still strong soldiers. |
| 若临大白虎，必是骁勇猛烈之雄兵也。 | 如果临大白虎，必是骁勇猛烈的雄兵。 | If it sits on the Greater White Tiger, they are surely valiant and fierce picked troops. |

> **按**:「福」是福神（子孙）。小白虎、大白虎的起例见 EL.28。「禄马贵人」是禄神、驿马、贵人三个神煞。「捷将」是能打胜仗的将领。
>
> **Note:** 'Fu' is the fortune spirit (Output). The setting out of the Lesser and Greater White Tiger is in EL.28. 'Emolument, the Horse and the Noble' are three spirit-stars. 'Jiejiang' is a commander who wins battles.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.34、LY.CAREER.EL.36、LY.CAREER.EL.28

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.36 出师征伐：劫煞凶星怕临鬼动，亡神恶曜忌并官兴；官鬼为贼魁，休衰可战易剿、旺相强梁谋勇、发动不可抵挡，岂可再加凶煞（劫煞太煞亡神）；官鬼发动勾同诸煞克身世彼势猖獗我军大损

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:劫煞是凶星，怕临官鬼发动；亡神是恶曜，忌和官鬼同时兴起。官鬼是贼魁：休衰，就可以战、容易剿灭；旺相，必是强横而有谋勇的贼；如果发动，就无法抵挡，哪里还能再加凶煞一起行动呢？凶煞就是劫煞、太煞、亡神，起例已注在前面。如果官鬼发动、勾结各煞来克身爻、世爻，必定对方势头猖獗，我军大有损伤。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Robbery is an ill star, and fears sitting with a moving Pressure; the Lost Spirit is an evil luminary, and must not rise together with Pressure. Pressure is the bandit chief: if resting or weak, one can fight and it is easy to destroy; if thriving, it is surely a fierce, wise and brave bandit; if it moves it cannot be withstood, so how could ill spirits be added to move with it? The ill spirits are Robbery, the Great Baleful Spirit and the Lost Spirit, whose setting out is noted earlier. If Pressure moves and colludes with the baleful spirits to control the Body and Self, the enemy is surely rampant and my army suffers heavy losses.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 369 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 369*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 劫煞乃是凶星，怕临鬼动。 | 劫煞是凶星，怕临官鬼发动； | Robbery is an ill star, and fears sitting with a moving Pressure; |
| 亡神谓之恶曜，忌并官兴。 | 亡神是恶曜，忌和官鬼同时兴起。 | the Lost Spirit is an evil luminary, and must not rise together with Pressure. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 370 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 370*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼谓之贼魁，若休衰，可战易剿。 | 官鬼是贼魁：休衰，就可以战、容易剿灭。 | Pressure is the bandit chief: if resting or weak, one can fight and it is easy to destroy. |
| 若旺相，必是强梁谋勇之贼。 | 旺相，必是强横而有谋勇的贼。 | If thriving, it is surely a fierce, wise and brave bandit. |
| 若遇发动，则不可抵挡，岂可再加凶煞并行乎？ | 如果发动，就无法抵挡，哪里还能再加凶煞一起行动呢？ | If it moves it cannot be withstood, so how could ill spirits be added to move with it? |
| 凶煞者，即劫煞、太煞、亡神也。 | 凶煞就是劫煞、太煞、亡神。 | The ill spirits are Robbery, the Great Baleful Spirit and the Lost Spirit. |
| 列注在前。 | 起例已注在前面。 | Their setting out is noted earlier. |
| 若官鬼发动，勾同诸煞来克身世者，决主彼势猖獗，我军大有损伤。 | 如果官鬼发动、勾结各煞来克身爻、世爻，必定对方势头猖獗，我军大有损伤。 | If Pressure moves and colludes with the baleful spirits to control the Body and Self, the enemy is surely rampant and my army suffers heavy losses. |

> **按**:「太煞」即大煞（见 EL.16）；「勾同」是勾结。第三联「忌并官兴」的「官」指官鬼。与易隐征战占「鬼带亡神劫杀在内卦动防奸细」（YY.62）和彼己的「鬼暗动加大杀劫杀刑刃克世防刺客」（YY.56）取同一类断语。
>
> **Note:** 'Tai sha' is the great baleful spirit (see EL.16); 'gou tong' is colluding. The 'official' of 'must not rise together with the official' means Pressure. It takes the same kind of judgment as 'Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves in the inner trigram, guard against spies' (YY.62) and 'Pressure moves in secret with a great baleful spirit, Robbery, punishment and the blade and controls the Self, guard against assassins' (YY.56) of the Yi Yin war divination.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.35、LY.CAREER.EL.37、LY.CAREER.YY.56、LY.CAREER.YY.62、LY.CAREER.HJC.109

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.37 出师征伐：杀陷鬼摇虽凶不振，官空煞动纵乱勿忧；鬼煞并兴兵马云屯成大战，官凶俱绝干戈顿息庆清平；最忌鬼煞二神，煞空绝鬼动难成大害，官空绝煞兴无重祸，鬼煞俱动鬼随煞势煞趋鬼威大肆鏖战，克世冲世必被刑戮，官煞二神衰静或空绝方是弓藏胄解太平之世

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:煞陷落、官鬼摇动，虽然凶也振作不起来；官鬼落空、煞发动，纵然有乱也不必忧虑。官鬼和煞同时兴起，兵马云集，成为大战；官鬼和凶煞都绝，战事顿时止息，庆贺太平。征战的爻里，最忌官鬼和煞两个神：如果煞神空、绝，官鬼纵然发动，也难成大害；如果官鬼逢空、绝，各煞虽然兴旺，也没有重大的祸害；如果官鬼和煞都发动，官鬼就随着煞的势头，煞趋附官鬼的威势，云集聚拢、大肆苦战；或者克世、冲世的，必被刑杀，不轻；如果官鬼和煞两个神衰弱而又安静，或者空或者绝，才是弓箭收起、盔甲解下的太平之世。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the baleful spirit sinks and Pressure stirs, though ominous it cannot rally; if Pressure is Void and the baleful spirit moves, even if there is disorder there is no need to worry. If Pressure and the baleful spirit rise together, horses and men gather like clouds and it becomes a great battle; if Pressure and the ill spirits are both extinct, arms are suddenly stilled and one celebrates peace. In the lines of war, what is most feared is the two spirits Pressure and the baleful spirit: if the baleful spirit is Void or extinct, even though Pressure moves it can hardly do great harm; if Pressure meets Void or extinction, though the baleful spirits flourish there is no heavy disaster; if Pressure and the baleful spirit both move, Pressure follows the baleful spirit's momentum and the baleful spirit joins Pressure's might, they gather like clouds and fight a bitter battle on a large scale; if they control or clash with the Self, one is surely punished and slain, and not lightly; if both Pressure and the baleful spirit are weak and still, or Void or extinct, only then is it an age of peace with bows put away and helmets removed.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 371 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 371*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 杀陷鬼摇虽凶不振，官空煞动纵乱勿忧。 | 煞陷落、官鬼摇动，虽然凶也振作不起来；官鬼落空、煞发动，纵然有乱也不必忧虑。 | If the baleful spirit sinks and Pressure stirs, though ominous it cannot rally; if Pressure is Void and the baleful spirit moves, even if there is disorder there is no need to worry. |
| 鬼煞并兴，兵马云屯成大战。 | 官鬼和煞同时兴起，兵马云集，成为大战； | If Pressure and the baleful spirit rise together, horses and men gather like clouds and it becomes a great battle; |
| 官凶俱绝，干戈顿息庆清平。 | 官鬼和凶煞都绝，战事顿时止息，庆贺太平。 | if Pressure and the ill spirits are both extinct, arms are suddenly stilled and one celebrates peace. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 372 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 372*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 征战爻中，最忌鬼煞二神，若煞神空绝，官鬼纵然发动，难成大害。 | 征战的爻里，最忌官鬼和煞两个神：如果煞神空、绝，官鬼纵然发动，也难成大害。 | In the lines of war, what is most feared is the two spirits Pressure and the baleful spirit: if the baleful spirit is Void or extinct, even though Pressure moves it can hardly do great harm. |
| 若官逢空绝，各煞虽是兴隆亦无重祸。 | 如果官鬼逢空、绝，各煞虽然兴旺，也没有重大的祸害。 | If Pressure meets Void or extinction, though the baleful spirits flourish there is no heavy disaster. |
| 若鬼煞俱动，则鬼随煞势煞趋鬼威，云屯曦聚大肆鏖战。 | 如果官鬼和煞都发动，官鬼就随着煞的势头，煞趋附官鬼的威势，云集聚拢，大肆苦战。 | If Pressure and the baleful spirit both move, Pressure follows the baleful spirit's momentum and the baleful spirit joins Pressure's might, they gather like clouds and fight a bitter battle on a large scale. |
| 或克世冲世者，必被刑戮不轻。 | 或者克世、冲世的，必被刑杀，不轻。 | If they control or clash with the Self, one is surely punished and slain, and not lightly. |
| 若得官煞二神衰而又静、或空或绝者，方是弓藏胃解太乎这世矣！ | 如果官鬼和煞两个神衰弱而又安静，或者空或者绝，才是弓箭收起、盔甲解下的太平之世！ | If both Pressure and the baleful spirit are weak and still, or Void or extinct, only then is it an age of peace with bows put away and helmets removed! |

> **按**:「曦聚」疑是「蚁聚」（像蚂蚁一样聚拢），「胃解」疑是「胄解」（盔甲解下），「太乎这世」疑是「太平之世」，译文按这样的读法译；原文几处笔误，大意清楚。这一联与易隐征战占「鬼衰静敌胆怯、鬼旺动敌势盛」「鬼空者敌必灭」（YY.62）一类。
>
> **Note:** 'Xi ju' is presumably 'yi ju', swarming like ants, 'wei jie' is presumably 'zhou jie', helmets removed, and 'tai hu zhe shi' is presumably 'taiping zhi shi', an age of peace, and the translation reads them so; the text has several slips of the pen but its general sense is clear. This couplet is of the kind of 'Pressure weak and still, the enemy is timid; Pressure thriving and moving, the enemy's force is strong' and 'Pressure Void, the enemy is destroyed' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.62).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.36、LY.CAREER.EL.38、LY.CAREER.YY.62、LY.CAREER.HJC.107

## LY.CAREER.EL.38 出师征伐：鬼煞克身吾兵难敌，交重伐应彼寇易诛；鬼煞二神来克世吾兵大伤，鬼煞静衰克旺世旺不受克无大害，鬼煞旺相克衰世旺能克衰吾兵大被伐戮，鬼煞旺动克应彼已受制易剿伐

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼和煞克身，我军难以抵敌；重叠发动去伐应爻，对方的贼寇容易诛灭。鬼是官鬼，煞是大煞、劫煞之类。官鬼和煞两个神如果来克世爻，我军大受损伤；如果官鬼和煞安静而又衰弱，来克旺相的世爻，是旺相不受克，料想没有大害；官鬼和煞旺相而克衰弱的世爻，这是旺相能克衰弱，我军大被讨伐杀戮；如果官鬼和煞旺相发动克应爻，对方已经受制，容易剿伐。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Pressure and the baleful spirit control the Body, my army can hardly stand against them; if they move in double to attack the Other, the enemy bandits are easily destroyed. 'Gui' is Pressure, and 'sha' is the great baleful spirit, Robbery and the like. If the two spirits Pressure and the baleful spirit come to control the Self, my army is badly injured; if Pressure and the baleful spirit are still and weak and come to control a thriving Self, a thriving line is not controlled and no great harm is expected; if Pressure and the baleful spirit are thriving and control a weak Self, this is the thriving controlling the weak, and my army is greatly attacked and slaughtered; if Pressure and the baleful spirit are thriving and moving and control the Other, the enemy is already restrained and easily destroyed.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 373 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 373*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼煞克身吾兵难敌， | 鬼煞克身，我军难以抵敌； | If Pressure and the baleful spirit control the Body, my army can hardly stand against them; |
| 交重伐应彼寇易诛。 | 重叠发动去伐应爻，对方的贼寇容易诛灭。 | if they move in double to attack the Other, the enemy bandits are easily destroyed. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 374 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 374*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼者，官鬼也。 | 鬼是官鬼。 | 'Gui' is Pressure. |
| 煞者、大煞、劫煞之类也。 | 煞是大煞、劫煞之类。 | 'Sha' is the great baleful spirit, Robbery and the like. |
| 鬼煞二神若来克世，吾兵大伤。 | 官鬼和煞两个神如果来克世爻，我军大受损伤。 | If the two spirits Pressure and the baleful spirit come to control the Self, my army is badly injured. |
| 若鬼煞静而又衰来克旺世者，是旺不受克，料无大害。 | 如果官鬼和煞安静而又衰弱，来克旺相的世爻，是旺相不受克，料想没有大害。 | If Pressure and the baleful spirit are still and weak and come to control a thriving Self, a thriving line is not controlled and no great harm is expected. |
| 鬼煞旺相而克衰世者，此为旺能克衰，吾兵大被伐戮。 | 官鬼和煞旺相而克衰弱的世爻，这是旺相能克衰弱，我军大被讨伐杀戮。 | If Pressure and the baleful spirit are thriving and control a weak Self, this is the thriving controlling the weak, and my army is greatly attacked and slaughtered. |
| 若鬼煞旺动克应者，彼已受制易以剿伐。 | 如果官鬼和煞旺相发动克应爻，对方已经受制，容易剿伐。 | If Pressure and the baleful spirit are thriving and moving and control the Other, the enemy is already restrained and easily destroyed. |

> **按**:「旺不受克」是「旺相的爻不受衰弱的爻所克」，这是五行生克里的通例。这一条与 EL.36、EL.37 连着读：官鬼与煞合起来看克世还是克应。
>
> **Note:** 'The thriving is not controlled' is the general rule in the generation and control of the elements that a thriving line is not controlled by a weak one. This entry reads on from EL.36 and EL.37: Pressure and the baleful spirit are looked at together for whether they control the Self or the Other.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.37、LY.CAREER.EL.39、LY.CAREER.YY.56

## LY.CAREER.EL.39 出师征伐：细究何爻克应、再查那象伤官，用此奇方妙计自然剿恶锄强；鬼空惟取应、应陷可从官；行兵之法须求克应伤官，应受伤彼遭屠戮，鬼披克贼必生擒；火爻临应或鬼须求水制火用黑旗为号并临水战；鬼空亡评应象，应落陷看官爻

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:细究是哪一爻克应爻，再查哪一个象伤官鬼；用这种奇方妙计，自然剿灭凶恶、锄除强梁。官鬼落空，只取应爻；应爻陷落，可以从官鬼（入手）。要懂得行兵的办法，必须找出克应爻、伤官鬼的爻：应爻如果受伤，对方就遭屠戮；官鬼如果被克，贼必定生擒。比如火爻临在应爻或官鬼上，须找水来制火，用黑旗为号，并且临水作战，自然得胜，其余的照这样推。官鬼如果空亡，就评断应象；应爻如果落陷，必须看官爻。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Examine closely which line controls the Other, and then see which image injures Pressure; with this marvellous method and clever plan, one naturally destroys the evil and weeds out the strong. If Pressure is Void, take only the Other; if the Other is sunk, one can start from Pressure. To know the method of moving troops, one must find the line that controls the Other and injures Pressure: if the Other is injured, the enemy is slaughtered; if Pressure is controlled, the bandits are surely taken alive. For example, if a Fire line sits on the Other or on Pressure, one must seek Water to control the Fire, use black flags as the signal and fight beside water, and one naturally wins; the rest follow this. If Pressure is Void, judge the Other image; if the Other is sunk, one must look at the Pressure line.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 375 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 375*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 细究何爻克应，再查那象伤官。 | 细究是哪一爻克应爻，再查哪一个象伤官鬼； | Examine closely which line controls the Other, and then see which image injures Pressure; |
| 用此奇方妙计，自然剿恶锄强。 | 用这种奇方妙计，自然剿灭凶恶、锄除强梁。 | with this marvellous method and clever plan, one naturally destroys the evil and weeds out the strong. |
| 鬼空惟取应，应陷可从官。 | 官鬼落空只取应爻，应爻陷落可以从官鬼（入手）。 | If Pressure is Void, take only the Other; if the Other is sunk, one can start from Pressure. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 376 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 376*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 要识行兵之法，须求克应伤官，应若受伤，彼遭屠戮。 | 要懂得行兵的办法，必须找出克应爻、伤官鬼的爻：应爻如果受伤，对方就遭屠戮。 | To know the method of moving troops, one must find the line that controls the Other and injures Pressure: if the Other is injured, the enemy is slaughtered. |
| 鬼如披克，贼必生擒。 | 官鬼如果被克，贼必定生擒。 | If Pressure is controlled, the bandits are surely taken alive. |
| 且如火爻临应或临鬼，须求水来制火，用黑旗为号，并临水战，自然得胜也，余皆仿此。 | 比如火爻临在应爻或官鬼上，须找水来制火，用黑旗为号，并且临水作战，自然得胜，其余的照这样推。 | For example, if a Fire line sits on the Other or on Pressure, one must seek Water to control the Fire, use black flags as the signal and fight beside water, and one naturally wins; the rest follow this. |
| 鬼若空亡，却评应象。 | 官鬼如果空亡，就评断应象。 | If Pressure is Void, judge the Other image. |
| 应如落陷，必看官爻。 | 应爻如果落陷，必须看官爻。 | If the Other is sunk, one must look at the Pressure line. |

> **按**:「披克」是被克；「奇方」是出奇的办法。「用黑旗为号」是水属黑色的取象。这一条与易隐征战占里「应空取鬼为用」（YY.54）一类：对方的将（应）和兵（官鬼）互为补位。
>
> **Note:** 'Pi ke' is being controlled; 'qi fang' is an unusual method. 'Using black flags as the signal' takes the image that Water is black. This entry is of the kind of 'if the Other is Void take Pressure as the Subject' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.54): the enemy's general (the Other) and troops (Pressure) stand in for each other.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.38、LY.CAREER.EL.40、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.HJC.86

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.40 出师征伐：子爻旺鬼爻休鬼虽克我我无忧，应爻强世爻弱世纵伤他他难伐，世遇生扶子象旺则将勇兵骁，应遭克破鬼爻空则贼衰寇退；子能制鬼，子旺官休则彼先已被制岂能克世；应旺世衰虽克应彼猖獗难取胜（旺不受克）；日辰月将生世合世旺子扶助则我兵精粮足决胜千里；应衰受克受冲官鬼空亡彼必丧胆倒戈拔寨而逃

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙爻旺相、官鬼爻休囚，官鬼虽然克我，我也无忧；应爻强、世爻弱，世爻纵然伤他，他也难被讨伐；世爻遇到生扶、子孙象旺相，就是将领勇敢、兵卒骁勇；应爻遭到克破、官鬼爻落空，就是贼衰寇退。子孙能制官鬼：如果子孙旺相、官鬼休囚，对方先已被制住，哪里能克世爻呢？就像大盗正想打劫别人，已经被官府搜捕擒获，哪有工夫行劫，我还忧虑什么呢？如果应爻旺相、世爻衰弱，虽然克应爻，无奈对方猖獗，难以取胜，「旺不受克」说的正是这个。如果日辰、月将生世、合世，旺相的子孙又来扶助，就是我兵精良、粮草充足、机谋深远、声势浩大，决胜千里；如果应衰弱受克受冲，官鬼又落空亡，对方必定丧胆、倒戈、拔寨逃走。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Output line is thriving and the Pressure line resting, though Pressure controls me I have nothing to fear; if the Other is strong and the Self weak, though the Self injures them, they are hard to attack; if the Self meets generation and support and the Output image is thriving, the commander is brave and the troops valiant; if the Other is controlled and broken and the Pressure line Void, the bandits decline and withdraw. Output can control Pressure: if Output is thriving and Pressure resting, the enemy has first been restrained, so how can it control the Self? It is like a great robber who is about to rob someone and has already been seized by the officers, so how has he time to rob, and what have I to fear? If the Other is thriving and the Self weak, though the Self controls the Other, the enemy is rampant and hard to beat, and 'the thriving is not controlled' says exactly this. If the Day and the Month General generate or combine with the Self, and a thriving Output also comes to support, my troops are well trained, grain is plentiful, plans are deep and strength great, and victory is decided a thousand miles away; if the Other is weak and controlled and clashed, and Pressure also falls into Void, the enemy will surely lose heart, turn their spears, pull up their stockades and flee.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 377 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 377*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子爻旺鬼爻休，鬼虽克我我无忧。 | 子孙爻旺相、官鬼爻休囚，官鬼虽然克我，我也无忧； | If the Output line is thriving and the Pressure line resting, though Pressure controls me I have nothing to fear; |
| 应爻强世爻弱，世纵伤他他难伐。 | 应爻强、世爻弱，世爻纵然伤他，他也难被讨伐； | if the Other is strong and the Self weak, though the Self injures them, they are hard to attack; |
| 世遇生扶子象旺，则将勇兵骁。 | 世爻遇到生扶、子孙象旺相，就是将领勇敢、兵卒骁勇； | if the Self meets generation and support and the Output image is thriving, the commander is brave and the troops valiant; |
| 应遭克破鬼爻空，则贼衰寇退。 | 应爻遭到克破、官鬼爻落空，就是贼衰寇退。 | if the Other is controlled and broken and the Pressure line Void, the bandits decline and withdraw. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 378–380 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 378–380*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子能制鬼，若子孙旺官鬼休，则彼先已被制，岂能克世乎？ | 子孙能制官鬼：如果子孙旺相、官鬼休囚，对方先已被制住，哪里能克世爻呢？ | Output can control Pressure: if Output is thriving and Pressure resting, the enemy has first been restrained, so how can it control the Self? |
| 譬诸剧盗正欲劫人已被官捕搜擒，何暇行劫，我何忧哉？ | 就像大盗正想打劫别人，已经被官府搜捕擒获，哪有工夫行劫，我还忧虑什么呢？ | It is like a great robber who is about to rob someone and has already been seized by the officers, so how has he time to rob, and what have I to fear? |
| 如应爻旺世爻衰然虽克应，奈彼猖獗难以取胜，旺不受克，正此之谓也。 | 如果应爻旺相、世爻衰弱，虽然克应爻，无奈对方猖獗，难以取胜，「旺不受克」说的正是这个。 | If the Other is thriving and the Self weak, though the Self controls the Other, the enemy is rampant and hard to beat, and 'the thriving is not controlled' says exactly this. |
| 若日辰月将生世合世，旺子又来扶助，则我兵精粮足机深势大决胜千里。 | 如果日辰、月将生世、合世，旺相的子孙又来扶助，就是我兵精良、粮草充足、机谋深远、声势浩大，决胜千里。 | If the Day and the Month General generate or combine with the Self, and a thriving Output also comes to support, my troops are well trained, grain is plentiful, plans are deep and strength great, and victory is decided a thousand miles away. |
| 若应衰受克受冲官鬼又落空亡，彼必丧胆倒戈拔寨而逃遁矣！ | 如果应衰弱受克受冲，官鬼又落空亡，对方必定丧胆、倒戈、拔寨逃走！ | If the Other is weak and controlled and clashed, and Pressure also falls into Void, the enemy will surely lose heart, turn their spears, pull up their stockades and flee! |

> **按**:「月将」是太阳所在的月份地支（神煞，不是月建）；「倒戈」是掉转兵器、反过来攻自己人。第一首四联的头两联是「子旺鬼休，鬼克我也无忧」和「应强世弱，世伤他也难伐」，解里正好对应到「子能制鬼」和「旺不受克」两个道理。第二个比喻在书里被断行隔开（「被官捕搜擒」在上一行末，「何暇行劫」在下一行首），这里连起来读。
>
> **Note:** The 'Month General' is the branch of the month in which the sun stands (a spirit-star, not the Month branch); 'dao ge' is turning one's weapons round and attacking one's own side. The first two couplets of the four are 'Output thriving, Pressure resting, though Pressure controls me I have no worry' and 'the Other strong, the Self weak, though the Self injures them they are hard to attack', and the explanation answers them with the two principles 'Output can control Pressure' and 'the thriving is not controlled'. The second simile is split by a line break in the book ('seized by the officers' at the end of one line and 'how has he time to rob' at the start of the next) and is read here joined up.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.39、LY.CAREER.EL.41、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.YY.55、LY.CAREER.HJC.95、LY.CAREER.HJC.96、LY.CAREER.HJC.107、LY.CAREER.HJC.108

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.41 出师征伐：金为兵甲值官兴旺防战斗，土作城池临鬼发动干戈；兵戈铠甲皆用金，临官鬼或旺或动彼必坚甲利刃谋奇策出其不意；土旺于四季分四隅又作城垣台堡，鬼带土发则干戈扰扰弥山漫野

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:金是兵甲，金值官鬼兴旺，要防战斗；土当城池，土临官鬼发动，就动起干戈。兵器铠甲都用金来造，如果金临官鬼，或者旺相、或者发动，对方必定甲坚刃利（与众不同），谋划奇策、出其不意地猛然冲突，不要小看；土旺在四季，分布四个角落，又作城墙、台堡，如果官鬼带土发动，就干戈扰攘，漫山遍野，无地不有。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Metal is arms and armour, and if Metal meets a flourishing Pressure, guard against fighting; Earth is the city walls, and if Earth sits on Pressure and moves, spears and shields are raised. Weapons and armour are all made of Metal, and if Metal sits on Pressure and is thriving or moving, the enemy surely has strong armour and sharp blades, unlike the common, and plans unusual strategies to charge suddenly and unexpectedly, which must not be made light of; Earth is thriving in the four seasons and spread over the four corners, and also serves as city walls, terraces and forts, and if Pressure carries Earth and moves, spears and shields are in turmoil, covering the hills and spreading over the fields, with no place free of them.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 381 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 381*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金为兵甲，值官兴旺防战斗。 | 金是兵甲，金值官鬼兴旺，要防战斗； | Metal is arms and armour, and if Metal meets a flourishing Pressure, guard against fighting; |
| 土作城池，临鬼发遁动干戈。 | 土当城池，土临官鬼发动，就动起干戈。 | Earth is the city walls, and if Earth sits on Pressure and moves, spears and shields are raised. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 382 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 382*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫兵戈铠甲，皆用金以造，若临官鬼、或旺、或动，彼必坚甲利刃异。 | 兵器铠甲都用金来造，如果金临官鬼，或者旺相、或者发动，对方必定甲坚刃利（与众不同）。 | Weapons and armour are all made of Metal, and if Metal sits on Pressure and is thriving or moving, the enemy surely has strong armour and sharp blades, unlike the common. |
| 谋奇策出其不意墟然冲突，勿为小观。 | （对方）谋划奇策、出其不意地猛然冲突，不要小看。 | They plan unusual strategies to charge suddenly and unexpectedly, which must not be made light of. |
| 夫土者，旺于四季，列分四隅，又作城垣台堡，若鬼带土发则干戈扰扰，弥山漫野无地无之。 | 土旺在四季，分布四个角落，又作城墙、台堡，如果官鬼带土发动，就干戈扰攘，漫山遍野，无地不有。 | Earth is thriving in the four seasons and spread over the four corners, and also serves as city walls, terraces and forts, and if Pressure carries Earth and moves, spears and shields are in turmoil, covering the hills and spreading over the fields, with no place free of them. |

> **按**:第二联「临鬼发遁动干戈」的「遁」是衍字或误字，译文按「临官鬼发动、动起干戈」译。「墟然」疑是「忽然」（或「虚然」），译作「猛然」。「干戈」是兵器，代指战争。这一条与易隐征战占「五行」里的金（兵甲）、土（炮石城池）（YY.66、YY.65）取象相同。
>
> **Note:** In the second couplet 'lin gui fa dun dong ganqe' the 'dun' is a superfluous or miswritten character, and the translation reads 'sits on Pressure and moves, spears and shields are raised'. 'Xu ran' is presumably 'hu ran', suddenly (or 'xu ran'), and is translated 'suddenly'. 'Ganqe' are weapons, standing for war. This entry takes the same images as Metal (arms and armour) and Earth (catapult stones and city walls) in the 'five elements' section of the Yi Yin war divination (YY.66, YY.65).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.40、LY.CAREER.EL.42、LY.CAREER.YY.65、LY.CAREER.YY.66、LY.CAREER.HJC.111

## LY.CAREER.EL.42 出师征伐：密密刀枪为金宫之空动，飘飘旗帜因火父之旺冲；刀剑属金又属官鬼，值空亡又逢发动必振扬，金空则鸣刀枪光耀日金鼓振轰雷营寨重重提防有备；旌旗以父母为用爻又属离火，旺相更遇日冲火发则焰，黄幡豹尾彩帜麒麟飘摇号令严明军机整肃

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:密密的刀枪，是金宫空而发动；飘飘的旗帜，是因为火的父爻旺相逢冲。刀剑本属金，又属官鬼，如果值空亡又逢发动，必定振扬，所以经书说「金空则鸣」，必是刀枪光芒耀日、金鼓声震如雷，营寨密密重重是提防有备的缘故。旌旗以父母爻为用爻，又属离火，如果旺相，更遇日辰冲，所以日冲火发就有焰，必是黄幡悬着豹尾、彩旗上写着麒麟，飘飘摇摇，引导军队、行阵，号令严明，军机整肃。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Dense blades and spears are Metal palace Void and moving; fluttering banners are the Fire Resource line thriving and clashed. Swords and blades belong to Metal, and also to Pressure, and if they meet Void and also move they surely rise up, so the classic says 'Metal Void rings', and surely the blades and spears flash against the sun, the gongs and drums shake like thunder, and the camps are dense and layered out of watchfulness and readiness. The banners take the Resource line as the Subject line and also belong to Li Fire, and if it is thriving and also meets the Day's clash, then when the Day clashes Fire moves there is flame, and surely yellow pennants hang with leopard tails and bright flags are written with the qilin, fluttering and waving, leading the army and the lines, orders strict and clear and military secrets well guarded.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 383 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 383*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 密密刀枪为金宫之空动， | 密密的刀枪，是金宫空而发动； | Dense blades and spears are Metal palace Void and moving; |
| 飘飘旗帜，因火父之旺冲。 | 飘飘的旗帜，是因为火父爻旺相逢冲。 | fluttering banners are the Fire Resource line thriving and clashed. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 384 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 384*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 刀剑本属金，又属官鬼，若值空亡又逢发动，是必振扬也，故经云金。 | 刀剑本属金，又属官鬼，如果值空亡又逢发动，必定振扬，所以经书说「金」。 | Swords and blades belong to Metal, and also to Pressure, and if they meet Void and also move they surely rise up, so the classic says 'Metal'. |
| 空则鸣，必是刀枪光耀日，金鼓振轰雷，密密重重营衡寨乃提防有备耳。 | （金）空则鸣，必是刀枪光芒耀日、金鼓声震如雷，营寨密密重重是提防有备的缘故。 | Void it rings: surely the blades and spears flash against the sun, the gongs and drums shake like thunder, and the camps are dense and layered out of watchfulness and readiness. |
| 旌旗，父母为之用爻，又属离火，若值旺相更遇日冲，故日火发则焰，乃是黄幡悬豹尾彩帜书麒麟飘飘摇摇道军引陈号令严明，军机整肃耳。 | 旌旗以父母爻为用爻，又属离火，如果旺相，更遇日辰冲，所以日冲火发就有焰，必是黄幡悬着豹尾、彩旗上写着麒麟，飘飘摇摇，引导军队、行阵，号令严明，军机整肃。 | The banners take the Resource line as the Subject line and also belong to Li Fire, and if it is thriving and also meets the Day's clash, then when the Day clashes Fire moves there is flame, and surely yellow pennants hang with leopard tails and bright flags are written with the qilin, fluttering and waving, leading the army and the lines, orders strict and clear and military secrets well guarded. |

> **按**:「金空则鸣」是「金空则鸣，金实则不鸣」的老话：金爻落空，就像空的钟一敲就响。「豹尾」「麒麟」是旌旗上的图案，「黄幡豹尾」是古代军中的旗饰。「营衡寨」疑是「营栅寨」，不影响大意。这一条与 EL.30 的旌旗合着读，与易隐征战占「父旺外动彼兴兵」等（YY.57）不同的是这里只说金、火两爻的外在景象。
>
> **Note:** 'Metal Void rings' is from the old saying 'Metal Void rings, Metal full does not ring': a Metal line that is Void is like an empty bell, which rings when struck. The 'leopard tail' and the 'qilin' are designs on the banners, and 'yellow pennant and leopard tail' is an old flag decoration of armies. 'Ying heng zhai' is presumably 'ying zha zhai', camps and stockades, which does not affect the general sense. This entry is read together with the banners of EL.30, and differs from 'Resource thriving and moving outside, the enemy raises troops' and the like in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.57) in speaking only of the outward scenes of the Metal and Fire lines.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.41、LY.CAREER.EL.43、LY.CAREER.EL.30、LY.CAREER.YY.58

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.43 出师征伐：水乃江河木为舟楫，二爻皆旺水战偏宜，两象并伤乘航欠利；水旺则盈绝则竭，木隆则船大衰则舟楫绝则无舟；二爻旺而又发万事齐备利用操舟，衰而又绝难以觅渡

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:水是江河，木是舟楫；两爻都旺相，水战特别适宜；两象都受伤，乘船渡水不利。水是江海的源流，木是舟船的根本，水战各有各的用处：水旺则满，水绝则竭；木旺则船大，木衰则只有小舟，木绝则没有船；两爻旺相又发动，万事齐备，利于操舟；如果两爻衰弱又绝，一切都不成，难以找到渡口了！

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Water is rivers and Wood is boats and oars; if both lines are thriving, a naval battle is especially fit; if both images are injured, going by boat is unfavourable. Water is the source and flow of rivers and seas, and Wood is the root of boats, each having its use in a naval battle: thriving Water is full, extinct Water dries up; thriving Wood means big boats, weak Wood only small boats, extinct Wood no boat; if both lines are thriving and also move, everything is ready and it is favourable to handle boats; if both lines are weak and also extinct, nothing succeeds and it is hard to find a crossing!

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 385 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 385*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水乃江河，木为舟楫， | 水是江河，木是舟楫； | Water is rivers and Wood is boats and oars; |
| 二爻皆旺，水战偏宜， | 两爻都旺相，水战特别适宜； | if both lines are thriving, a naval battle is especially fit; |
| 雨像并伤，乘航欠利。 | 两象都受伤，乘船渡水不利。 | if both images are injured, going by boat is unfavourable. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 386 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 386*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水为江海源流，木作舟船之本，水战各有所用，若水旺则盈，水绝则竭。 | 水是江海的源流，木是舟船的根本，水战各有各的用处：水旺则满，水绝则竭。 | Water is the source and flow of rivers and seas, and Wood is the root of boats, each having its use in a naval battle: thriving Water is full, extinct Water dries up. |
| 木隆则船大，木衰则舟楫，木绝则无舟。 | 木旺则船大，木衰则只有小舟，木绝则没有船。 | Thriving Wood means big boats, weak Wood only small boats, extinct Wood no boat. |
| 二爻旺而又发，万事齐备利用操舟。 | 两爻旺相又发动，万事齐备，利于操舟。 | If both lines are thriving and also move, everything is ready and it is favourable to handle boats. |
| 若二爻衰而又绝，凡物无成难以觅渡矣！ | 如果两爻衰弱又绝，一切都不成，难以找到渡口了！ | If both lines are weak and also extinct, nothing succeeds and it is hard to find a crossing! |

> **按**:「雨像并伤」的「雨像」是「二象」（水、木两象）之误。这一条与易隐征战占「五行」里的木（舟）、水（泉）（YY.63、YY.67）取象相同，但这里只说水木旺衰与水战的关系，没有船的各部分。
>
> **Note:** 'Yu xiang bing shang' has 'yu xiang' for 'er xiang', the two images (Water and Wood). This entry takes the same images as Wood (boats) and Water (springs) in the 'five elements' section of the Yi Yin war divination (YY.63, YY.67), but speaks only of the strength of Water and Wood in relation to naval battle, without the parts of the boat.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.42、LY.CAREER.EL.44、LY.CAREER.YY.63、LY.CAREER.YY.67、LY.CAREER.HJC.89

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.44 出师征伐：火多营寨土是城垣，此象兴隆最宜陆战，其爻空绝不利出征；营垒以火为用城台赖土生成，火空营险土空城陷，二爻旺相动冲可使西域沙漠之胜，衰休空绝必有华容小道之危

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:火多是营寨，土是城墙，这两个象兴旺，最宜陆战；这两爻空、绝，不利出征。营垒以火为用，城台靠土生成：火空则营寨危险，土空则城池陷落；两爻如果值旺相、动、冲，可以取得西域沙漠（那样的）胜利；（两象）如果遇到衰、休、空、绝，必有华容小道（曹操败走）那样的危险。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Fire is mostly the camp and Earth is the city wall; if these two images flourish, they are most fit for a land battle; if these two lines are Void or extinct, it is not favourable to set out. Camps and ramparts take Fire as the Subject, and city terraces rely on Earth for their making: if Fire is Void the camp is in danger, if Earth is Void the city falls; if the two lines meet thriving, moving and clashing, one can win a victory such as that in the western deserts; if they meet weak, resting, Void or extinct, there will surely be danger like that of the small road at Huarong.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 387 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 387*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火多营寨，土是城垣， | 火多是营寨，土是城墙， | Fire is mostly the camp and Earth is the city wall; |
| 此象兴隆，最宜陆战。 | 这两个象兴旺，最宜陆战； | if these two images flourish, they are most fit for a land battle; |
| 其爻空绝，不利出征。 | 这两爻空、绝，不利出征。 | if these two lines are Void or extinct, it is not favourable to set out. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 388 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 388*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 营垒以火为用，城台赖土生成，火空营险，土空成陷，二爻若值旺相动冲，可使西域沙漠之胜、雨象若遇衰休空绝、必有华容小道之危。 | 营垒以火为用，城台靠土生成：火空则营寨危险，土空则城池陷落；两爻如果值旺相、动、冲，可以取得西域沙漠（那样的）胜利；（两象）如果遇到衰、休、空、绝，必有华容小道（曹操败走）那样的危险。 | Camps and ramparts take Fire as the Subject, and city terraces rely on Earth for their making: if Fire is Void the camp is in danger, if Earth is Void the city falls; if the two lines meet thriving, moving and clashing, one can win a victory such as that in the western deserts; if they meet weak, resting, Void or extinct, there will surely be danger like that of the small road at Huarong. |

> **按**:「华容小道」是《三国演义》里曹操赤壁战败后败走华容道的故事；「西域沙漠之胜」书里没有说是哪一场战役，译文照字面。「雨象」又是「二象」之误（火、土两象）。这一条与易隐征战占「五行」里的火（营寨）、土（城池）（YY.64、YY.65）取象相同。
>
> **Note:** 'The small road at Huarong' is the story, in the Romance of the Three Kingdoms, of Cao Cao fleeing by the Huarong road after his defeat at Red Cliff; the book does not say which campaign 'the victory in the western deserts' is, and the translation follows the letter. 'Yu xiang' is again a slip for 'er xiang', the two images (Fire and Earth). This entry takes the same images as Fire (camps) and Earth (city walls) in the 'five elements' section of the Yi Yin war divination (YY.64, YY.65).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.43、LY.CAREER.EL.45、LY.CAREER.YY.64、LY.CAREER.YY.65、LY.CAREER.HJC.89

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.45 出师征伐：火鬼伤身须防劫寨，土官克世恐堕陷坑；官鬼带火克世须慎火攻而劫寨，鬼杀以土伤世恐防堕堑之危

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:火爻官鬼伤身，须防劫寨；土爻官鬼克世，恐怕掉进陷坑。官鬼带火克世的，须慎防火攻、劫寨；官鬼和煞以土伤世的，恐怕有掉进壕沟的危险。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a Fire Pressure line injures the Body, guard against a raid on the stockade; if an Earth Pressure line controls the Self, one may fall into a pit. If Pressure carrying Fire controls the Self, one must take care against attack by fire and a raid on the stockade; if Pressure and a baleful spirit injure the Self by Earth, there may be the danger of falling into a trench.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 389 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 389*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火鬼伤身，须防劫寨。 | 火爻官鬼伤身，须防劫寨； | If a Fire Pressure line injures the Body, guard against a raid on the stockade; |
| 土官克世，恐堕陷坑。 | 土爻官鬼克世，恐怕掉进陷坑。 | if an Earth Pressure line controls the Self, one may fall into a pit. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 390 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 390*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼带火克世者，须慎火攻而劫寨。 | 官鬼带火克世的，须慎防火攻、劫寨。 | If Pressure carrying Fire controls the Self, one must take care against attack by fire and a raid on the stockade. |
| 鬼杀以土伤世者，恐防堕堑之危矶。 | 官鬼和煞以土伤世的，恐怕有掉进壕沟的危险。 | If Pressure and a baleful spirit injure the Self by Earth, there may be the danger of falling into a trench. |

> **按**:「危矶」疑是「危机」，译作「危险」。这一条与易隐征战占「火鬼伤世防劫营」「阴土鬼…防陷阱」（YY.64、YY.65）取同一件事。
>
> **Note:** 'Wei ji' is presumably 'weiji', danger. This entry takes up the same matter as 'a Fire Pressure line injures the Self, guard against a raid on the camp' and 'a yin Earth Pressure line ... guard against pits' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.64, YY.65).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.44、LY.CAREER.EL.46、LY.CAREER.YY.64、LY.CAREER.YY.65

## LY.CAREER.EL.46 出师征伐：火动则利迁营寨，父空则宜改旌旗；火发动则变化故营寨利迁移，被刑冲或加玄武兄弟必遭侵劫急宜迁改；旌旗以父为用，值旬空月破必然损伤急须改换

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:火发动，利于迁移营寨；父爻落空，宜改换旌旗。军营以火为用，火如果发动，动就是变化，所以营寨利于迁移才吉；如果被刑、冲，或者加玄武、加兄弟，必遭侵劫，急宜迁改。旌旗以父爻为用，如果值旬空、月破，必然损伤，急须改换。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Fire moves, it favours moving the camp; if the Resource line is Void, it is fit to change the banners. The army camp takes Fire as the Subject, and if Fire moves, moving means change, so it is auspicious to move the camp; if it is punished or clashed, or the Dark Tortoise or Peer is added, the camp will surely be raided, and it should be moved and changed urgently. The banners take the Resource line as the Subject, and if it meets decade Void or Month-break they are surely damaged and must be changed urgently.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 391 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 391*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火动则利迁营寨， | 火发动，利于迁移营寨； | If Fire moves, it favours moving the camp; |
| 父空则宜改旌旗。 | 父爻落空，宜改换旌旗。 | if the Resource line is Void, it is fit to change the banners. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 392 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 392*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 军营以火为用，火若发动，动则变化，故营寨必利迁移为吉，若被刑冲、或加玄武、或加兄弟，必遭侵劫，急宜迁改。 | 军营以火为用，火如果发动，动就是变化，所以营寨利于迁移才吉；如果被刑、冲，或者加玄武、加兄弟，必遭侵劫，急宜迁改。 | The army camp takes Fire as the Subject, and if Fire moves, moving means change, so it is auspicious to move the camp; if it is punished or clashed, or the Dark Tortoise or Peer is added, the camp will surely be raided, and it should be moved and changed urgently. |
| 旌旗以父为用。 | 旌旗以父爻为用。 | The banners take the Resource line as the Subject. |
| 若值旬空月破必然损伤，急须改换。 | 如果值旬空、月破，必然损伤，急须改换。 | If it meets decade Void or Month-break, they are surely damaged and must be changed urgently. |

> **按**:这一条与易隐征战占「火动利迁营」「父被刑害克破宜改旗帜」（YY.64、YY.58）取同一件事。
>
> **Note:** This entry takes up the same matter as 'if Fire moves, it favours moving camp' and 'if Resource is punished, harmed, controlled or broken, it is fit to change the banners' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.64, YY.58).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.45、LY.CAREER.EL.47、LY.CAREER.YY.58、LY.CAREER.YY.64

## LY.CAREER.EL.47 出师征伐：土官动克世爻他兴炮石，火鬼发伤身象彼用火攻；官鬼兴隆带土克世须防擂木炮石来攻，鬼动加火同克世必举火烧屯旺则太甚衰则稍可

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:土爻官鬼发动克世爻，对方兴用炮石；火爻官鬼发动伤身象，对方用火攻。官鬼兴旺带土来克世的，须防对方制备擂木、炮石来攻；如果官鬼发动、加火一起克世，他必定举火烧营，旺相则太厉害，衰弱则稍微好一些。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If an Earth Pressure line moves and controls the Self, the enemy uses catapult stones; if a Fire Pressure line moves and injures the Body image, they attack by fire. If Pressure is flourishing and carries Earth to control the Self, one must guard against their preparing rolling logs and catapult stones to attack; if Pressure moves and, with Fire added, controls the Self together, they will surely raise fire and burn the camp, and if it is thriving it is very serious and if weak it is somewhat better.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 393 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 393*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土官动克世爻，他兴炮石。 | 土爻官鬼发动克世爻，对方兴用炮石； | If an Earth Pressure line moves and controls the Self, the enemy uses catapult stones; |
| 火鬼发伤身象，彼用火攻 | 火爻官鬼发动伤身象，对方用火攻。 | if a Fire Pressure line moves and injures the Body image, they attack by fire. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 394 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 394*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼兴隆带土来克世者，须防彼制擂木炮石来攻。 | 官鬼兴旺带土来克世的，须防对方制备擂木、炮石来攻。 | If Pressure is flourishing and carries Earth to control the Self, one must guard against their preparing rolling logs and catapult stones to attack. |
| 若鬼动加火同来克世者，他必举火烧屯，旺则太甚，衰则稍可。 | 如果官鬼发动、加火一起克世，他必定举火烧营，旺相则太厉害，衰弱则稍微好一些。 | If Pressure moves and, with Fire added, controls the Self together, they will surely raise fire and burn the camp, and if it is thriving it is very serious and if weak it is somewhat better. |

> **按**:「擂木」是从高处滚下砸人的原木。这一条与 EL.45 连着读，也和易隐征战占「阳土鬼旺动…防炮石」（YY.65）取同一件事。
>
> **Note:** 'Leimu' are logs rolled down from a height to smash people. This entry reads on from EL.45 and also takes up the same matter as 'a yang Earth Pressure line thriving and moving ... guard against catapult stones' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.65).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.46、LY.CAREER.EL.48、LY.CAREER.YY.65

## LY.CAREER.EL.48 出师征伐：鬼藏世下克世爻切虑自兵谋主，子伏身中合应象谨离我将降夷；世下伏鬼克世则家兵杀主，世旺鬼衰奸心已蓄无隙可乘、世衰鬼旺则逆节潜谋乘虚而入（张飞受范疆之刺）；世下伏子合应则军心已离各怀去意，世衰应旺他国强我军弱（匈奴迫李陵之降）；日辰生子又加雀虎必被阴谮阳唆背义从仇

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼藏在世爻下面克世爻，切记忧虑自己的兵谋害主将；子孙伏在身爻中合应象，当心离开我的将领、投降夷人。世爻下面伏着官鬼、而又克世爻的，就是家兵杀主：如果世旺鬼衰，奸心已经存着、无隙可乘；世衰鬼旺，则叛逆潜谋，乘虚而入，所以张飞受到范疆的刺杀。如果世爻下面伏着子孙而合应爻，就是军心已经离散，各怀去意；如果世衰应旺，就是他国强横、我军卑弱，所以匈奴逼迫李陵投降。如果日辰生子孙，又加朱雀、白虎临在上面，必定被人暗中进谗、公开唆使，背弃道义、投靠仇人。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Pressure lies hidden under the Self and controls the Self, take care to worry about one's own troops plotting against the commander; if Output lies hidden in the Body and combines with the Other image, beware of the generals leaving me and surrendering to the foreigners. If a Pressure line lies hidden under the Self and also controls the Self, it is household troops killing their master: if the Self is thriving and Pressure weak, treacherous thoughts have been stored but no gap is open; if the Self is weak and Pressure thriving, the rebellion plots in secret and enters where there is a gap, as Zhang Fei was stabbed by Fan Jiang. If Output lies hidden under the Self and combines with the Other, the hearts of the army have already left and each harbours thoughts of leaving; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, the other state is strong and my army low, as the Xiongnu forced Li Ling to surrender. If the Day generates Output and the Vermilion Bird and White Tiger sit on it, one will surely be slandered in secret and incited in the open, betraying righteousness and following the enemy.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 395 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 395*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼藏世下克世爻，切虑自兵谋主。 | 官鬼藏在世爻下面克世爻，切记忧虑自己的兵谋害主将； | If Pressure lies hidden under the Self and controls the Self, take care to worry about one's own troops plotting against the commander; |
| 子伏身中合应象，谨离我将降夷。 | 子孙伏在身爻中合应象，当心离开我的将领、投降夷人。 | if Output lies hidden in the Body and combines with the Other image, beware of the generals leaving me and surrendering to the foreigners. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 396 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 396*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世下之伏鬼而克世者，则家兵杀主。 | 世爻下面伏着官鬼、而又克世爻的，就是家兵杀主。 | If a Pressure line lies hidden under the Self and also controls the Self, it is household troops killing their master. |
| 若世旺鬼衰，奸心已蓄无隙可乘，世衰鬼旺，则逆节潜谋，乘虚而入，故张飞受范疆之刺。 | 如果世旺鬼衰，奸心已经存着、无隙可乘；世衰鬼旺，则叛逆潜谋，乘虚而入，所以张飞受到范疆的刺杀。 | If the Self is thriving and Pressure weak, treacherous thoughts have been stored but no gap is open; if the Self is weak and Pressure thriving, the rebellion plots in secret and enters where there is a gap, as Zhang Fei was stabbed by Fan Jiang. |
| 若世下之伏子而合应者，则军心已离，各怀去意。 | 如果世爻下面伏着子孙而合应爻，就是军心已经离散，各怀去意。 | If Output lies hidden under the Self and combines with the Other, the hearts of the army have already left and each harbours thoughts of leaving. |
| 如世衰应旺，则他国强梁我军卑弱，故匈奴迫李陵之降。 | 如果世衰应旺，就是他国强横、我军卑弱，所以匈奴逼迫李陵投降。 | If the Self is weak and the Other thriving, the other state is strong and my army low, as the Xiongnu forced Li Ling to surrender. |
| 若日辰生子又加雀虎临之，必被阴谮阳唆，背羲从仇耳。 | 如果日辰生子孙，又加朱雀、白虎临在上面，必定被人暗中进谗、公开唆使，背弃道义、投靠仇人。 | If the Day generates Output and the Vermilion Bird and White Tiger sit on it, one will surely be slandered in secret and incited in the open, betraying righteousness and following the enemy. |

> **按**:「范疆」就是刺杀张飞的范疆、张达（《三国志》作范强、张达），「李陵」是汉武帝时兵败降匈奴的将领；这两个典故书里自己写出，和黄金策征战「子化官爻克世，张飞遭范张之诛」「外宫子动化绝爻，李陵所以降虏」（HJC.110、HJC.105）用的是同一批。「雀虎」是朱雀、白虎的省称；「背羲」是「背义」。「夷」是外族。
>
> **Note:** 'Fan Jiang' is Fan Jiang, who with Zhang Da assassinated Zhang Fei (the Records of the Three Kingdoms writes Fan Qiang and Zhang Da), and 'Li Ling' is the general of Emperor Wu of the Han who was defeated and surrendered to the Xiongnu; the book writes out both allusions itself, and they are the same pair that the Huangjin Ce uses in 'Output turns into the Pressure line and controls the Self, Zhang Fei suffers the execution by Fan and Zhang' and 'Output moves in the outer palace and turns into an extinct line, which is why Li Ling surrendered to the captors' (HJC.110, HJC.105). 'Que hu' is an abbreviation for the Vermilion Bird and the White Tiger; 'bei xi' is 'bei yi', betraying righteousness. 'Yi' is the foreign peoples.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.47、LY.CAREER.EL.49、LY.CAREER.YY.62、LY.CAREER.HJC.105、LY.CAREER.HJC.110
