## LY.CAREER.EL.49 出师征伐：内外鬼兴伤世祸由内应外通，前后子旺生身利在前攻后击；内官乃己之人外鬼为彼之兵，发动克世则己人纠合他人内应外合，世旺鬼衰难以伤克，世变子或鬼变子则鬼受制无大害，本卦子孙旺而生世先攻后取全功，内官旺相外鬼兴隆则彼此相抗难断胜负

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:内外两个官鬼兴起伤世爻，祸患由内应外通而起；前后（内外）的子孙旺相生身，利于前攻后击。内卦的官鬼是自己人，外卦的官鬼是对方的兵：如果发动而克世爻，祸患出自自己人勾结外人，这就是内应外合的残害；如果世旺鬼衰，虽然发动也难以伤克；如果世爻变子孙或官鬼变子孙，就是官鬼受制，谋划不会决裂，没有夭折的祸害，经书说「有人制鬼，鬼动何妨」；本卦子孙之卦，子孙旺相而生世爻的，先攻后取，顺着心意行军，都获得全功；如果内卦的官鬼旺相、外卦的官鬼兴旺，就彼此对抗，难以判断胜负。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Pressure rises both inside and outside and injures the Self, the disaster comes from collusion between inside and outside; if Output is thriving before and behind and generates the Body, it favours attacking in front and striking behind. The inner Pressure is one's own men and the outer Pressure is the enemy's troops: if they move and control the Self, the disaster is one's own men joining others, which is the injury of inside and outside in collusion; if the Self is thriving and Pressure weak, though they move it is hard for them to injure or control; if the Self turns into Output or Pressure turns into Output, Pressure is restrained, the plot does not break out and there is no untimely harm, as the classic says 'if someone restrains Pressure, what harm is there in its moving'; if in the original hexagram Output is thriving and generates the Self, one attacks first and takes after, and moving troops as one wishes one gains complete success; if the inner Pressure is thriving and the outer Pressure flourishing, they resist each other and it is hard to judge victory.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 398 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 398*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内外鬼兴伤世，祸由内应外通。 | 内外两个官鬼兴起伤世爻，祸患由内应外通而起； | If Pressure rises both inside and outside and injures the Self, the disaster comes from collusion between inside and outside; |
| 前后子旺生身，利在前攻后击。 | 前后（内外）的子孙旺相生身，利于前攻后击。 | if Output is thriving before and behind and generates the Body, it favours attacking in front and striking behind. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 399 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 399*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内官乃己之人，外鬼为彼之兵。 | 内卦的官鬼是自己人，外卦的官鬼是对方的兵。 | The inner Pressure is one's own men and the outer Pressure is the enemy's troops. |
| 若逢发动，而克世者，祸从己人纠合他人，是为内应外合残害。 | 如果发动而克世爻，祸患出自自己人勾结外人，这就是内应外合的残害。 | If they move and control the Self, the disaster is one's own men joining others, which is the injury of inside and outside in collusion. |
| 若世旺鬼衰，虽然发动难以伤克。 | 如果世旺鬼衰，虽然发动也难以伤克。 | If the Self is thriving and Pressure weak, though they move it is hard for them to injure or control. |
| 若世变子或鬼变子，乃鬼受制，则谋无决裂，是无夭害，经云有人制鬼，鬼动何妨。 | 如果世变子孙或官鬼变子孙，就是官鬼受制，谋划不会决裂，没有夭折的祸害，经书说「有人制鬼，鬼动何妨」。 | If the Self turns into Output or Pressure turns into Output, Pressure is restrained, the plot does not break out and there is no untimely harm, as the classic says 'if someone restrains Pressure, what harm is there in its moving'. |
| 本卦子孙之卦子孙旺而生世者，先攻后取顺意行军皆获全功。 | 本卦子孙之卦，子孙旺相而生世爻的，先攻后取，顺着心意行军，都获得全功。 | If in the original hexagram Output is thriving and generates the Self, one attacks first and takes after, and moving troops as one wishes one gains complete success. |
| 若内官旺相外鬼兴隆，则彼此相抗，难以胜断也。 | 如果内卦的官鬼旺相、外卦的官鬼兴旺，就彼此对抗，难以判断胜负。 | If the inner Pressure is thriving and the outer Pressure flourishing, they resist each other and it is hard to judge victory. |

> **按**:「内官」「外鬼」是内卦的官鬼和外卦的官鬼。「有人制鬼，鬼动何妨」是引的另一句经书，与黄金策「官化兄反、子孙制鬼」一类同义，书里没有说出处。「内应外合」是内外勾结。第一联「前后子旺」的「前后」按解里应是内外；译文写成「前后（内外）」。这一条与易隐征战占「内外两鬼动克世者，防内应外合」（YY.56）相同。
>
> **Note:** 'Inner official' and 'outer Pressure' are the Pressure lines of the inner and of the outer trigram. 'If someone restrains Pressure, what harm is there in its moving' is quoted from another classic that the book does not name, and has the same sense as 'Output restrains Pressure' sayings in the Huangjin Ce. 'Nei ying wai he' is inside and outside colluding. The 'front and back' of 'Output thriving before and behind' should be inside and outside by the explanation, and the translation writes 'before and behind (inside and outside)'. This entry is the same as 'if Pressure in both the inner and outer trigram moves to control the Self, guard against collusion between inside and outside' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.56).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.48、LY.CAREER.EL.50、LY.CAREER.YY.56、LY.CAREER.HJC.104

## LY.CAREER.EL.50 出师征伐：鬼架长生帝旺寇兴世乱之时，官居墓库空亡国泰民安之象；内外无鬼盗兵不来，世应俱空战征已绝；战征以世为主将子作兵卒、以应为彼官鬼为贼，官鬼值长生帝旺则盗寇纵横，卦中内外无官或逢墓绝世应皆空则干戈偃息国泰民安

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼乘着长生、帝旺，是贼寇兴起、世道大乱的时候；官鬼落在墓库、空亡，是国泰民安的景象；内外都没有官鬼，盗兵不来；世爻、应爻都空，战事已经断绝。战征的事，以世爻为主将，子孙当兵卒；以应爻为对方，官鬼为贼：如果官鬼值在长生、帝旺的位置，就盗寇纵横、西夷肆虐；如果卦中内外没有官鬼，或者逢墓、绝，世、应都空，就干戈止息，国泰民安了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Pressure rides Birth or Emperor's Peak, it is a time of bandits rising and the world in disorder; if Pressure sits in the tomb or in Void, it is the image of a state at peace and a people secure; if there is no Pressure inside or outside, bandit troops do not come; if the Self and Other are both Void, war has ceased. In matters of war and campaign, the Self is the commander and Output is the soldiers; the Other is the enemy and Pressure is the bandits: if Pressure sits in the position of Birth or Emperor's Peak, bandits run wild and the western barbarians rage; if the hexagram has no Pressure inside or outside, or it meets tomb or extinction, and the Self and Other are both Void, arms are laid down and the state is at peace and the people secure.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 400 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 400*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼架长生帝旺，寇兴世乱之时。 | 官鬼乘着长生、帝旺，是贼寇兴起、世道大乱的时候； | If Pressure rides Birth or Emperor's Peak, it is a time of bandits rising and the world in disorder; |
| 官居墓库空亡，国泰民安之象。 | 官鬼落在墓库、空亡，是国泰民安的景象； | if Pressure sits in the tomb or in Void, it is the image of a state at peace and a people secure; |
| 内外无鬼，盗兵不来。 | 内外都没有官鬼，盗兵不来； | if there is no Pressure inside or outside, bandit troops do not come; |
| 世应俱空，战征已绝。 | 世爻、应爻都空，战事已经断绝。 | if the Self and Other are both Void, war has ceased. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 401 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 401*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如战征之事，以世为主将，子作兵卒。 | 战征的事，以世爻为主将，子孙当兵卒。 | In matters of war and campaign, the Self is the commander and Output is the soldiers. |
| 以应为彼，官鬼为贼，如官鬼值长生帝旺之乡，则盗寇纵横，酉夷肆虐，若卦中内外无官、或逢墓绝、世应皆空，则干戈眼偃息，国泰民安矣！ | 以应爻为对方，官鬼为贼：如果官鬼值在长生、帝旺的位置，就盗寇纵横、西夷肆虐；如果卦中内外没有官鬼，或者逢墓、绝，世、应都空，就干戈止息，国泰民安了！ | The Other is the enemy and Pressure is the bandits: if Pressure sits in the position of Birth or Emperor's Peak, bandits run wild and the western barbarians rage; if the hexagram has no Pressure inside or outside, or it meets tomb or extinction, and the Self and Other are both Void, arms are laid down and the state is at peace and the people secure! |

> **按**:「酉夷」疑是「西夷」，「干戈眼偃息」的「眼」疑是「顿」，译文按「西夷」「干戈止息」译。「架」是乘、骑。这一条与易隐征战占「官鬼旺动敌势盛」「内外无鬼敌必遁去」「世应俱空两将休兵」（YY.62、YY.54）取同一类断语。
>
> **Note:** 'You yi' is presumably 'xi yi', the western barbarians, and the 'yan' of 'ganqe yan yanxi' is presumably 'dun', suddenly; the translation reads 'the western barbarians' and 'arms are laid down'. 'Jia' is to ride. This entry takes the same kind of judgment as 'Pressure thriving and moving, the enemy's force is strong', 'no Pressure inside or outside, the enemy surely slips away' and 'the Self and Other both Void, the two commanders rest their troops' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.62, YY.54).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.49、LY.CAREER.EL.51、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.YY.62、LY.CAREER.HJC.103

## LY.CAREER.EL.51 出师征伐结语：胜败我先知匪伊测度，吉凶神已告据理推详

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:胜负我先知道，不是靠揣测；吉凶神明已经告知，要依据道理推究详察。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Victory or defeat I know beforehand, and it is not by guesswork; good and ill the spirits have already told, and one should work them out by principle.

**《易林补遗·出师征伐章第五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 402 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 5: Sending out troops and campaigning", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 402*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 胜败我先知，匪伊测度。 | 胜负我先知道，不是靠揣测； | Victory or defeat I know beforehand, and it is not by guesswork; |
| 吉凶神已告，据理推详。 | 吉凶神明已经告知，要依据道理推究详察。 | good and ill the spirits have already told, and one should work them out by principle. |

> **按**:这是出师征伐章的结语，后面没有解说；「匪伊」是「不是」。
>
> **Note:** This is the closing couplet of the chapter on sending out troops and campaigning, and the book gives no explanation after it; 'fei yi' is 'not'.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.50、LY.CAREER.EL.12

## LY.CAREER.EL.62 袭求武弁总纲：以官爻为主、世象为凭；动业赞皇猷鹰扬渭水、簪缨绳祖武虎拜枫宸；大凡袭爵承官皆沐君恩祖荫，欲识利名显晦远凭卦象推详

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占承袭爵位、谋求武职，以官爻为主，以世爻为凭据。承袭爵位、接任官职，都是沾受君王的恩典和祖先的荫庇；想知道利和名是显达还是暗淡，要靠卦象去推详。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining about inheriting a title or seeking a military rank, take the Pressure line as the chief and the Self image as the ground. Inheriting a title and succeeding to an office are all through the sovereign's grace and the shade of one's ancestors; to know whether gain and name will be bright or dim, one must work it out from the images of the hexagram.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 772 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 772*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以官爻为主，世象为凭。 | 以官爻为主要看的，以世爻为凭据。 | Take the Pressure line as the chief to be looked at, and the Self image as the ground. |

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 773 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 773*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 动业赞皇猷，鹰扬渭水； | 建立功业，辅佐皇上的谋略，像在渭水边鹰扬奋起的吕尚； | Doing deeds to assist the sovereign's plans, like the eagle soaring at the Wei river; |
| 簪缨绳租武，虎拜枫宸。 | 世代簪缨的官宦子弟，继承祖先的武功，像虎臣那样在枫宸（宫廷）叩拜君王。 | the sons of capped and tasselled officials follow in the footsteps of their ancestors' martial deeds, and bow like tiger-vassals in the Maple Court. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 773 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 773*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 大凡袭爵承官，皆沐君恩祖阴，欲识利名显晦，远凭卦象推详。 | 凡是承袭爵位、接任官职，都沾受君王的恩典和祖先的荫庇；要知道利和名是显达还是暗淡，须靠卦象从远处推详。 | Whenever one inherits a title or succeeds to an office, it is all through the sovereign's grace and the shade of one's ancestors; to know whether gain and name will be bright or dim, one must rely on the images of the hexagram and reckon it out in detail from afar. |

> **按**:「鹰扬渭水」用的是吕尚（姜太公）在渭水边遇周文王、后来在牧野《诗经》称他「维师尚父，时维鹰扬」的典故；「虎拜」用的是《诗经》「虎拜稽首」，召虎受命后叩拜周王；「枫宸」指宫廷。「绳租武」的「租」是「祖」的误写，「绳祖武」是继承祖先的足迹；「祖阴」是「祖荫」，祖先的庇荫。「弁」是武弁，指武官。这一章讲的是承袭武爵和谋求武职两件事，取法与前面的仕宦升迁章一样：官爻为主、世爻为凭。
>
> **Note:** 'Yingyang Weishui' alludes to Lü Shang (the Grand Duke Jiang), who met King Wen by the Wei river and of whom the Book of Odes says 'the Grand Teacher Shangfu, here the eagle soaring'; 'hu bai' alludes to the Odes' 'the tiger bows and touches his head to the ground', Shao Hu bowing to the Zhou king after receiving his charge; 'fengchen', the Maple Court, is the imperial court. The 'zu' of 'sheng zu wu' is written with the character for 'rent' and is a miswriting of the character for 'ancestor': 'to follow the ancestors' footsteps'; the 'yin' of 'zu yin' is written with the character for 'yin and yang' and is a miswriting of 'yin', shade or protection, the shelter of ancestors. 'Bian' in the chapter title is a military officer. The chapter deals with two things, inheriting a military title and seeking a military post, and takes the same method as the preceding chapter on official promotion: the Pressure line as chief and the Self line as ground.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.63、LY.CAREER.EL.55、LY.CAREER.EL.12、LY.CAREER.HJC.24

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.63 袭求武弁：献策请缨父象要兴财忌动；献策论者专看文书，父旺必然高中、父空岂得成名，父若不空又嫌财动

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:向朝廷献策、自请从军，要看父母爻：父爻旺相，一定高中；父爻落空，怎么能成名？父爻即使不空，还忌讳财爻发动。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To submit a plan to the court and volunteer for service, look at Resource: if Resource is thriving, one will surely pass high; if Resource is Void, how can one make a name? Even if Resource is not Void, one still dislikes Wealth moving.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 773 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 773*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 献策请缨，父象要兴财忌动。 | 献策、请缨，父爻之象要兴旺，财爻忌发动。 | To submit a plan and ask for the tassel, the image of Resource must flourish and Wealth must not move. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 775 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 775*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 献策论者，专看文书，父旺必然高中，父空，岂得成名？ | 献策、论事的人，专看文书爻：父爻旺相，一定高中；父爻落空，怎么能成名？ | For those who present plans and discuss policy, look especially at the document line: if Resource is thriving, one will surely pass high; if Resource is Void, how can one make a name? |
| 父若不空又嫌财动。 | 父爻如果不空，又嫌财爻发动。 | If Resource is not Void, one still dislikes Wealth moving. |

> **按**:「请缨」出自终军「愿受长缨，必羁南越王而致之阙下」，是自请出征；「献策」是向朝廷上策论。武职这条路上献策请缨看文书爻（父母爻），因为文书就是策论、章奏。财爻忌动，是因为财克父（五行相克），书里没有明说。「高中」是考中高第。
>
> **Note:** 'Qingying', to ask for the tassel, comes from Zhong Jun's 'I wish to receive a long tassel and surely bind the king of Southern Yue and bring him to the palace', and means volunteering for a campaign; 'xiance' is presenting a policy memorial to the court. On this path to a military post, presenting a plan and volunteering is read from the document line, Resource, because the document is the memorial and the policy paper. That Wealth must not move is because Wealth controls Resource (five-element control); the book does not say so. 'Gao zhong' is passing with a high place.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.62、LY.CAREER.EL.64、LY.CAREER.HJC.05

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.64 袭求武弁：从戎比试世爻喜旺应宜衰；若交锋者又凭世应——世旺应衰他必弱、世衰应旺彼当强，世克应则大胜、应克世则难赢

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:从军、比试武艺，世爻喜欢旺相，应爻适宜衰弱。如果是临阵交锋，又凭世、应：世旺应衰，对方一定弱；世衰应旺，对方就强；世克应是大胜，应克世就难以取胜。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For joining the army or a military trial, the Self line likes to be thriving and the Other should be weak. For those who cross swords in battle, rely again on the Self and the Other: if the Self is thriving and the Other weak, the other side is surely weak; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, the other side is strong; if the Self controls the Other it is a great victory, and if the Other controls the Self it is hard to win.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 773 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 773*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 从戎比试，世爻喜旺应宜衰。 | 从军、比试，世爻喜欢旺相，应爻适宜衰弱。 | For joining the army or a military trial, the Self line likes to be thriving and the Other should be weak. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 775–776 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 775–776*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若交锋者，又凭世应，世旺应衰他必弱，世衰应旺彼当强。 | 如果是交锋（临阵交战）的，又凭世、应：世旺应衰，对方一定弱；世衰应旺，对方就强。 | For those who cross swords in battle, rely again on the Self and the Other: if the Self is thriving and the Other weak, the other side is surely weak; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, the other side is strong. |
| 世克应则大胜，应克世则难赢。 | 世爻克制应爻，就是大胜；应爻克制世爻，就难以取胜。 | If the Self controls the Other it is a great victory; if the Other controls the Self it is hard to win. |

> **按**:「又凭」在上一行末，「世应」在下一行首，这里连起来读。「交锋」是两军交战。这一条与易隐征战占「世为我帅应为彼将；世旺应衰宜攻、世衰应旺宜守；世克应可战」（YY.54）、黄金策征战「观世应之旺衰，以决两家之胜负」（HJC.86）取同一个关系：世为己方，应为对方。
>
> **Note:** 'You ping' is at the end of the previous line and 'shi ying' at the start of the next, and they are read joined up here. 'Jiaofeng' is two armies meeting in battle. This entry takes the same relation as 'the Self is my commander and the Other the enemy general; with the Self thriving and the Other weak, attack; with the Self weak and the Other thriving, defend; if the Self controls the Other, one can fight' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.54) and 'watch the strength of the Self and the Other to decide the victory of the two houses' in the Huangjin Ce (HJC.86): the Self is one's own side and the Other the opposing side.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.63、LY.CAREER.EL.65、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.HJC.86、LY.CAREER.EL.12

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.65 袭求武弁：受荫袭封喜遇贵乘禄马、买官进爵喜逢龙聚财官；所忌者六冲世陷空徒费力，所喜者六合官兴财旺总如心；军中占袭职民间卜官各忌世空官空六冲之卦，得官与世旺定显威风，官世带青龙贵人及卦逢六合威福更加

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:靠祖荫承袭封爵，喜欢遇到贵人乘着禄、马；捐买官职、进升爵位，喜欢遇到青龙聚着财爻、官爻。所忌讳的是六冲、世爻落陷落空，白费力气；所喜欢的是六合、官鬼兴旺、财爻旺相，事事称心。军中占承袭职位、民间卜官职，各自都忌世爻空、官空和六冲的卦；如果官与世都旺相，一定显出威风；官爻或世爻带青龙、贵人，以及卦遇六合，威势和福分更加增添。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To inherit a title by ancestral privilege, one is glad to meet the Noble riding on the Emolument and the Horse; to buy an office and advance in rank, one is glad to meet the Dragon gathering Wealth and the star of office. What is feared is a six-clash with the Self sunk or Void, which is wasted effort; what is welcomed is a six-combine with Pressure flourishing and Wealth thriving, everything as one wishes. In the army divining for inheriting a post, or among the people divining for office, each fears the Self Void, the star of office Void, and a six-clash hexagram; if the star of office and the Self are both thriving, one will surely show authority; if the Pressure line or the Self line carries the Azure Dragon or the Noble, or the hexagram meets a six-combine, authority and fortune are further increased.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 777 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 777*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 受荫袭封，喜遇贵乘禄马。 | 靠祖荫承袭封爵，喜欢遇到贵人乘着禄、马； | To inherit a title by ancestral privilege, one is glad to meet the Noble riding on the Emolument and the Horse; |
| 买官进爵，恢逢龙聚财官。 | 捐买官职、进升爵位，喜欢遇到青龙聚着财爻、官爻。 | to buy an office and advance in rank, one is glad to meet the Dragon gathering Wealth and the star of office. |
| 所忌者六冲世陷空徒费力， | 所忌讳的是六冲、世爻落陷落空，白费力气； | What is feared is a six-clash with the Self sunk or Void, which is wasted effort; |
| 所喜者六合官兴财旺总如心。 | 所喜欢的是六合、官鬼兴旺、财爻旺相，总是称心如意。 | what is welcomed is a six-combine with Pressure flourishing and Wealth thriving, everything as one wishes. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 778 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 778*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 军中占袭职，民间卜官，各忌世空官空、六冲之卦，若得官与世旺，定显威风。 | 军中占承袭职位、民间卜官职，各自都忌讳世爻空、官空和六冲的卦；如果官与世都旺相，一定显出威风。 | In the army divining for inheriting a post, or among the people divining for office, each fears the Self Void, the star of office Void, and a six-clash hexagram; if the star of office and the Self are both thriving, one will surely show authority. |
| 官爻或世爻若带青龙贵人、及卦逢六合，威福更加。 | 官爻或者世爻如果带青龙、贵人，以及卦遇六合，威势和福分更加增添。 | If the Pressure line or the Self line carries the Azure Dragon or the Noble, or the hexagram meets a six-combine, authority and fortune are further increased. |

> **按**:「受荫」是靠祖先的功劳取得封爵；「买官进爵」是捐纳。「恢逢」疑是「喜逢」（上一联是「喜遇」），译作「喜欢遇到」。「贵乘禄马」是贵人、禄、驿马这几样同时遇上（见术语表）。「官空」的「官」是官鬼。「六合」「六冲」指卦的六合、六冲。这一条与易隐官禄占「何官」一组里的「世持官贵禄马」（YY.03、YY.04）取同一类象。
>
> **Note:** 'Shou yin' is obtaining a title through the merit of one's ancestors; 'mai guan jin jue' is buying office and rank by contribution. The 'hui' of 'hui feng' is presumably 'xi', glad (the couplet before has 'xi yu'), and is translated 'glad to meet'. 'Gui cheng lu ma' is the Noble, the Emolument and the Post Horse met together (see the glossary). The 'guan' of 'guan kong' is Pressure. 'Six-combine' and 'six-clash' are the hexagram patterns. This entry takes the same kind of image as 'the Self holds the Noble, Emolument and Horse' in the 'which office' group of the Yi Yin divination on official salary (YY.03, YY.04).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.64、LY.CAREER.EL.66、LY.CAREER.YY.03、LY.CAREER.YY.04

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.66 袭求武弁：鬼在酉申耀武扬威膺世禄、官临乾兑攘夷发夏树奇功；官爻或世爻如临乾兑卦中或在酉申爻内俱当名振华夷

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼在酉爻、申爻，显耀武功、扬起威风，享受世袭的俸禄；官爻落在乾卦、兑卦，抵御外族、振奋华夏，树立非凡的功劳。官爻或者世爻如果落在乾、兑卦中，或者在酉、申爻上，都会名声震动华夏和外族。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Pressure is on a You or Shen line, one displays martial prowess and raises one's awe, and receives a hereditary emolument; if the star of office is in the Qian or Dui trigram, one drives out the barbarians, raises up the Hua-Xia, and sets up extraordinary merit. If the Pressure line or the Self line falls in the Qian or Dui trigram, or is on a You or Shen line, in every case one's name shakes both the Chinese and the barbarians.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 779 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 779*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼在酉申，耀武扬威膺世禄。 | 官鬼在酉爻、申爻，显耀武功、扬起威风，享受世袭的俸禄； | If Pressure is on a You or Shen line, one displays martial prowess and raises one's awe, and receives a hereditary emolument; |
| 官临乾兑，攘夷发夏树奇功。 | 官爻落在乾卦、兑卦，抵御外族、振奋华夏，树立非凡的功劳。 | if the star of office is in the Qian or Dui trigram, one drives out the barbarians, raises up the Hua-Xia, and sets up extraordinary merit. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 780 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 780*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻或世爻如临乾兑卦中，或在酉申爻内，俱当名振华夷。 | 官爻或者世爻如果落在乾、兑卦中，或者在酉、申爻上，都会名声震动华夏和外族。 | If the Pressure line or the Self line falls in the Qian or Dui trigram, or is on a You or Shen line, in every case one's name shakes both the Chinese and the barbarians. |

> **按**:酉、申属金，乾、兑也属金；金主兵刃，所以取武职要看它们（黄金策征战「金为刀箭」，HJC.111）。「世禄」是世代承袭的俸禄。「攘夷发夏」的「发夏」疑是「发扬华夏」，译作「振奋华夏」；「华夷」是华夏和外族。
>
> **Note:** You and Shen belong to Metal, and Qian and Dui also belong to Metal; Metal governs blades and weapons, which is why they are looked at for military posts (the Huangjin Ce on campaigns says 'Metal is knife and arrow', HJC.111). 'Shi lu' is an emolument handed down from generation to generation. The 'fa xia' of 'rang yi fa xia' is presumably 'fayang Huaxia', to bring glory to the Chinese, and is translated 'raises up the Hua-Xia'; 'hua yi' is the Chinese and the barbarians.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.65、LY.CAREER.EL.67、LY.CAREER.HJC.111、LY.CAREER.YY.66

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.67 袭求武弁：内外卦得兴隆则功高誉远、世旺逢生勇冠三军蒙上赏、官兴得助爵尊一品沐殊恩、身遇岁君生合战征场内作高魁；武职行中得内外卦兴隆世应旺相势压旁疆，世旺再逢太岁或日辰月建生扶者勇冠三军

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:武职之中，内卦、外卦都兴旺，功劳就高、名誉就远；世爻旺相又逢生，勇冠三军、蒙受上司的赏赐；官爻兴旺又得帮助，爵位尊到一品、沐浴特殊的恩典；卦身遇到太岁生合，在战场上拔得头魁。内外卦兴旺、世应旺相，威势一定压倒邻近的疆界；世旺再遇太岁，或者日辰、月建生扶的，勇冠三军。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Among military posts, if the inner and outer trigrams are both flourishing, merit is high and fame far; if the Self is thriving and is generated, one's courage tops the three armies and one receives the superior's reward; if the star of office flourishes and gains aid, one's rank is of the first grade and one bathes in special favour; if the Body meets generation or combination from Tai Sui, one becomes the top man in the field of war. With the inner and outer trigrams flourishing and the Self and the Other thriving, one's power will surely overawe the neighbouring borders; if the Self is thriving and also meets Tai Sui, or the Day and the Month generate and support it, one's courage tops the three armies.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 781 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 781*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内外卦得兴隆，则功高誉远。 | 内卦、外卦都兴旺，功劳就高、名誉就远。 | If the inner and outer trigrams are flourishing, then merit is high and fame travels far. |

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 781 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 781*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世旺逢生，勇冠三军蒙上赏，官兴得助爵尊一品沐殊恩。 | 世爻旺相又逢生，勇气冠绝三军、蒙受上司的赏赐；官爻兴旺又得帮助，爵位尊到一品、沐浴特殊的恩典。 | If the Self is thriving and meets generation, one's courage tops the three armies and receives the superior's reward; if the star of office flourishes and gains aid, one's rank is of the first grade and one bathes in special favour. |

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 781 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 781*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身遇岁君生合，战征场内作高魁。 | 卦身遇到太岁生合，在战场上拔得头魁。 | If the Body meets generation or combination from Tai Sui, in the field of war one becomes the top man. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 782 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 782*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 武职行中，若得内卦外卦兴隆，世爻应爻旺相，定然势压旁疆。 | 在武职之中，如果内卦、外卦兴旺，世爻、应爻旺相，威势一定压倒邻近的疆界。 | Among the military posts, if the inner and outer trigrams are flourishing and the Self and the Other lines are thriving, one's power will surely overawe the neighbouring borders. |
| 世旺再逢太岁，或日辰月建生扶者，勇冠三军。 | 世爻旺相，又遇太岁，或者日辰、月建生扶它的，勇冠三军。 | If the Self is thriving and also meets Tai Sui, or the Day and the Month generate and support it, one's courage tops the three armies. |

> **按**:这一首诀有五联，这一条取其中好的三联和解里前两句，坏的两联放在下一条（EL.68）。「身」是卦身（见术语表），「岁君」是太岁。「戈戟」「三军」是军队。「魁」是第一名。「内外卦」是内卦（下卦）和外卦（上卦）。这一条与易隐征战占「世持虎福旺相决胜千里，加日辰生扶百战百胜」（YY.55）是同一类断语。
>
> **Note:** This verse has five couplets; this entry takes the three auspicious ones and the first two sentences of the explanation, and the two ominous couplets are in the next entry (EL.68). 'Shen' is the Body of the hexagram (see the glossary), and 'suijun' is Tai Sui. 'Sanjun' is the armies. 'Kui' is the first place. The 'inner and outer trigrams' are the inner (lower) and outer (upper) trigrams. This entry is the same kind of judgment as 'the Self holds the Tiger and Output, thriving, decides victory a thousand miles away; with the Day generating and supporting it, a hundred battles, a hundred victories' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.55).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.66、LY.CAREER.EL.68、LY.CAREER.YY.55

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.68 袭求武弁：身世爻当衰弱则力怯机疏、世逢日月克冲戈戟最为下辈；世若休囚卦不旺相当推无力无能，又被日辰月建冲克世爻者提拔全无反遭弹劾，其中又看官爻旺则声名显赫空则劾削兵权

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:身爻、世爻衰弱，就力量怯弱、机会稀少；世爻遇到日、月克制或冲，在拿戈戟的人当中也是最下等的。世爻如果休囚、卦又不旺相，就该断为没有力量、没有才能；又被日辰、月建冲克世爻的，完全没有提拔，反而遭到弹劾，哪里能在军队中出名？这中间还要看官爻：旺相就声名显赫，落空就被弹劾、削去兵权。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Body and Self lines are weak, one's strength is timid and one's chances sparse; if the Self meets control or clash from the Day and the Month, even among bearers of spear and halberd one is of the lowest rank. If the Self is at rest or imprisoned and the hexagram is not thriving, one should be judged as without strength or ability; if the Self line is again clashed or controlled by the Day and the Month, there is no advancement at all and on the contrary impeachment, so how could one win fame in the ranks? Among these, look also at the Pressure line: if it is thriving, fame is eminent; if it is Void, one is impeached and stripped of military power.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 781 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 781*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身世爻当衰弱，则力怯机疏。 | 身爻、世爻衰弱，就力量怯弱、机会稀少。 | If the Body and Self lines are weak, then one's strength is timid and one's chances sparse. |

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 781 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 781*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世逢日月克冲，戈戟最为下辈。 | 世爻遇到日、月克制或冲，拿戈戟的人当中也是最下等的。 | If the Self meets control or clash from the Day and the Month, even among bearers of spear and halberd one is of the lowest rank. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 782 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 782*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世若休囚，卦不旺相，当推无力无能。 | 世爻如果休囚，卦又不旺相，就该断为没有力量、没有才能。 | If the Self is at rest or imprisoned and the hexagram is not thriving, one should be judged as without strength or ability. |
| 又被日辰月建冲克世爻者，提拔全无，反遭弹劾，岂得驰名于军伍？ | 又被日辰、月建冲克世爻的，完全没有提拔，反而遭到弹劾，哪里能在军队中出名？ | If the Self line is again clashed or controlled by the Day and the Month, there is no advancement at all and on the contrary impeachment, so how could one win fame in the ranks? |
| 其中又看官爻，旺则声名显赫，空则劾削兵权。 | 这中间还要看官爻：旺相就声名显赫，落空就被弹劾、削去兵权。 | Among these, look also at the Pressure line: if it is thriving, fame is eminent; if it is Void, one is impeached and stripped of military power. |

> **按**:「休囚」是休、囚两种不得令的状态（见术语表）。「戈戟最为下辈」是拿戈戟的兵卒里也是最低的一等。「劾削兵权」是被弹劾、削去兵权。这一条与增删卜易防参劾「身衰鬼克，贬责凌呵」（ZS.41）、黄金策仕宦「日辰冲克，定然诽谤之多招」（HJC.12）取同一个关系：日月冲克世爻，有祸。
>
> **Note:** 'Xiu qiu' are the two states of 'at rest' and 'imprisoned', both out of season (see the glossary). 'Ge ji zui wei xia bei' is the lowest grade even among the soldiers who carry spears and halberds. 'Hexue bingquan' is being impeached and stripped of military power. This entry takes the same relation as 'a weak Self controlled by Pressure brings demotion and rebuke' in the Zengshan Buyi on guarding against impeachment (ZS.41) and 'if the Day clashes or controls, there will surely be much slander' in the Huangjin Ce on official careers (HJC.12): the Day and Month clashing or controlling the Self line means trouble.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.67、LY.CAREER.EL.69、LY.CAREER.ZS.41、LY.CAREER.HJC.12

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.69 袭求武弁：世带青龙名驰朝野、官居白虎威振华夷；青龙为大贵之星、白虎乃兵权之煞，此二兽若得临世临官定主威扬海内

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻带青龙，名声传遍朝廷和民间；官爻坐着白虎，威势震动华夏和外族。青龙是大贵的星，白虎是兵权的煞，这两兽如果能临在世爻上、官爻上，一定主威名传扬海内。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self carries the Azure Dragon, one's name runs through court and countryside; if the star of office sits on the White Tiger, one's might shakes the Chinese and the barbarians. The Azure Dragon is the star of great nobility and the White Tiger is the baleful spirit of military power; if these two beasts come to the Self or to the star of office, one's might will surely spread throughout the land.

**《易林补遗·袭求武弁章第三十》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 783 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 30: Inheriting and seeking military rank", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 783*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世带青龙，名驰朝野。 | 世爻带青龙，名声传遍朝廷和民间； | If the Self carries the Azure Dragon, one's name runs through court and countryside; |
| 官居白虎，威振华夷。 | 官爻坐着白虎，威势震动华夏和外族。 | if the star of office sits on the White Tiger, one's might shakes the Chinese and the barbarians. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 784 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 784*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 青龙为大贵之星，白虎乃兵权之煞，此二兽若得临世临官，定主威扬海内。 | 青龙是大贵的星，白虎是兵权的煞，这两兽如果能临在世爻上、官爻上，一定主威名传扬海内。 | The Azure Dragon is the star of great nobility and the White Tiger is the baleful spirit of military power; if these two beasts come to the Self or to the star of office, one's might will surely spread throughout the land. |

> **按**:「二兽」是青龙、白虎。白虎在别的断法里多是凶煞（黄金策、易隐里常见），这里因为问的是武职，把它看作兵权之煞，与青龙并取；这是这一章的取法，不是白虎的通例。「海内」是天下。
>
> **Note:** The 'two beasts' are the Azure Dragon and the White Tiger. In other methods the White Tiger is mostly a baleful spirit (as often seen in the Huangjin Ce and the Yi Yin), but since the question here is about a military post it is taken as the baleful spirit of military power and is taken together with the Azure Dragon; this is the method of this chapter and not the general rule for the White Tiger. 'Hainei' is all under heaven.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.68、LY.CAREER.EL.70、LY.CAREER.YY.55

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.124 投充兵卒总纲：以世身为主、财福为凭；投兵须把世爻详，逢旺逢生去必昌，若遇旬空无对敌，如逢月破丧他乡；凡占投充兵者世以为用，得日辰生世或世旺相则功成利厚力加体康，若值旬空则无人对敌，若逢月破则大有刑伤大不吉利

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:投军当兵，以世爻、身爻为主，以财爻、子孙（福）为凭据。投兵必须细看世爻：逢旺、逢生，去了必定兴旺；如果遇到旬空，就没有对手可打；如果逢月破，就死在他乡。凡是占投充当兵的，以世爻为用，只要得到日辰生世、或者世旺相，就功成利厚、力量增加、身体康健；如果值旬空，就没有人对敌；如果逢月破，就大有刑伤，非常不吉利。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In enlisting as a soldier, take the Self and the Body as the chief and Wealth and Output as the ground. In joining the army one must examine the Self line in detail: if it meets thriving or generation, going will surely bring prosperity; if it meets the ten-day Void, there is no enemy to face; if it meets Month-break, one dies in a foreign land. In every divination about enlisting as a soldier, the Self is the Subject: if the Day generates the Self or the Self is thriving, one's service is accomplished, the profit is great, one's strength increases and one's body is hale; if it falls on the ten-day Void there is no one to oppose; if it meets Month-break there is much punishment and injury and it is greatly inauspicious.

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2512 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2512*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以世身为主，财福为凭。 | 以世爻、身爻为主要看的，以财爻、子孙（福）为凭据。 | Take the Self and the Body as the chief to be looked at, and Wealth and Output as the ground. |

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2513 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2513*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 投兵须把世爻详，逢旺逢生去必昌， | 投军必须细看世爻：逢旺、逢生，去了必定兴旺； | In joining the army one must examine the Self line in detail: if it meets thriving or generation, going will surely bring prosperity; |
| 若遇旬空无对敌，如逢月破丧他乡。 | 如果遇到旬空，就没有对手可打；如果逢月破，就死在他乡。 | if it meets the ten-day Void, there is no enemy to face; if it meets Month-break, one dies in a foreign land. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 2515–2516 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 2515–2516*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡占投充兵者，世以为用，但得日辰生世或世旺相，则功成利厚力加体康，若德旬空，则无人对敌。 | 凡是占投充当兵的，以世爻为用，只要得到日辰生世、或者世旺相，就功成利厚、力量增加、身体康健；如果值旬空，就没有人对敌。 | In every divination about enlisting as a soldier, the Self is the Subject: if the Day generates the Self or the Self is thriving, one's service is accomplished, the profit is great, one's strength increases and one's body is hale; if it falls on the ten-day Void there is no one to oppose. |
| 若逢月破，则大有刑伤，大不吉利。 | 如果逢月破，就大有刑伤，非常不吉利。 | If it meets Month-break there is much punishment and injury and it is greatly inauspicious. |

> **按**:「投充」是应募、投身，「投充兵」就是投军当兵。「世身」是世爻和卦身。「德旬空」的「德」疑是「值」，译作「值旬空」。「旬空无对敌」是没有对手可以交战，书里没有说这是好是坏，这里只照字面译。「丧他乡」是死在外乡。这是书中事业各章的最后一章。
>
> **Note:** 'Touchong' is volunteering and committing oneself, and 'touchong bing' is joining the army as a soldier. 'Shishen' is the Self line and the Body of the hexagram. The 'de' of 'de xunkong' is presumably 'zhi', to fall on, and it is translated 'falls on the ten-day Void'. 'Xunkong wu duidi' is having no opponent to fight; the book does not say whether this is good or bad and it is translated literally. 'Sang taxiang' is dying in a foreign land. This is the last of the book's chapters on a career.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.125、LY.CAREER.EL.12、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.HJC.86

## LY.CAREER.EL.125 投充兵卒：随官入墓身遭厄、助鬼伤身命受殃；随军出战所忌者官也，临世临身临命而入墓者名曰随鬼入墓必有丧亡之凶难，鬼宜安静若逢财动能助鬼兴又伤身世者名曰助鬼伤身决主身陷命倾难归乡井

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻随着官爻入墓，身遭厄难；助官鬼伤身，性命受殃。随军出战，所忌的是官爻。如果官爻临在世爻、身爻、命爻上而入墓，叫做随鬼入墓，必定有丧亡的凶难；官鬼适宜安静，如果遇到财爻发动、能帮助官鬼兴旺，又伤克身爻、世爻，叫做助鬼伤身，一定主身陷险地、性命倾覆，难以回到家乡。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self follows the star of office into the tomb, one's person meets disaster; if one helps Pressure to injure the Body, one's life suffers calamity. In going out to battle with the army, what is feared is the star of office. If it rests on the Self, the Body or the fate line and enters the tomb, it is called following Pressure into the tomb, and there will surely be the misfortune of death; Pressure is best quiet, and if Wealth moves and can help Pressure to flourish, and also injures the Body or the Self, it is called helping Pressure to injure the Body, and it surely means that one's person falls into danger, one's life is overturned, and it is hard to return to one's native village.

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2517 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2517*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 随官入墓身遭厄，助鬼伤身命受殃。 | 世爻随着官爻入墓，身遭厄难；助官鬼伤身，性命受殃。 | If the Self follows the star of office into the tomb, one's person meets disaster; if one helps Pressure to injure the Body, one's life suffers calamity. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 2518 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 2518*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 随军出战，所忌者官也。 | 随军出战，所忌的是官爻。 | In going out to battle with the army, what is feared is the star of office. |
| 若临世临身临命而入墓者，名曰随鬼入墓，必有丧亡之凶难。 | 如果官爻临在世爻、身爻、命爻上而入墓，叫做随鬼入墓，必定有丧亡的凶难。 | If it rests on the Self, the Body or the fate line and enters the tomb, it is called following Pressure into the tomb, and there will surely be the misfortune of death. |
| 鬼宜安静，若逢财动能助鬼兴，又伤身世者，名曰助鬼伤身，决主身陷命倾，难归乡井。 | 官鬼适宜安静；如果遇到财爻发动、能帮助官鬼兴旺，又伤克身爻、世爻，叫做助鬼伤身，一定主身陷险地、性命倾覆，难以回到家乡。 | Pressure is best quiet, and if Wealth moves and can help Pressure to flourish, and also injures the Body or the Self, it is called helping Pressure to injure the Body, and it surely means that one's person falls into danger, one's life is overturned, and it is hard to return to one's native village. |

> **按**:「随官入墓」「助鬼伤身」增删卜易对它们另有说法（ZS.06）：古人当作凶，作者说世旺的反而升官；那是问升选，这里是随军出战，问的事不同，取法也不同，这里一律以官鬼为大忌。「临命」的「命」按文意是本命。「乡井」是家乡。
>
> **Note:** 'Following the star of office into the tomb' and 'helping Pressure to injure the Body' have another account in the Zengshan Buyi (ZS.06): the ancients took them as ominous, and the author said that a thriving Self on the contrary rises; that was asking about promotion and selection, while here it is going out to battle with the army, so the matter asked differs and so does the method, and here Pressure is throughout the great taboo. The 'fate' of 'lin ming' is by the sense the native fate. 'Xiangjing' is one's native place.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.124、LY.CAREER.EL.126、LY.CAREER.ZS.06、LY.CAREER.HJC.99

## LY.CAREER.EL.126 投充兵卒：官鬼若还冲克世不如不去反为良；军旅之忌惟以官鬼，若来冲世不过被彼克伐未决输赢，若来克世大有刑伤乃未沾利禄先遭戕戮，不如安分且守故乡

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼如果冲克世爻，不如不去，反而是好。军旅的忌讳，只在官鬼：它来冲世，不过是被对方克伐，还没有决出输赢；它如果来克世，大有刑伤，就是还没有沾到利禄，就先遭杀戮；不如安守本分，暂且守着故乡。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Pressure clashes with or controls the Self, it is better not to go, which is on the contrary good. The taboo of the army is nothing but Pressure: if it comes to clash with the Self, one is merely attacked by the other side and victory or defeat is not yet decided; if it comes to control the Self, there is much punishment and injury, and before one has shared in any pay or rank one is struck down; it is better to keep to one's lot and hold one's native place for now.

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2519 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2519*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼若还冲克世，曾如不去反为良。 | 官鬼如果冲克世爻，不如不去，反而是好。 | If Pressure clashes with or controls the Self, it is better not to go, which is on the contrary good. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 2520 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 2520*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 军旅之忌，惟以官鬼，若来冲世，不过被彼克伐，未决输赢。 | 军旅的忌讳，只在官鬼；它来冲世，不过是被对方克伐，还没有决出输赢。 | The taboo of the army is nothing but Pressure: if it comes to clash with the Self, one is merely attacked by the other side and victory or defeat is not yet decided. |
| 若来克世，大有刑伤，乃未沾利禄，选遭戕戮。 | 它如果来克世，大有刑伤，就是还没有沾到利禄，就先遭杀戮。 | If it comes to control the Self, there is much punishment and injury, and before one has shared in any pay or rank one is struck down. |
| 不如安分，且守故乡。 | 不如安守本分，暂且守着故乡。 | It is better to keep to one's lot and hold one's native place for now. |

> **按**:诀里「曾如不去反为良」的「曾如」按文意是「不如」，译作「不如不去」。「选遭戕戮」的「选」疑是「先」，译作「先遭杀戮」。这一条与前面一条（EL.125）取同一个关系：官鬼是随军出战的大忌。
>
> **Note:** The 'zeng ru' of 'zeng ru bu qu fan wei liang' in the verse is by the sense 'bu ru', better not, and it is translated 'it is better not to go'. The 'xuan' of 'xuan zao qianglu' is presumably 'xian', first, and it is translated 'before one has shared in any pay or rank one is struck down'. This entry takes the same relation as the previous one (EL.125): Pressure is the great taboo of going out to battle with the army.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.125、LY.CAREER.EL.127

## LY.CAREER.EL.127 投充兵卒：土官克世遭坑陷、金鬼冲身刀箭伤、水鬼定然逢波险、火官必主犯红光、木爻值鬼多刑责，福德交重最吉祥；克世之官大凶之象须分五属可以拒避，子孙为和解之神须得一发则万祸自消

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:土属的官克世，遭坑陷；金属的官鬼冲身，被刀箭所伤；水属的官鬼，一定遇到风波险阻；火属的官，必主触犯火光；木爻值官鬼，多有刑责；福德（子孙）发动，最吉祥。克世的官是大凶之象，必须分它属于五行中的哪一类，才可以躲避：官鬼属土，要防对方挖陷坑，又要防疾病；金属的官鬼，忌讳锋利的刀箭，也防跌扑；水属的官鬼，必然设下背水的计谋，又被风波险阻；火属的官鬼，只要逃火患，又要顾虑营屯被烧的劫难；木属的官鬼，会有鞭打杖责之刑。子孙是和解之神，只要发动一次，万般祸患自然消散。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If an Earth star of office controls the Self, one falls into a pit-trap; if a Metal Pressure clashes with the Body, one is hurt by knife and arrow; if a Water Pressure, one will surely meet the danger of waves; if a Fire star of office, one will surely run into the red glare; if a Wood line holds Pressure, there are many beatings and punishments; Output, the fortune virtue, moving is most auspicious. The star of office that controls the Self is an image of great ill, and one must tell which of the five kinds it belongs to in order to ward it off: if Pressure is of Earth, guard against the other side digging a trap-pit for one to fall into, and against illness; a Metal Pressure fears sharp blades and arrows, and one must guard against falls; a Water Pressure surely sets a back-to-the-river stratagem, and one is also blocked by wind and waves; with a Fire Pressure one need only flee the danger of fire and also fear the calamity of the camp being burned; a Wood Pressure brings the punishment of whip and cane. Output is the spirit of reconciliation, and if it moves even once, ten thousand calamities melt away of themselves.

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2521 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2521*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土官克世遭坑陷，金鬼冲身刀箭伤， | 土属的官克世，遭坑陷；金属的官鬼冲身，被刀箭所伤； | If an Earth star of office controls the Self, one falls into a pit-trap; if a Metal Pressure clashes with the Body, one is hurt by knife and arrow; |
| 水鬼定然逢波险，火官必主犯红光， | 水属的官鬼，一定遇到风波险阻；火属的官，必主触犯火光； | if a Water Pressure, one will surely meet the danger of waves; if a Fire star of office, one will surely run into the red glare; |
| 木爻值鬼多刑责，福德交重最吉祥。 | 木爻值官鬼，多有刑责；福德发动，最吉祥。 | if a Wood line holds Pressure, there are many beatings and punishments; Output, the fortune virtue, moving is most auspicious. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 2522 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 2522*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 克世之官，大凶之象，须分五属可以拒避，如鬼属土，防彼掘陷坑入，又防疾病。 | 克世的官是大凶之象，必须分它属于五行中的哪一类，才可以躲避：官鬼属土，要防对方挖陷坑，又要防疾病。 | The star of office that controls the Self is an image of great ill, and one must tell which of the five kinds it belongs to in order to ward it off: if Pressure is of Earth, guard against the other side digging a trap-pit for one to fall into, and against illness. |
| 金鬼忌伊利锋刀箭，及防跌蹼。 | 金属的官鬼，忌讳锋利的刀箭，也要防跌扑。 | A Metal Pressure fears sharp blades and arrows, and one must guard against falls. |
| 水鬼必设背水之略，又被风波险阻。 | 水属的官鬼，必然设下背水的计谋，又被风波险阻。 | A Water Pressure surely sets a back-to-the-river stratagem, and one is also blocked by wind and waves. |
| 火鬼唯逃火患，又虑烧屯之劫。 | 火属的官鬼，只要逃火患，又要顾虑营屯被烧的劫难。 | With a Fire Pressure one need only flee the danger of fire and also fear the calamity of the camp being burned. |
| 木鬼是有鞭朴之刑。 | 木属的官鬼，会有鞭打杖责之刑。 | A Wood Pressure brings the punishment of whip and cane. |
| 子孙为和解之神，须得一发，则万祸自消矣！ | 子孙是和解之神，只要发动一次，万般祸患自然消散！ | Output is the spirit of reconciliation, and if it moves even once, ten thousand calamities melt away of themselves! |

> **按**:「五属」是官鬼所属的五行。「坑陷」是陷阱。「红光」是火光，指火灾。「背水之略」是背水结阵、没有退路的战法，用的是韩信背水一战的典故。「烧屯」是烧营寨、屯粮之所。「鞭朴」是鞭打杖责。这一条与易隐征战占「五行」一组（YY.63 起：木为舟楫、火营寨、土炮石、金刀刃、水水泉）取同一类五行取象，但这里是按官鬼所属的五行看灾：土陷坑、金刀箭、水波险、火火患、木鞭朴。「和解之神」是能化解凶的神，即子孙（福神）。
>
> **Note:** 'Five kinds' are the five elements to which Pressure belongs. 'Kengxian' is a pit-trap. 'Hongguang' is the red glare, meaning fire. 'Beishui zhi lüe' is the stratagem of drawing up with the back to the water with no way to retreat, and alludes to Han Xin's battle with his back to the river. 'Shaotun' is burning the camp and the place where grain is stored. 'Bianpu' is the whip and cane. This entry takes the same kind of five-element images as the 'five elements' group of the Yi Yin war divination (from YY.63: Wood is boats, Fire is the camp, Earth is catapult stones, Metal is blades, Water is water and springs), but here the disaster is read by the element of Pressure: Earth the pit, Metal blade and arrow, Water the danger of waves, Fire the fire, Wood the whip and cane. 'The spirit of reconciliation' is the spirit that can dissolve ill, that is Output (the fortune spirit).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.126、LY.CAREER.EL.128、LY.CAREER.YY.63、LY.CAREER.YY.65、LY.CAREER.YY.66、LY.CAREER.HJC.111

## LY.CAREER.EL.128 投充兵卒：世去克他宜出战、应来克我莫登场；世为我应为彼，若世克应爻则战必胜彼，应若克世彼必多能

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻去克对方，适宜出战；应爻来克我，不要登场。世爻是我，应爻是对方：如果世克应爻，打仗必定胜过对方；应如果克世，对方一定很有本事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self goes to control the other, it is fitting to go out and fight; if the Other comes to control me, do not enter the field. The Self is me and the Other is the enemy: if the Self controls the Other line, one will surely beat the other in battle; if the Other controls the Self, he is surely very able.

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2523 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2523*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世去克他宜出战，应来克我莫登场。 | 世爻去克对方，适宜出战；应爻来克我，不要登场。 | If the Self goes to control the other, it is fitting to go out and fight; if the Other comes to control me, do not enter the field. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 2524 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 2524*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世为我，应为彼，若世克应爻，则战必胜彼。 | 世爻是我，应爻是对方；如果世克应爻，就一定战胜对方。 | The Self is me and the Other is the enemy: if the Self controls the Other line, one will surely beat the other in battle. |
| 应若克世，彼必多能。 | 应爻如果克世，对方一定很有本事。 | If the Other controls the Self, he is surely very able. |

> **按**:这一条和前面的「官鬼是随军出战的大忌」是两层：官鬼看自己会不会遭难，世应看这一仗能不能胜。与出师征伐章（EL.12 起）、易隐征战占「世克应可战、应克世宜守防贼」（YY.54）和本书前面「世克应则大胜，应克世则难赢」（EL.64）取同一个关系。
>
> **Note:** This entry and the earlier 'Pressure is the great taboo of going out to battle' are two layers: Pressure shows whether one will meet disaster, and the Self and the Other show whether this battle can be won. It takes the same relation as the chapter on sending out troops (from EL.12), 'if the Self controls the Other one can fight, if the Other controls the Self defend and guard against bandits' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.54), and 'if the Self controls the Other it is a great victory, if the Other controls the Self it is hard to win' earlier in this book (EL.64).

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.127、LY.CAREER.EL.129、LY.CAREER.EL.64、LY.CAREER.YY.54、LY.CAREER.HJC.86

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.129 投充兵卒：财旺饷资加倍得、兄兴诚恐灭兵粮；财乃口粮旺相则丰余休囚则不敷，兄为耗祟安静则可发动则有扣除之患

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:财旺相，饷银资助成倍得到；兄爻兴旺，实在怕兵粮被灭掉。财是口粮，旺相就丰足有余，休囚就不够；兄是耗损的祸祟，安静就可以，发动就有被克扣的忧患。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Wealth is thriving, pay and supplies are gained doubled; if Peer flourishes, one truly fears the soldier's rations being wiped out. Wealth is the ration, which when thriving is abundant and when resting or imprisoned is not enough; Peer is the baleful drain, and if quiet it is tolerable, and if it moves there is the trouble of deductions.

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2525 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2525*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财旺饷资加倍得，兄兴诚恐灭兵粮。 | 财旺相，饷银资助成倍得到；兄爻兴旺，实在怕兵粮被灭掉。 | If Wealth is thriving, pay and supplies are gained doubled; if Peer flourishes, one truly fears the soldier's rations being wiped out. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 2526 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 2526*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财乃口粮，旺相则丰余，休囚则不敷，兄为耗祟，安静则可。 | 财是口粮，旺相就丰足有余，休囚就不够；兄是耗损的祸祟，安静就可以。 | Wealth is the ration, which when thriving is abundant and when resting or imprisoned is not enough; Peer is the baleful drain, and if quiet it is tolerable. |
| 发动，则有扣除之患。 | 发动，就有被克扣的忧患。 | If it moves, there is the trouble of deductions. |

> **按**:「饷」是军饷，「口粮」是口粮、兵粮。「耗祟」是耗损钱粮的祸祟。「扣除」是克扣。这一条与易隐征战占「财旺相粮草丰足……内兄动克财」（YY.59）、黄金策征战「财为粮草之本根，兄乃伏兵之形势」（HJC.88）取同一类象：财是粮饷，兄劫财。
>
> **Note:** 'Xiang' is military pay, and 'kouliang' is rations and soldiers' grain. 'Haosui' is the baleful drain that wastes money and grain. 'Kouchu' is deducting and skimming. This entry takes the same kind of image as 'Wealth thriving, provisions are abundant ... the inner Peer moves to control Wealth' in the Yi Yin war divination (YY.59) and 'Wealth is the root of provisions and Peer is the shape of an ambush' in the Huangjin Ce on campaigns (HJC.88): Wealth is pay and grain, and Peer robs Wealth.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.128、LY.CAREER.EL.130、LY.CAREER.EL.33、LY.CAREER.YY.59、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.130 投充兵卒：官爻持世无冲克必作先锋佐帝皇；官爻最宜持世又宜安静旺相则有奇能美爵，若值冲克必有奸佞窃夺功权之祸

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官爻持世、没有冲克，必定做先锋，辅佐帝王。官爻最适宜持世，又适宜安静旺相，这样就有奇特的才能和美好的爵位；如果遇到冲克，必有奸佞之人窃夺功劳权柄的祸患。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line holds the Self with no clash or control, one will surely be a vanguard and assist the sovereign. The Pressure line is best holding the Self, and also best quiet and thriving, and then one has rare ability and a fine rank; if it meets clash or control, there will surely be the misfortune of treacherous men stealing one's merit and power.

**《易林补遗·投充兵卒章第一百三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 2527 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 139: Enlisting as a soldier", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 2527*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻持世无冲克，必作先锋佐帝皇。 | 官爻持世、没有冲克，必定做先锋，辅佐帝王。 | If the Pressure line holds the Self with no clash or control, one will surely be a vanguard and assist the sovereign. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 2528 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 2528*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻最宜持世，又宜安静旺相，则有奇能美爵。 | 官爻最适宜持世，又适宜安静旺相，就有奇特的才能和美好的爵位。 | The Pressure line is best holding the Self, and also best quiet and thriving, and then one has rare ability and a fine rank. |
| 若值冲克，必有奸佞窃夺功权之祸。 | 如果遇到冲克，必有奸佞窃夺功劳权柄的祸患。 | If it meets clash or control, there will surely be the misfortune of treacherous men stealing one's merit and power. |

> **按**:这一条说官爻持世是吉：投军这件事里，官鬼既是随军出战的大忌（EL.125、EL.126），又是持世作先锋、有爵位的吉象。两处不冲突：忌的是官鬼冲克世、随官入墓、助鬼伤身，喜的是官爻持世、安静旺相、不受冲克。这是投充兵卒一章的收尾。
>
> **Note:** This entry says that the Pressure line holding the Self is auspicious: in joining the army, Pressure is both the great taboo of going out to battle (EL.125, EL.126) and the auspicious image of holding the Self as a vanguard and winning rank. The two do not clash: what is feared is Pressure clashing with or controlling the Self, following the star of office into the tomb, and helping Pressure to injure the Body; what is welcomed is the Pressure line holding the Self, quiet and thriving, and not clashed or controlled. This closes the chapter on enlisting as a soldier.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.129、LY.CAREER.EL.125、LY.CAREER.EL.126

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.24 仕宦·出征剿捕，福德兴而寇贼歼亡（旧注：将帅平居卜问不宜子孙发动主降调贬责、不宜应动克世；岁君月建冲动官鬼或世爻有敕命征讨；临敌卜问喜子孙发动必成剿捕之功；官鬼当贼寇论，世克应亦吉）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:担任将帅或征讨之官的人，平时占问，不适宜子孙发动，主有降职调任、贬责，也不适宜应爻发动克世爻，主有意想不到的变故；太岁、月建冲动官鬼或世爻，主有敕命征讨的事。如果临阵对敌占问，就喜欢子孙发动，必成剿捕的功绩，再得到太岁、月建生合，还有升赏；这时官鬼不当作爵位，要当作贼寇来论，世克应也吉。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For one holding the post of commander or an officer of punitive expeditions, in divining in ordinary times it is not fitting that Output moves, which means demotion, transfer and rebuke, nor that the Other moves and controls the Self, which means an unforeseen change; if the Tai Sui or the Month clashes Pressure or the Self into motion, there will be an imperial order for a punitive expedition. If the divination is made facing the enemy, it is welcome that Output moves, and one will surely achieve merit in suppressing the bandits, and if the Tai Sui and the Month also generate or combine, there will be promotion and reward as well; Pressure is then not taken as rank but as the bandits, and the Self controlling the Other is also auspicious.

**《黄金策·仕宦》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7736 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7736*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 出征剿捕，福德兴而寇贼歼亡； | 出兵征讨、剿灭盗寇，福德（子孙）兴旺，寇贼就被歼灭； | In going out to campaign and wipe out bandits, when Output flourishes the robbers are destroyed; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7737–7740 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 7737–7740*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡任将帅之职，或征讨之官，平居卜问，不宜子孙发动，主有降调贬责； | 凡是担任将帅的职位，或者征讨的官，平时占问，不适宜子孙发动，主有降职调任、贬责； | For one holding the post of commander or an officer of punitive expeditions, in divining in ordinary times it is not fitting that Output moves, which means demotion, transfer and rebuke; |
| 亦不宜应动克世，主有不测变故。 | 也不适宜应爻发动克世爻，主有意想不到的变故。 | nor is it fitting that the Other moves and controls the Self, which means an unforeseen change. |
| 若岁君月建冲动官鬼，或世爻，主有敕命征讨之事。 | 如果太岁、月建冲动官鬼或世爻，主有敕命征讨的事。 | If the Tai Sui or the Month clashes Pressure or the Self into motion, there will be an imperial order for a punitive expedition. |
| 如临敌卜问，则喜子孙发动，必成剿捕之功，更得岁君月建生合，仍有升赏。 | 如果临阵对敌占问，就喜欢子孙发动，必成剿捕的功绩，再得到太岁、月建生合，还有升赏。 | If the divination is made facing the enemy, it is welcome that Output moves, and one will surely achieve merit in suppressing the bandits, and if the Tai Sui and the Month also generate or combine, there will be promotion and reward as well. |
| 官鬼不作爵位，当作贼寇论之。 | 官鬼这时不当作爵位，要当作贼寇来论。 | Pressure is then not taken as rank but as the bandits. |
| 世克应亦吉。 | 世克应也吉。 | The Self controlling the Other is also auspicious. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1974 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1974*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡任将帅之职，或征讨之官，平居卜问，不宜子孙发动，主有降调； | 凡是担任将帅的职位，或者征讨的官，平时占问，不适宜子孙发动，主有降职调任； | For one holding the post of commander or an officer of punitive expeditions, in divining in ordinary times it is not fitting that Output moves, which means demotion and transfer; |
| 如临事问，则喜子孙发动，必成剿捕大功； | 如果临事占问，就喜欢子孙发动，必成剿捕的大功； | if the divination is made at the time of the event, it is welcome that Output moves, and one will surely achieve great merit in suppressing the bandits; |
| 更得岁君月建生合世爻，主有升赏。 | 再得到太岁、月建生合世爻，主有升赏。 | and if the Tai Sui and the Month also generate or combine with the Self, there will be promotion and reward. |
| 官鬼不作爵位，当作寇贼论。 | 官鬼不当作爵位，要当作寇贼来论。 | Pressure is not taken as rank but as the bandits. |
| 世克应亦吉。 | 世克应也吉。 | The Self controlling the Other is also auspicious. |

> **按**:这一联要分两种问法：平时问（还没有开战，问自己的仕途）和临阵问（敌人当前，问这一仗）。平时问，子孙是「伤官之杀」，发动就降调贬责；临阵问，子孙是「克鬼」的神（官鬼这时是贼寇），发动就能灭贼。所以同一个子孙发动，平时凶、临阵吉，这和 LY.CAREER.HJC.03（子孙发动剥官削职）不矛盾，是用神换了：官鬼不再是你的官，而是你的敌人。「敕命征讨」是皇帝下令出兵，「剿捕」是围剿捕捉。
>
> **Note:** This couplet must be read for two kinds of question: in ordinary times (no war has begun, and one asks about one's own career) and facing the enemy (the enemy is before one, and one asks about this battle). In ordinary times Output is 'the killer that harms office', and moving brings demotion and rebuke; facing the enemy Output is the spirit that controls Pressure (which is then the bandits), and moving can wipe them out. So the same moving Output is ominous in ordinary times and auspicious facing the enemy, which does not contradict LY.CAREER.HJC.03 (Output moving strips the office): the Subject has changed, and Pressure is no longer your office but your enemy. 'An imperial order for a punitive expedition' is the emperor ordering out the army, and 'suppress' is surrounding and seizing.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1974 行:出征剿捕，福德兴而寇贼歼亡
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1791 行:出征剿捕，福德兴而寇贼歼亡

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.03、LY.CAREER.HJC.25

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.25 仕宦·镇守边陲，卦爻静而华夷安泰（旧注：镇守不拘文武皆宜六爻安静，世应生合比和日辰月建不冲克则安然无警，世应空亡亦吉；官鬼发动世应冲克必多侵扰）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:镇守一方，不论文官武职，都适宜六爻安静；世应生合比和，日辰月建不互相冲克，就能安睡在边庭，安然没有警报，世应空亡也吉；如果遇到官鬼发动、世应冲克，必定多有侵扰，要通变着断。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In guarding a region, whether in a civil or military post, it is fitting that the six lines are still; if the Self and the Other generate, combine or are in harmony and the Day and the Month do not clash or control each other, one sleeps soundly at the border, at ease and without alarm, and the Self and the Other Void is also auspicious; if Pressure moves and the Self and the Other clash or control each other, there will surely be many incursions, and one should judge flexibly.

**《黄金策·仕宦》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 7742 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7742*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 镇守边陲，卦爻静而华夷安泰。 | 镇守边疆，卦爻安静，华夏和外族就安稳太平。 | In guarding the frontier, when the hexagram and its lines are still, the Chinese and the border peoples are at peace. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 7743 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 7743*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 镇守地方，不拘文官武职，皆宜六爻安静； | 镇守地方，不论文官武职，都适宜六爻安静； | In guarding a region, whether in a civil or military post, it is fitting that the six lines are still; |
| 世应生合比和，日辰月建，不相冲克，则鼾睡边庭，安然无警，世应空亡亦吉。 | 世应生合比和，日辰、月建不互相冲克，就能安睡在边庭，安然没有警报，世应空亡也吉。 | if the Self and the Other generate, combine or are in harmony, and the Day and the Month do not clash or control each other, one sleeps soundly at the border, at ease and without alarm, and the Self and the Other Void is also auspicious. |
| 若遇官鬼发动，世应冲克，必多侵扰，宜通变断之。 | 如果遇到官鬼发动，世应冲克，必定多有侵扰，要通变着断。 | If Pressure moves and the Self and the Other clash or control each other, there will surely be many incursions, and one should judge flexibly. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1976 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1976*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 镇守地方，不拘文武官职，皆宜六爻安静，日辰月建不相冲克，则安然无惊； | 镇守地方，不论文武官职，都适宜六爻安静，日辰、月建不互相冲克，就安然没有惊扰； | In guarding a region, whether in a civil or military post, it is fitting that the six lines are still and the Day and the Month do not clash or control each other, and then one is at ease and without alarm; |
| 若遇官鬼发动，世应冲克，必多骚扰。 | 如果遇到官鬼发动，世应冲克，必定多有骚扰。 | if Pressure moves and the Self and the Other clash or control each other, there will surely be many disturbances. |
| 用宜通变推之。 | 用的时候要通变着推。 | In use it should be inferred flexibly. |

> **按**:「华夷」是中原和边地的民族，「安泰」是平安。「鼾睡边庭」是说在边疆睡得安稳，不用戒备。「世应空亡亦吉」只有旧注写了：世应都空，双方都不动，边境无事。这一联讲的是「守」，和上一联 LY.CAREER.HJC.24 的「攻」（出征剿捕）正好相反：守要静，攻要动。旧注结尾「宜通变断之」、王洪绪「用宜通变推之」，都是提醒不要死守这条。
>
> **Note:** 'The Chinese and the border peoples' are the peoples of the heartland and of the border lands, and 'at peace' is peace and safety. 'Sleeps soundly at the border' says one sleeps easily on the frontier without having to be on guard. 'The Self and the Other Void is also auspicious' is only in the old commentary: when both the Self and the Other are Void, neither side moves and there is nothing on the border. This couplet speaks of 'defending', the very opposite of the preceding couplet, LY.CAREER.HJC.24, on 'attacking' (campaigning and suppressing bandits): defending wants stillness, and attacking wants movement. The old commentary's closing 'judge it flexibly' and Wang Hongxu's 'in use it should be inferred flexibly' both warn against holding to this rule rigidly.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1976 行:镇守边陲，卦爻静而华夷安泰
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 1794 行:镇守边陲，卦爻静而华夷安泰

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.24

## LY.CAREER.HJC.86 征战·观世应之旺衰，以决两家之胜负；将福官之强弱，以分彼我之军师（旧注：世为我、应为彼，世旺克应则胜、应旺克世则负；子孙为我之将军、官鬼为彼之敌帅）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占征战，世爻是我方，应爻是对方：世爻旺相、克住应爻就胜，应爻旺相、克住世爻就败。子孙是我方的将军，官鬼是对方的主帅，两边军师的强弱，就从这两个爻上分。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In a question about a campaign the Self is our side and the Other is the enemy: if the Self is thriving or strong and controls the Other we win, and if the Other is thriving and controls the Self we lose. Output is our general and Pressure is the enemy commander, and the strength of the two sides' leaders is told apart from these two lines.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5899–5900 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5899–5900*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 医不执方，兵不执法，堪推大将之才能； | 医生不拘泥于一个药方，用兵也不拘泥于一种战法，这样才称得上大将的才能； | A physician does not hold to one prescription and a soldier does not hold to one method, and this is what is meant by a great general's talent; |
| 谋事在人，成事在天，当究先师之妙论。 | 谋划在人，成败在天，要钻研前辈高人的精妙议论。 | planning lies with men and success with heaven, so one should study the fine words of the masters before us. |

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5902 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5902*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 观世应之旺衰，以决两家之胜负； | 看世、应的旺衰，来判定两家的胜负； | Look at the strength and weakness of the Self and the Other to decide the victory or defeat of the two houses; |
| 将福官之强弱，以分彼我之军师。 | 看福神、官鬼的强弱，来分辨我方和对方的军师。 | look at the strength of Output and Pressure to tell apart the leaders of our side and theirs. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5904 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5904*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世为我，应为彼。 | 世是我方，应是对方。 | The Self is us and the Other is them. |
| 世旺克应则胜，应旺克世则负。 | 世旺相、克住应就胜，应旺相、克住世就败。 | If the Self is thriving and controls the Other we win; if the Other is thriving and controls the Self we lose. |
| 子孙为我之将军，官鬼为彼之敌帅。 | 子孙是我方的将军，官鬼是对方的敌帅。 | Output is our general and Pressure is the enemy's commander. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1512 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1512*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世为我，应为彼，世旺克应则胜，应旺克世则负。 | 世是我方，应是对方，世旺相克应就胜，应旺相克世就败。 | The Self is us and the Other is them; if the Self is thriving and controls the Other we win, and if the Other is thriving and controls the Self we lose. |
| 子为我之将，鬼彼之师。 | 子孙是我方的将，官鬼是对方的军队主帅。 | Output is our general and Pressure is the enemy's commander. |

> **按**:卜筮正宗收的是同一段，只有末一句「鬼彼之师」比旧注的「官鬼为彼之敌帅」简略。
>
> **Note:** The Bu Shi Zheng Zong gives the same passage; only its last phrase, 'Pressure is their army', is shorter than the old commentary's 'Pressure is the enemy's commander'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.87) 第 1512 行:医不执方兵不执法，堪称大将才能;异文(相似 0.87) 第 1512 行:谋事在人成事在天，当究得先师妙论
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 375 行:医不执方，兵不执法，堪推大将之才能;同文 第 375 行:谋事在人，成事在天，当究先师之妙论
- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1512 行:观世应之旺衰，以决两家之胜负;同文 第 1512 行:将福官之强弱，以分彼我之军师
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 375 行:观世应之旺衰，以决两家之胜负;同文 第 375 行:将福官之强弱，以分彼我之军师

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.87、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

## LY.CAREER.HJC.87 征战·父母兴隆，立望旌旗之蔽野；金爻空动，侧听金鼓之喧天（旧注：父母为旌旗，旺动主兵起；金空动则闻金鼓声，金空则响故也）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父母爻是旌旗。父母旺相发动，主兵马起动，旌旗遮满原野；金爻落空而动，就听到金鼓声喧闹震天，因为金落空了才会响。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Resource line is the banners. If Resource is thriving and moves, an army is rising and banners cover the plain; if a Metal line is Void and moves, one hears the din of gongs and drums shaking the sky, since Metal sounds when it is void.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5906 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5906*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母兴隆，立望旌旗之蔽野； | 父母爻兴旺，站着就能望见旌旗遮满原野； | If Resource is thriving, one standing and looking sees banners covering the plain; |
| 金爻空动，侧听金鼓之喧天。 | 金爻落空而发动，侧耳就能听到锣鼓声喧闹震天。 | if a Metal line is Void and moves, listening aside one hears the din of gongs and drums shaking the sky. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5907 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5907*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母为旌旗。 | 父母是旌旗。 | Resource is the banners. |
| 旺动主兵起。 | 旺相发动，主兵马起动。 | If it is thriving and moves, it means an army rises. |
| 金空动，则闻金鼓声。 | 金落空而发动，就能听到金鼓之声。 | If Metal is Void and moves, one hears the sound of gongs and drums. |
| 金空则响，故也。 | 金落空就会响，所以是这样。 | Metal sounds when it is void, which is why. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1514 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1514*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母为旌旗； | 父母是旌旗； | Resource is the banners; |
| 金动则闻金鼓声，金空则响故也。 | 金爻发动就能听到金鼓之声，因为金落空了才会响。 | if Metal moves one hears the sound of gongs and drums, since Metal sounds when it is void. |

> **按**:旧注在「父母兴持」一联末尾说「以上数节，出《天玄赋》，不细解」，所以这一组几联的注都很简短。
>
> **Note:** At the end of the couplet 'Resource holds the Self' the old commentary says 'the sections above come from the Tianxuan Fu and are not explained in detail', so the notes on this group of couplets are brief.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.91) 第 1514 行:父母兴隆，主望旌旗之蔽野;同文 第 1514 行:金爻空动，侧听金鼓之喧天
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 378 行:父母兴隆，立望旌旗之蔽野;同文 第 378 行:金爻空动，侧听金鼓之喧天

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.86、LY.CAREER.HJC.88

## LY.CAREER.HJC.88 征战·财为粮草之本根，兄乃伏兵之形势（旧注：财为粮草，旺多衰少、空为无粮；兄为伏兵又为夺粮之神，不宜旺动）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:妻财是粮草：旺相的粮草多，衰弱的粮草少，落空就是没有粮；兄弟是伏兵，也是抢夺粮草的神，不宜旺相发动。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Wealth is grain and fodder: thriving means plenty, weak means little, and Void means none; Peer is the ambush, and also the spirit that seizes the grain, and should not be thriving and moving.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5909 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5909*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财为粮草之本根，兄乃伏兵之形势。 | 财是粮草的根本，兄是伏兵的形势。 | Wealth is the root of grain and fodder, and Peer is the shape of an ambush. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5910 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5910*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财为粮草。 | 财是粮草。 | Wealth is grain and fodder. |
| 旺多衰少，空为无粮。 | 旺相的多，衰弱的少，落空就是没有粮。 | If it is thriving there is plenty, if it is weak there is little, and if it is Void there is no grain. |
| 兄为伏兵，又为夺粮之神，不宜旺动。 | 兄是伏兵，又是夺粮的神，不宜旺相发动。 | Peer is the ambush and also the spirit that seizes the grain, and should not be thriving and moving. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1516 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1516*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财为粮草，旺多衰少，空为无粮； | 财是粮草，旺相的多，衰弱的少，落空就是没有粮； | Wealth is grain and fodder; thriving means plenty, weak means little, and Void means no grain; |
| 兄为伏兵，又为夺粮之神，不宜旺动。 | 兄是伏兵，又是夺粮的神，不宜旺相发动。 | Peer is the ambush and also the spirit that seizes the grain, and should not be thriving and moving. |

> **按**:旧注在「父母兴持」一联末尾说「以上数节，出《天玄赋》，不细解」，所以这一组几联的注都很简短。
>
> **Note:** At the end of the couplet 'Resource holds the Self' the old commentary says 'the sections above come from the Tianxuan Fu and are not explained in detail', so the notes on this group of couplets are brief.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1516 行:财为粮草之本根，兄乃伏兵之形势
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 381 行:财力粮草之本根，兄乃伏兵之形势

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.86、LY.CAREER.HJC.87、LY.CAREER.HJC.89
