## LY.CAREER.HJC.89 征战·木兴扶世，济川宜驾乎轻舟；火旺生身，立寨必安于胜地（旧注：木为舟楫，动来生扶世身，或水爻与子孙动，宜乘舟决战；火为营寨，旺动生扶世身，结寨必得形胜之地）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:木是舟船：木爻发动来生扶世爻，或者水爻和子孙发动，适合乘船决战取胜；火是营寨：火爻旺相发动来生扶世身，扎寨必定得到地形优越的好地方。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Wood is boats: if a Wood line moves to generate or support the Self, or a Water line moves together with Output, it is fit to fight a decisive battle in boats and win; Fire is the camp: if a Fire line is thriving and moves to generate or support the Self, the stockade will surely stand in a place of natural advantage.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5912 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5912*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 木兴扶世，济川宜驾乎轻舟； | 木爻兴旺来扶助世爻，渡河作战适合驾着轻快的小船； | If Wood is thriving and supports the Self, to cross the river it is fit to sail in light boats; |
| 火旺生身，立寨必安于胜地。 | 火爻旺相来生助世身，扎营立寨必定安稳地处在形势优越的地方。 | if Fire is thriving and generates the Self or Body, the camp set up will surely rest in a place of advantage. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5913 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5913*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 木为舟揖。 | 木是舟船和桨。 | Wood is boats and oars. |
| 若动来生扶世身，或水爻与子孙动，宜乘舟决战以取胜； | 如果它发动来生扶世身，或者水爻和子孙一起发动，适合乘船决战来取胜； | If it moves to generate or support the Self, or a Water line moves together with Output, it is fit to fight a decisive battle in boats to win; |
| 火为营寨。 | 火是营寨。 | Fire is the camp. |
| 若旺动生扶世身，结寨必得形胜之地也。 | 如果它旺相发动来生扶世身，扎寨必定得到地形优越的好地方。 | If it is thriving and moves to generate or support the Self, the stockade will surely stand in a place of natural advantage. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1518 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1518*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水若动来生扶世身，或水爻子孙动，宜乘舟决战以取胜； | 水爻如果发动来生扶世身，或者水爻、子孙发动，适合乘船决战来取胜； | If Water moves to generate or support the Self, or a Water line and Output move, it is fit to fight a decisive battle in boats to win; |
| 火若旺动生扶世身，结寨必得形胜之地也。 | 火爻如果旺相发动来生扶世身，扎寨必定得到地形优越的好地方。 | if Fire is thriving and moves to generate or support the Self, the stockade will surely stand in a place of natural advantage. |

> **打架**:舟是木还是水，两本不一样：卜筮大全的经文是「木兴扶世」，旧注先说「木为舟楫」、又说「水爻与子孙动」；卜筮正宗正文写成「水兴扶世」，注里只说水。
>
> **Where they disagree:** The two books differ on whether the boat is Wood or Water: the verse in the Bu Shi Da Quan reads 'Wood thriving supports the Self', and the old commentary says first 'Wood is boats and oars' and then 'a Water line and Output move'; the main text of the Bu Shi Zheng Zong reads 'Water thriving supports the Self', and its note speaks only of Water.

> **按**:旧注在「父母兴持」一联末尾说「以上数节，出《天玄赋》，不细解」，所以这一组几联的注都很简短。
>
> **Note:** At the end of the couplet 'Resource holds the Self' the old commentary says 'the sections above come from the Tianxuan Fu and are not explained in detail', so the notes on this group of couplets are brief.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.91) 第 1518 行:水兴扶世，济川宜驾乎轻舟;同文 第 1518 行:火旺生身，立寨必安于胜地
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 384 行:木兴扶世，济川宜驾乎轻舟;同文 第 384 行:火旺生身，立寨必安于胜地

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.88、LY.CAREER.HJC.90

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.90 征战·父母兴持，主帅无宽仁之德；子孙得地，将军有决胜之才（旧注：父母持世动，主帅不恤士卒、上下离心，带兄弟凶神须防自变；子孙持世身旺动，将军必决胜千里外）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父母爻持世发动，主帅不体恤士兵，上下离心，如果又带着兄弟或凶神，要防备内部自己生变；子孙持世、旺相发动，将军一定能在千里之外决定胜负。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Resource holds the Self and moves, the commander has no kindness for his soldiers and high and low are of different minds, and if it also carries Peer or a baleful spirit one must guard against a change from within; if Output holds the Self and is thriving and moves, the general will surely decide the victory from a thousand li away.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5915 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5915*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母兴持，主帅无宽仁之德； | 父母爻兴旺而持世，主帅没有宽厚仁爱的德行； | If Resource is thriving and holds the Self, the commander lacks the virtue of leniency and kindness; |
| 子孙得地，将军有决胜之才。 | 子孙得地，将军就有决胜的才能。 | if Output gains its ground, the general has the talent to decide the battle. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5916–5917 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), lines 5916–5917*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母持世动，乃主帅不恤士卒，上下离心。 | 父母持世并且发动，是主帅不体恤士兵，上下离心。 | If Resource holds the Self and moves, the commander does not care for his soldiers and high and low are of different minds. |
| 若带兄弟凶神，须防自变。 | 如果又带着兄弟、凶神，要防备内部自己生变。 | If it also carries Peer or a baleful spirit, one must guard against a change from within. |
| 子孙持世身旺动，将军必决胜千里外。 | 子孙持世、世身旺相发动，将军一定能在千里之外决定胜负。 | If Output holds the Self and the Self is thriving and moves, the general will surely decide the victory from a thousand li away. |
| 以上数节，出《天玄赋》，不细解。 | 以上几节出自《天玄赋》，这里不详细解释。 | The sections above come from the Tianxuan Fu and are not explained in detail here. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1520 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1520*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母持世动，乃主帅不恤士卒，上下离心，若带兄弟官鬼，须防自变； | 父母持世发动，是主帅不体恤士兵，上下离心，如果又带着兄弟、官鬼，要防备内部自己生变； | If Resource holds the Self and moves, the commander does not care for his soldiers and high and low are of different minds, and if it also carries Peer or Pressure, one must guard against a change from within; |
| 若子孙持世旺动，将军必决胜千里。 | 如果子孙持世、旺相发动，将军一定能在千里之外决定胜负。 | if Output holds the Self and is thriving and moves, the general will surely decide the victory from a thousand li away. |

> **按**:旧注说「带兄弟凶神」，卜筮正宗说「带兄弟官鬼」。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary says 'carrying Peer or a baleful spirit'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says 'carrying Peer or Pressure'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1520 行:父母兴持，主帅无宽仁之德;同文 第 1520 行:子孙得地，将军有决胜之才
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 387 行:父母兴持，主帅无宽仁之德;同文 第 387 行:子孙得地，将军有决胜之才

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.89、LY.CAREER.HJC.91

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.91 征战·水爻克子子孙强，韩信背水阵而陈余被斩（旧注：卦中水爻动、世持水爻、或水爻动克伤子孙，若子孙旺相得日月生扶或子孙旺动，可效韩信背水战，死中求生反胜）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦中水爻发动，或者世爻持着水爻，或者水爻发动克伤子孙，只要子孙旺相、得到月建日辰生扶，或者子孙旺相发动，就可以效法韩信背水列阵，在绝境里求生，反而取胜。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a Water line moves in the hexagram, or the Self holds a Water line, or a Water line moves and controls and injures Output, then so long as Output is thriving or strong and is generated and supported by the Month and Day, or Output is thriving and moves, one may follow Han Xin's battle with the river at his back, seeking life in the midst of death, and win instead.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5919 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5919*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水爻克子子孙强，韩信背水阵而陈余被斩； | 水爻克子孙而子孙强，就像韩信摆背水之阵、陈余被斩； | If Water controls Output and Output is strong, it is like Han Xin drawing up his army with the river at his back and Chen Yu being cut down; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5920 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5920*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦中水爻动，及世持水爻，或动出水爻克伤子孙，若子孙旺相，得日辰月建生扶，或子孙旺动，则可效韩信背水战，死中求生而反胜也。 | 卦中水爻发动，以及世爻持着水爻，或者发动变出水爻来克伤子孙，如果子孙旺相，得到日辰月建生扶，或者子孙旺相发动，就可以效法韩信背水一战，在死地里求生而反过来取胜。 | If a Water line moves in the hexagram, or the Self holds a Water line, or a moving line turns into a Water line that controls and injures Output, then if Output is thriving or strong and is generated and supported by the Day and Month, or Output is thriving and moves, one may follow Han Xin's battle with the river at his back, seeking life in the midst of death, and win instead. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1522 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1522*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持水动或水爻克子孙，若子孙亦动，得日月生扶，可效韩信背水战而反胜也。 | 世爻持着水爻发动，或者水爻克子孙，如果子孙也发动、得到月建日辰生扶，可以效法韩信背水一战而反过来取胜。 | If the Self holds a Water line that moves, or a Water line controls Output, and Output also moves and is generated and supported by the Month and Day, one may follow Han Xin's battle with the river at his back and win instead. |

> **按**:旧注列了水爻动、世持水爻、动出水爻克子孙三种情形，并要子孙旺相或旺动；卜筮正宗压缩成两种，要「子孙亦动」。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary lists three cases: a Water line moving, the Self holding Water, and a moving line turning into Water that controls Output, with Output thriving or thriving and moving; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong compresses them into two and requires 'Output also moving'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1522 行:水爻克子子孙强，韩信背水阵而陈余被斩
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 390 行:水爻克子子孙强，韩信背水阵而陈余被斩

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.90、LY.CAREER.HJC.92

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.92 征战·阴象持兄兄克应，李愬雪夜走而元济遭擒（旧注：兄为伏兵，内象旺动克应是我伏兵、克世是他伏兵；兄在阳象宜日间伏、在阴象宜夜间伏，如李愬雪夜衔枚直捣蔡城擒吴元济）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:兄弟是伏兵。兄弟在内卦旺相发动、克应爻，是我方设伏；克世爻，是对方设伏。兄弟在阳象适合白天设伏，在阴象适合夜里设伏，就像唐宪宗时李愬雪夜衔枚疾行，直捣蔡州城，擒住了吴元济。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Peer is the ambush. If Peer in the inner trigram is thriving and moves and controls the Other, it is our ambush; if it controls the Self, it is the enemy's. If Peer is in a yang image the ambush is fit for the day and in a yin image for the night, like Li Su in the reign of Emperor Xianzong of Tang, who marched through a snowy night with his men holding sticks in their mouths for silence, struck straight at the city of Cai and captured Wu Yuanji.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5922 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5922*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 阴象持兄兄克应，李愬雪夜走而元济遭擒。 | 兄弟在阴象上、克制应爻，就像李愬雪夜行军而吴元济被擒。 | If Peer sits in a yin image and controls the Other, it is like Li Su's march on a snowy night and the capture of Yuanji. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5923 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5923*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄为伏兵。 | 兄弟是伏兵。 | Peer is the ambush. |
| 如在内象旺动，克应，乃我伏兵； | 如果它在内卦旺相发动、克应爻，就是我方的伏兵； | If it is in the inner trigram, thriving and moving, and controls the Other, it is our ambush; |
| 克世，是他伏兵。 | 克世爻，就是对方的伏兵。 | if it controls the Self, it is the enemy's ambush. |
| 若兄在阳象，宜日间伏； | 如果兄弟在阳象，适合白天设伏； | If Peer is in a yang image, the ambush is fit for the day; |
| 兄在阴象，宜夜间伏。 | 兄弟在阴象，适合夜里设伏。 | if Peer is in a yin image, the ambush is fit for the night. |
| 如唐宪宗朝，李朔雪夜衔枚走，直捣蔡城，以擒吴元济也。 | 就像唐宪宗时，李愬（这里原文作「李朔」）雪夜衔枚疾行，直捣蔡州城，擒住吴元济。 | It is like Li Su (written Li Shuo here) in the reign of Emperor Xianzong of Tang, who marched through a snowy night with his men holding sticks in their mouths for silence, struck straight at the city of Cai and captured Wu Yuanji. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1524 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1524*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄为伏兵，在内象动克应爻，乃我之伏兵也； | 兄弟是伏兵，在内卦发动、克应爻，是我方的伏兵； | Peer is the ambush; if it moves in the inner trigram and controls the Other, it is our ambush; |
| 克世是他人之伏兵。 | 克世爻的是对方的伏兵。 | if it controls the Self, it is the enemy's ambush. |
| 若在阳象宜日间伏，在阴象宜夜间伏，如唐宪宗朝李愬雪夜衔枚，直捣蔡城以擒吴元济也。 | 如果在阳象适合白天设伏，在阴象适合夜里设伏，就像唐宪宗时李愬雪夜衔枚疾行，直捣蔡州城，擒住吴元济。 | If it is in a yang image the ambush is fit for the day and in a yin image for the night, like Li Su in the reign of Emperor Xianzong of Tang, who marched through a snowy night with his men holding sticks in their mouths for silence, struck straight at the city of Cai and captured Wu Yuanji. |

> **按**:旧注把李愬写成「李朔」，卜筮正宗写对了。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary writes Li Su as 'Li Shuo', and the Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes it correctly.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1524 行:阴象持兄兄克应，李愬雪夜走而元济遭擒
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.94) 第 393 行:阴象持兄兄克应，李朔雪夜走而元济遭擒

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.88、LY.CAREER.HJC.91、LY.CAREER.HJC.93

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.93 征战·世持子而被伤，可效周亚夫坚壁不战（旧注：世持子孙则将有才能可克敌，若父动克则宜固守避其锋、不利速战；如汉景帝时七国反，周亚夫屯细柳，中夜军惊坚卧不起，深沟高垒数日乃定，遂破七国之兵）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻持着子孙，将领有才能，可以克敌；但如果被父母发动来克，就应当固守营垒、避开敌人的锋芒，不利于速战，就像汉景帝时七国造反，周亚夫驻军细柳，半夜军营受惊他也卧着不起，深沟高垒，几天后营里安定下来，终于击破了七国的军队。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self holds Output, the general has the ability to defeat the enemy; but if Resource moves to control it, one should hold the fort and avoid the enemy's edge, and a quick battle is not favourable, as when the seven states rebelled under Emperor Jing of Han and Zhou Yafu camped at Xiliu and lay still when the camp was alarmed at midnight, kept his ditches deep and his ramparts high, and after some days the camp settled and in the end he broke the armies of the seven states.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5925 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5925*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持子而被伤，可效周亚夫坚壁不战； | 世爻持着子孙却被克伤，可以效法周亚夫坚守壁垒、不出战； | If the Self holds Output but is injured, one may follow Zhou Yafu, who held his ramparts and would not fight; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5926 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5926*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持子孙，将必才能，可以克敌。 | 世爻持着子孙，将领必定有才能，可以克敌。 | If the Self holds Output, the general surely has ability and can defeat the enemy. |
| 若父动克，宜且固守，以避其锋，不利速战。 | 如果父爻发动来克，应当暂且固守，避开敌人的锋芒，不利于速战。 | If Resource moves to control it, one should for the time being hold firm to avoid the enemy's edge, and a quick battle is not favourable. |
| 如汉景帝朝，七国反，帝使周亚夫，屯细柳以攻之。 | 就像汉景帝时，七国造反，景帝派周亚夫驻军细柳去攻打他们。 | It is like the reign of Emperor Jing of Han, when the seven states rebelled and the emperor sent Zhou Yafu to camp at Xiliu and attack them. |
| 中夜军惊，扰乱至帐下，亚夫坚卧不起，深沟高垒，数日乃定，遂破七国之兵。 | 半夜军营受惊，骚乱一直闹到他的帐下，周亚夫仍然稳卧不起，深挖壕沟、高筑壁垒，几天后军营才安定下来，终于击破了七国的军队。 | At midnight the army was alarmed and the disorder reached his tent, but Yafu lay firmly and did not rise, kept his ditches deep and his ramparts high, and after some days the camp settled, and in the end he broke the armies of the seven states. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1526 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1526*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持子孙，将必才能，可以克敌。 | 世爻持着子孙，将领必定有才能，可以克敌。 | If the Self holds Output, the general surely has ability and can defeat the enemy. |
| 若被动克宜固守，不宜速战，如汉景帝时七国反，帝使周亚夫屯细柳以攻之，中夜军惊，扰乱至帐下，亚夫坚卧不起，深沟高垒，数日乃定，遂破七国之兵。 | 如果被动爻克，应当固守，不宜速战，就像汉景帝时七国造反，景帝派周亚夫驻军细柳去攻打他们，半夜军营受惊，骚乱一直闹到他的帐下，周亚夫稳卧不起，深挖壕沟、高筑壁垒，几天后军营才安定下来，终于击破了七国的军队。 | If it is controlled by a moving line one should hold firm and not fight a quick battle, as when the seven states rebelled under Emperor Jing of Han and the emperor sent Zhou Yafu to camp at Xiliu and attack them: at midnight the army was alarmed and the disorder reached his tent, but Yafu lay firmly and did not rise, kept his ditches deep and his ramparts high, and after some days the camp settled, and in the end he broke the armies of the seven states. |

> **按**:旧注说克它的是「父动克」，卜筮正宗说「被动克」，没有指明是父爻。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary says the controller is 'Resource moving to control'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says only 'controlled by a moving line' and does not name Resource.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 1526 行:世持子而彼伤，可效周亚夫坚壁不战
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 396 行:世持子而被伤，可效周亚夫坚壁不战

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.92、LY.CAREER.HJC.94

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.94 征战·应临官而遭克，当如司马懿固垒休兵（旧注：应持官鬼旺相则彼将才能、我难与敌，卦有子孙发动终不能大胜；如司马懿自料不如孔明，甘受巾帼之辱，坚垒不战）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:应爻持着旺相的官鬼，对方的将领有才能，我方难以对敌；就算卦中有子孙发动，最终也不能大胜，应当像司马懿那样，自知才能不如诸葛孔明，甘愿受送来妇人头巾的羞辱，坚守营垒不出战。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Other line holds Pressure that is thriving or strong, the enemy general is able and it is hard for us to face him, and even if Output in the hexagram moves, in the end there will be no great victory; one should do as Sima Yi did, who judged that his ability did not match Kongming's, swallowed the insult of the women's head-cloths, and held his ramparts without fighting.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5928 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5928*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应临官而遭克，当如司马懿固垒休兵。 | 应爻临着官鬼却被克，应当像司马懿那样巩固营垒、停止出兵。 | If the Other sits on Pressure and is controlled, one should do as Sima Yi did, strengthening the ramparts and resting the troops. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5929 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5929*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应持官旺相，彼将才能，我难与敌。 | 应爻持着官鬼而且旺相，对方的将领有才能，我方难以与他对敌。 | If the Other holds Pressure that is thriving or strong, the enemy general is able and it is hard for us to face him. |
| 虽卦有子孙发动，终不能大胜。 | 即使卦里有子孙发动，最终也不能大胜。 | Even if Output moves in the hexagram, in the end there will be no great victory. |
| 如三国时，司马懿自料才能不如孔明，甘受巾帼之辱，坚垒不战尚矣。 | 就像三国时，司马懿自己估量才能不如诸葛孔明，甘愿受送来妇人头巾的羞辱，坚守营垒不出战，这就最好了。 | It is like Sima Yi in the Three Kingdoms, who judged that his own ability was not equal to Kongming's, accepted the insult of the women's head-cloths, and held his ramparts without fighting, which was best. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1528 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1528*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应持官旺，彼将才能，我难与敌，虽有子孙动不能大胜，如三国时司马懿自料不能如孔明，甘受巾帼，坚壁不战也。 | 应爻持着旺相的官，对方的将领有才能，我方难以对敌，即使有子孙发动也不能大胜，就像三国时司马懿自己估量不能像孔明那样，甘愿受妇人头巾的羞辱，坚守壁垒不出战。 | If the Other holds Pressure that is thriving, the enemy general is able and it is hard for us to face him, and even if Output moves there will be no great victory, as Sima Yi in the Three Kingdoms judged that he could not match Kongming, accepted the insult of the women's head-cloths, and held his ramparts without fighting. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1528 行:应临官而遭克，当如司马懿固垒休兵
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 399 行:应临官而遭克，当如司马懿固垒休兵

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.93、LY.CAREER.HJC.95

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.95 征战·世持衰福得生扶，王翦以六十万众而胜楚（旧注：世身虽持子孙而衰弱难胜敌，若得月建日辰生扶，可效始皇朝王翦以六十万人成胜楚之功）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻虽然持着子孙，但子孙衰弱也难以战胜敌人；如果得到月建、日辰生扶，就可以效法秦始皇时王翦率六十万大军取得灭楚的功劳。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Although the Self holds Output, a weak Output can hardly overcome the enemy; if it gets generation and support from the Month and Day, one may follow Wang Jian in the reign of the First Emperor, who with six hundred thousand men achieved the conquest of Chu.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5931 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5931*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世持衰福得生扶，王翦以六十万众而胜楚； | 世爻持着衰弱的福神却得到生扶，就像王翦率六十万大军战胜楚国； | If the Self holds a weak Output and gets generation and support, it is like Wang Jian who with six hundred thousand men overcame Chu; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5932 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5932*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世身虽持子孙，衰弱亦难胜敌。 | 世身虽然持着子孙，但衰弱也难以战胜敌人。 | Although the Self or Body holds Output, if it is weak it can hardly overcome the enemy. |
| 若得月建日辰生扶，可效始皇朝，王翦以六十万众，卒成胜楚之功。 | 如果得到月建、日辰生扶，可以效法秦始皇时，王翦率六十万大军，终于成就战胜楚国的功劳。 | If it gets generation and support from the Month and Day, one may follow the reign of the First Emperor, when Wang Jian with six hundred thousand men in the end achieved the victory over Chu. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1530 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1530*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 身世虽持子孙，衰弱亦难胜，若得月建日辰生扶，可效始皇时王翦以六十万人而成胜楚之功。 | 世身虽然持着子孙，但衰弱也难以取胜，如果得到月建、日辰生扶，可以效法秦始皇时王翦率六十万人而成就战胜楚国的功劳。 | Although the Self or Body holds Output, if it is weak it can hardly win; if it gets generation and support from the Month and Day, one may follow Wang Jian in the time of the First Emperor, who with six hundred thousand men achieved the victory over Chu. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1530 行:世持衰福得生扶，王翦以六十万众而胜楚
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 402 行:世持衰福得生扶，王翦以六十万众而胜楚

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.94、LY.CAREER.HJC.96

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.96 征战·卦有众官临旺子，谢玄以八千之兵而破秦（旧注：卦有官鬼、父母虽多而安静休囚，子孙虽少而当权旺动，是寡胜众之象，如晋谢玄刘牢之以八千兵渡江破秦王苻坚九十万众）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里官鬼、父母虽然多，却安静休囚；子孙虽然少，却当令旺相而发动，这是以少胜多的象，就像晋朝的谢玄、刘牢之，以八千兵马渡江，击破秦王苻坚的九十万大军。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the hexagram has many Pressure and Resource lines that are still and resting or trapped, while Output is few but holds the season and is thriving and moving, it is the image of the few overcoming the many, like Xie Xuan and Liu Laozhi of Jin, who crossed the river with eight thousand men and broke the nine hundred thousand of Fu Jian, King of Qin.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5934 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5934*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦有众官临旺子，谢玄以八千之兵而破秦。 | 卦里有很多官鬼，而子孙旺相，就像谢玄凭八千兵马击破了秦国。 | If the hexagram has many Pressure lines while Output is thriving, it is like Xie Xuan who with eight thousand soldiers broke Qin. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5935 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5935*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦有官鬼，父母虽多，而安静休囚； | 卦里有官鬼，父母虽然多，却安静休囚； | If the hexagram has Pressure, and Resource lines are many but still and resting or trapped; |
| 子孙虽少，而当权旺动。 | 子孙虽然少，却当令旺相而发动。 | and Output is few but holds the season and is thriving and moving. |
| 此乃寡胜众之象。 | 这就是以少胜多的象。 | This is the image of the few overcoming the many. |
| 如晋谢玄、刘牢之，以八千兵渡江，破秦王符坚九十万众也。 | 就像晋朝的谢玄、刘牢之，以八千兵马渡江，击破了秦王符坚的九十万大军。 | It is like Xie Xuan and Liu Laozhi of Jin, who crossed the river with eight thousand soldiers and broke the nine hundred thousand of Fu Jian, King of Qin. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1532 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1532*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官父虽多而安静，子孙虽少而旺动，必寡可胜众也，如晋谢玄、刘牢以八千兵破秦王苻坚九十万众也。 | 官鬼、父母虽然多却安静，子孙虽然少却旺相发动，必定是以少胜多，就像晋朝的谢玄、刘牢以八千兵马击破秦王苻坚的九十万大军。 | Though Pressure and Resource lines are many, they are still, and Output is few but thriving and moving; the few will surely overcome the many, like Xie Xuan and Liu Lao of Jin who with eight thousand soldiers broke the nine hundred thousand of Fu Jian, King of Qin. |

> **按**:人名写法不同：旧注作「刘牢之」「符坚」，卜筮正宗作「刘牢」「苻坚」；译文按通行写法。
>
> **Note:** The names are written differently: the old commentary has 'Liu Laozhi' and a variant character for 'Fu', the Bu Shi Zheng Zong has 'Liu Lao' and the usual 'Fu'; the translation follows the common forms.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1532 行:卦有众官临旺子，谢玄以八千之兵而破秦
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.94) 第 405 行:卦有众官临旺子，谢玄以八千精兵而破秦

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.95、LY.CAREER.HJC.97

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.97 征战·两子合世扶身，李郭同心而兴唐室（旧注：子孙为将、世为国君，卦有两子旺动生扶世身，主国有二将合谋胜敌，如唐李光弼、郭子仪同心忠义，终能靖乱复兴唐室）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙是将，世爻是国君。卦里有两个子孙旺相发动，生扶世爻或世身，主国家有两位将领合谋战胜敌人，就像唐朝的李光弼、郭子仪，两人同心，以忠义自勉，终于平定叛乱，复兴了唐朝。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output is the general and the Self is the ruler. If the hexagram has two Output lines that are thriving and moving and generate or support the Self, the state has two generals who plot together to overcome the enemy, like Li Guangbi and Guo Ziyi of Tang, who were of one heart, urged themselves on with loyalty and righteousness, and in the end put down the rebellion and restored the house of Tang.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5937 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5937*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 两子合世扶身，李郭同心而兴唐室； | 两个子孙合住世爻、扶助世身，就像李光弼、郭子仪同心协力而复兴唐朝； | If two Output lines combine with the Self and support the Self or Body, it is like Li and Guo working with one heart and restoring the house of Tang; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5938 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5938*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙为将，世为国君。 | 子孙是将，世是国君。 | Output is the general and the Self is the ruler. |
| 若卦中有两子旺动，生扶世身； | 如果卦里有两个子孙旺相发动，生扶世身； | If the hexagram has two Output lines thriving and moving that generate and support the Self or Body; |
| 主国有二将，合谋胜敌之兆。 | 主国家有两位将领合谋战胜敌人的征兆。 | it is a sign that the state has two generals plotting together to overcome the enemy. |
| 如唐李光弼、郭子仪，二人同心，以忠义自励，终能靖乱，复兴唐室。 | 就像唐朝的李光弼、郭子仪，两人同心，以忠义自勉，终于能平定叛乱，复兴唐朝。 | It is like Li Guangbi and Guo Ziyi of Tang, who were of one heart, urged themselves on with loyalty and righteousness, and in the end were able to put down the rebellion and restore the house of Tang. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1534 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1534*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦有两子旺动生世，主有二将合谋胜敌，如唐李弼、郭子仪二人，同心以忠义自厉，终能靖乱复兴唐室。 | 卦里有两个子孙旺相发动、生扶世，主有两位将领合谋战胜敌人，就像唐朝的李光弼（这里作「李弼」）、郭子仪，两人同心，以忠义自勉，终于能平定叛乱、复兴唐朝。 | If the hexagram has two Output lines thriving and moving that generate the Self, there are two generals plotting together to overcome the enemy, like Li Bi (for Li Guangbi) and Guo Ziyi of Tang, who were of one heart, urged themselves on with loyalty and righteousness, and in the end were able to put down the rebellion and restore the house of Tang. |

> **按**:卜筮正宗把「李光弼」写成「李弼」，少了一个字。
>
> **Note:** The Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes 'Li Guangbi' as 'Li Bi', one character short.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1534 行:两子合世扶身，李郭同心而兴唐室
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 408 行:两子合世扶身，李郭同心而兴唐室

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.96、LY.CAREER.HJC.98

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.98 征战·二福刑冲化绝，钟邓互隙而丧身家（旧注：两个子孙生旺变动相冲相刑、两化入死墓绝空，虽胜敌将必争权夺宠互相残害，如晋钟会邓艾平蜀后嫌隙互生，自相屠戮，身家俱丧）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:两个子孙爻生旺发动，互相冲、刑，又都变入死、墓、绝、空，虽然能战胜敌将，但必定争权夺宠、互相残害，就像晋朝的钟会、邓艾带兵平定蜀国，蜀国平定之后彼此生出嫌隙，以致自相残杀，身家都丧。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If two Output lines are thriving and moving, clash with and punish each other, and both turn into death, tomb, extinction or Void, then though they may overcome the enemy's generals they will surely struggle for power and favour and harm each other, like Zhong Hui and Deng Ai of Jin, who led troops to subdue Shu and, when Shu was settled, fell into mutual suspicion, until they killed each other and lost both their persons and their families.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5940 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5940*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 二福刑冲化绝，钟邓互隙而丧身家。 | 两个福神相刑相冲又变成绝爻，就像钟会、邓艾互相结下嫌隙而丧了身家。 | If the two Output lines punish and clash with each other and turn into extinction, it is like Zhong and Deng falling out and losing both their persons and their families. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5941 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5941*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若有二子爻生旺变动，相冲，相刑、两化入死墓绝空，虽胜敌将，必争权夺宠，相残害之兆。 | 如果有两个子孙爻生旺变动，互相冲、互相刑，两个都变入死、墓、绝、空，虽然能战胜敌将，必定争权夺宠，是互相残害的征兆。 | If there are two Output lines that are thriving and changing, clash with and punish each other, and both turn into death, tomb, extinction or Void, then though they may overcome the enemy's generals they will surely struggle for power and favour, a sign that they will harm each other. |
| 如晋钟会、邓艾领兵平蜀，蜀平，而嫌隙互生，乃至自相屠戮、身家俱丧。 | 就像晋朝的钟会、邓艾带兵平定蜀国，蜀国平定之后，彼此生出嫌隙，以致自相屠杀，身家都丧。 | It is like Zhong Hui and Deng Ai of Jin, who led troops to subdue Shu and, when Shu was settled, fell into mutual suspicion, until they slaughtered each other and lost both their persons and their families. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1536 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1536*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 两重子孙旺动皆化入死墓绝空，虽胜敌将，必争权夺宠，两相残害，如晋钟会、邓艾领兵平蜀，蜀平而嫌隙互生，乃至自相屠戮，身家俱丧。 | 两个子孙旺相发动，都变入死、墓、绝、空，虽然能战胜敌将，必定争权夺宠，互相残害，就像晋朝的钟会、邓艾带兵平定蜀国，蜀国平定之后彼此生出嫌隙，以致自相屠杀，身家都丧。 | If two Output lines are thriving and moving and both turn into death, tomb, extinction or Void, then though they may overcome the enemy's generals they will surely struggle for power and favour and harm each other, like Zhong Hui and Deng Ai of Jin, who led troops to subdue Shu and, when Shu was settled, fell into mutual suspicion, until they slaughtered each other and lost both their persons and their families. |

> **按**:书里写「晋钟会、邓艾」；钟会、邓艾平蜀（公元 263 年）时的国家是魏，晋是三年后才建立的，译文照书写作晋朝。旧注还写了两个子孙「相冲相刑」，卜筮正宗没有这一层。
>
> **Note:** The books write 'Zhong Hui and Deng Ai of Jin'; when they subdued Shu (263 CE) the state was Wei and Jin was founded three years later, but the translation follows the books and says Jin. The old commentary also has the two Output lines clashing with and punishing each other; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong has no such clause.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1536 行:二福刑冲化绝，钟邓互隙而丧身家
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 411 行:二福刑冲化绝，锺邓互隙而丧身家

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.97、LY.CAREER.HJC.99

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.99 征战·子化死爻，曹操丧师于赤壁（旧注：子孙为我军卒，动入死墓空绝败，应又克世，或鬼父动伤身世，必致三军丧命、损兵折将；如曹操夸水军八十万众袭吴，为周瑜黄盖火攻所败）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙是我方的士兵。子孙发动变入死、墓、空、绝、败，应爻又来克世爻，或者官鬼、父母发动伤克世身，必定三军丧命、损兵折将，就像曹操夸耀水军八十万，乘势袭击吴国，被周瑜、黄盖的火攻打败。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output is our soldiers. If Output moves into death, tomb, Void, extinction or defeat, and the Other also controls the Self, or Pressure or Resource moves and injures the Self or Body, the three armies will surely lose their lives and the troops and officers be lost, like Cao Cao, who boasted of eight hundred thousand marines, attacked Wu in the same breath, and was beaten by the fire attack of Zhou Yu and Huang Gai.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5943 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5943*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子化死爻，曹操丧师于赤壁； | 子孙变成死爻，就像曹操在赤壁丧失了大军； | If Output turns into a dead line, it is like Cao Cao losing his army at Red Cliff; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5944 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5944*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙为我军卒。 | 子孙是我方的士兵。 | Output is our soldiers. |
| 若动入死墓空绝败，应又克世，或鬼父动伤身世； | 如果它发动变入死、墓、空、绝、败，应爻又克世爻，或者官鬼、父母发动伤克世身； | If it moves into death, tomb, Void, extinction or defeat, and the Other also controls the Self, or Pressure or Resource moves and injures the Self or Body; |
| 必致三军丧命，损兵折将之兆。 | 必定是三军丧命、损兵折将的征兆。 | it is surely a sign that the three armies will lose their lives and the troops and officers be lost. |
| 如曹操夸水军八十万众，乘势袭吴，而为周瑜、黄盖火攻所败。 | 就像曹操夸耀水军八十万，乘势袭击吴国，结果被周瑜、黄盖的火攻打败。 | It is like Cao Cao, who boasted of eight hundred thousand marines, attacked Wu in the same breath, and was beaten by the fire attack of Zhou Yu and Huang Gai. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1538 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1538*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙为我军卒，若动入死墓绝败，应临鬼父动伤身世，必致损兵折将，如曹操为周瑜黄盖火攻所败。 | 子孙是我方的士兵，如果发动变入死、墓、绝、败，应爻临着官鬼、父母发动伤克世身，必定损兵折将，就像曹操被周瑜、黄盖的火攻打败。 | Output is our soldiers; if it moves into death, tomb, extinction or defeat, and the Other sits on Pressure or Resource and moves to injure the Self or Body, the troops and officers will surely be lost, like Cao Cao beaten by the fire attack of Zhou Yu and Huang Gai. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1538 行:子化死爻，曹操丧师于赤壁
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 414 行:子化死爻，曹操丧师于赤壁

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.98、LY.CAREER.HJC.100

## LY.CAREER.HJC.100 征战·世逢绝地，项羽自刎于乌江（旧注：世为一国之君、三军之帅，宜旺相克应为吉，若被应爻刑冲克害而世又休囚，或动入死墓空绝，主将必不利；如项羽百战百胜而一朝兵散，羞见江东父老自刎乌江）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻是一国之君、三军之帅，最好旺相、克住应爻才吉；如果被应爻刑、冲、克、害，世爻又休囚，或者发动变入死、墓、空、绝，主将必然不利，就像项羽百战百胜，一朝兵败星散，羞于去见江东父老，在乌江自刎。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Self is the ruler of a state and the commander of the three armies; it is best thriving or strong and controlling the Other. If it is punished, clashed with, controlled or harmed by the Other, and the Self is also resting or trapped, or moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, the chief commander is surely in trouble, like Xiang Yu, who won a hundred battles in a hundred, yet in one morning his troops scattered and, ashamed to face the elders east of the river, he cut his own throat at Wu River.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5946 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5946*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世逢绝地，项羽自刎于乌江。 | 世爻遇到绝地，就像项羽在乌江自刎。 | If the Self meets a place of extinction, it is like Xiang Yu cutting his own throat at Wu River. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5947 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5947*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世乃一国之君，三军之帅。 | 世爻是一国的君主、三军的统帅。 | The Self is the ruler of a state and the commander of the three armies. |
| 最宜旺相，克应为吉，若被应爻刑冲克害，而世又休囚，及动入死墓空绝，主将必然不利。 | 最好旺相，克住应爻才吉；如果被应爻刑、冲、克、害，而世爻又休囚，以及发动变入死、墓、空、绝，主将必然不利。 | It is best thriving or strong and controlling the Other; if it is punished, clashed with, controlled or harmed by the Other, and the Self is also resting or trapped, or moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, the chief commander is surely in trouble. |
| 如项羽百战百胜，而一朝兵散，羞见江东父老，自刎乌江也。 | 就像项羽百战百胜，一朝兵败星散，羞于去见江东父老，在乌江自刎。 | It is like Xiang Yu, who won a hundred battles in a hundred, yet in one morning his troops scattered and, ashamed to face the elders east of the river, he cut his own throat at Wu River. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1540 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1540*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世为国主、三军之帅，宜旺动克应，若衰世而被应爻刑冲克害，动入死墓空绝者，如项羽自刎于乌江也。 | 世是国主、三军的统帅，宜旺相发动、克住应爻；如果世衰弱而被应爻刑、冲、克、害，发动变入死、墓、空、绝，就像项羽在乌江自刎。 | The Self is the ruler of the state and commander of the three armies, and should be thriving and moving and controlling the Other; if the Self is weak and is punished, clashed with, controlled or harmed by the Other and moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, it is like Xiang Yu cutting his own throat at Wu River. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1540 行:世逢绝地，项羽自刎于乌江
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 417 行:世逢绝地，项羽自刎于乌江

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.99、LY.CAREER.HJC.101

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.101 征战·水鬼克身，秦符坚有淝水之败（旧注：水鬼旺动伤克世身，敌兵必得舟楫渡江之利，如苻坚以投鞭断流之众竟败于谢玄八千渡江之兵）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:水爻的官鬼旺相发动，伤克世爻或世身，敌兵必定占到乘船渡江的便利，就像苻坚凭着能投鞭断流的大军，竟然败给了谢玄八千渡江的兵马。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a Water Pressure line is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Self or Body, the enemy troops will surely gain the advantage of crossing the river by boat, like Fu Jian, who with a host that could dam the river with the whips they threw in still lost to the eight thousand troops Xie Xuan brought across the river.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5949 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5949*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水鬼克身，秦符坚有淝水之败； | 水爻官鬼克制世身，就像秦国的苻坚遭到淝水的失败； | If a Water Pressure line controls the Self or Body, it is like Fu Jian of Qin meeting defeat at the Fei River; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5950 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5950*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水鬼旺动，伤克世身，敌兵必得舟揖渡江之利。 | 水爻的官鬼旺相发动，伤克世身，敌兵必定占到舟船渡江的便利。 | If a Water Pressure line is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Self or Body, the enemy troops will surely gain the advantage of crossing the river by boat. |
| 如秦符坚，以投鞭断流之众，而竟败于谢玄八千渡江之兵也。 | 就像秦国的苻坚，凭着投鞭断流的大军，竟然败给了谢玄八千渡江的兵马。 | It is like Fu Jian of Qin, who with a host that could dam the river with the whips they threw in still lost to the eight thousand troops Xie Xuan brought across the river. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1542 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1542*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 水鬼旺动伤克世身，敌兵必得舟楫渡江之利，如秦苻坚败于谢玄八千渡江之兵也。 | 水爻的官鬼旺相发动，伤克世身，敌兵必定占到舟船渡江的便利，就像秦国的苻坚败给了谢玄八千渡江的兵马。 | If a Water Pressure line is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Self or Body, the enemy troops will surely gain the advantage of crossing the river by boat, like Fu Jian of Qin who lost to the eight thousand troops Xie Xuan brought across the river. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.92) 第 1542 行:水鬼克身，秦苻坚有淝水之败
- 《黄金策》:异文(相似 0.92) 第 420 行:水鬼克身，秦苻坚有淝水之败

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.100、LY.CAREER.HJC.102

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.102 征战·火官持世，汉高祖遇平城之围（旧注：火爻带鬼则贼寨必近，火鬼持世须防困围；子孙旺动虽被围亦得胜如田单下齐城，子衰官旺必致困围如汉高祖被匈奴围于平城七日乃解）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:火爻带着官鬼，敌人的营寨一定很近；火鬼持世，要防被围困。这时如果子孙旺相发动，即使被围也能得胜，像田单下齐城；如果子孙衰弱、官鬼旺相，必定陷入围困，像汉高祖被匈奴围在平城，七天才解围。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a Fire line carries Pressure, the enemy's stockade is surely near; if a Fire Pressure line holds the Self, guard against being besieged. Then if Output is thriving and moves, one wins even when besieged, like Tian Dan's taking of the cities of Qi; if Output is weak and Pressure is thriving, one is surely besieged, like the founder of Han, whom the Xiongnu surrounded at Pingcheng until the siege was lifted after seven days.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5952 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5952*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火官持世，汉高祖遇平城之围。 | 火官持世，就像汉高祖遇到平城之围。 | If a Fire Pressure line holds the Self, it is like the founder of Han meeting the siege of Pingcheng. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5953 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5953*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火爻带鬼，贼寨必近。 | 火爻带着官鬼，敌人的营寨一定很近。 | If a Fire line carries Pressure, the enemy's stockade is surely near. |
| 若火鬼持世，须防困围。 | 如果火鬼持世，要防被围困。 | If a Fire Pressure line holds the Self, guard against being besieged. |
| 若得子孙旺动，虽被围得胜。 | 如果得到子孙旺相发动，即使被围也能得胜。 | If Output is thriving and moves, one wins even when besieged. |
| 如田单之下齐城。 | 就像田单攻下齐国的城池。 | It is like Tian Dan's taking of the cities of Qi. |
| 若子衰官旺，必致困围。 | 如果子孙衰弱、官鬼旺相，必定陷入围困。 | If Output is weak and Pressure is thriving, one is surely besieged. |
| 如汉高祖被匈奴围于平城，七日乃解。 | 就像汉高祖被匈奴围困在平城，七天才解围。 | It is like the founder of Han, whom the Xiongnu surrounded at Pingcheng until the siege was lifted after seven days. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1544 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1544*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 火官带鬼，贼寨必近，火爻持世，须防困围。 | 火官带着官鬼，敌人的营寨一定很近，火爻持世，要防被围困。 | If a Fire line carries Pressure, the enemy's stockade is surely near, and if a Fire line holds the Self, guard against being besieged. |
| 子孙旺动被围得胜，若子衰官旺，如汉高祖被围平城七日乃解。 | 子孙旺相发动，即使被围也得胜；如果子孙衰弱、官鬼旺相，就像汉高祖被围在平城，七天才解围。 | If Output is thriving and moves, one wins even when besieged; if Output is weak and Pressure is thriving, it is like the founder of Han surrounded at Pingcheng until the siege was lifted after seven days. |

> **按**:旧注只写「如田单之下齐城」；田单是被燕军围在即墨、反而打败燕军的齐国将领，正合「虽被围得胜」。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary writes only 'like Tian Dan's taking of the cities of Qi'; Tian Dan was a Qi general besieged at Jimo by the army of Yan who turned round and beat it, which fits 'wins even when besieged'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1544 行:火官持世，汉高祖遇平城之围
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 423 行:火官持世，汉高祖遇平城之围

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.101、LY.CAREER.HJC.103

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.103 征战·应官克世卦无财，张睢阳食尽而毙（旧注：应持鬼克世、卦又无财，是食尽死亡之象，如张巡被围睢阳城）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:应爻持着官鬼来克世爻，卦里又没有妻财，是粮食吃尽、困死的象，就像张巡被围在睢阳城。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Other holds Pressure and controls the Self, and there is no Wealth in the hexagram, it is the image of running out of food and dying, like Zhang Xun besieged in Suiyang.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5955 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5955*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应官克世卦无财，张睢阳食尽而毙。 | 应爻的官鬼克世爻，卦里又没有财，就像张睢阳粮食吃尽而死。 | If the Other's Pressure line controls the Self and the hexagram has no Wealth, it is like Zhang of Suiyang who ran out of food and died. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5956 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5956*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应持鬼，克世，卦又无财，乃是食尽死亡之象。 | 应爻持着官鬼，克世爻，卦里又没有妻财，就是粮食吃尽、困死的象。 | If the Other holds Pressure and controls the Self, and the hexagram has no Wealth, it is the image of running out of food and dying. |
| 如张巡被围睢阳城也。 | 就像张巡被围在睢阳城。 | It is like Zhang Xun besieged in Suiyang. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1546 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1546*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应爻持鬼冲世，卦中无财，乃食尽死亡之象，如张巡被围睢阳城也。 | 应爻持着官鬼冲世爻，卦中没有妻财，是粮食吃尽、困死的象，就像张巡被围在睢阳城。 | If the Other holds Pressure and clashes with the Self, and there is no Wealth in the hexagram, it is the image of running out of food and dying, like Zhang Xun besieged in Suiyang. |

> **按**:旧注说应爻鬼「克世」，卜筮正宗说「冲世」。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary says the Other's Pressure line 'controls the Self'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says 'clashes with the Self'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1546 行:应官克世卦无财，张睢阳食尽而毙
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 426 行:应官克世卦无财，张睢阳食尽而毙

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.102、LY.CAREER.HJC.104

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.104 征战·世鬼兴隆生合应，吕文焕无援而降（旧注：旺鬼持世是困围之象，卦又无财、子孙又弱、世又生合应爻，是兵少食尽降敌之兆；如宋吕文焕守襄阳，元兵围之甚久，贾似道隐蔽不援，城中食尽遂降）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:旺相的官鬼持世，是被围困的象；卦里又没有妻财、子孙又衰弱，世爻还去生合应爻，是兵少粮尽、向敌人投降的征兆，就像宋朝的吕文焕守襄阳，被元兵围了很久，贾似道隐瞒不发援兵，城中粮尽，于是投降。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A thriving Pressure line holding the Self is the image of being besieged; if the hexagram also has no Wealth, Output is weak, and the Self also generates or combines with the Other, it is a sign of few troops, food run out and surrender to the enemy, like Lü Wenhuan of Song, who held Xiangyang while the Yuan troops besieged it for a very long time, Jia Sidao concealed the matter and sent no relief, and the city ran out of food and surrendered.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5958 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5958*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世鬼兴隆生合应，吕文焕无援而降。 | 世爻上的官鬼兴旺、又生合应爻，就像吕文焕没有援兵而投降。 | If a thriving Pressure line on the Self generates or combines with the Other, it is like Lü Wenhuan who had no relief and surrendered. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5959 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5959*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旺鬼持世，乃困围之象。 | 旺相的官鬼持世，是被围困的象。 | A thriving Pressure line holding the Self is the image of being besieged. |
| 卦又无财，子孙又弱，世又生合应爻，乃兵少食尽降敌之兆。 | 卦里又没有妻财，子孙又衰弱，世爻又生合应爻，是兵少粮尽、向敌人投降的征兆。 | If the hexagram also has no Wealth, Output is weak, and the Self also generates or combines with the Other, it is a sign of few troops, food run out and surrender to the enemy. |
| 宋吕文焕守襄阳，元兵围之甚久，贾似道隐蔽不援，城中食尽遂降。 | 宋朝的吕文焕守襄阳，元兵围了他很久，贾似道隐瞒不发援兵，城中粮食吃尽，于是投降。 | Lü Wenhuan of Song held Xiangyang while the Yuan troops besieged him for a very long time, Jia Sidao concealed the matter and sent no relief, and when the food in the city ran out he surrendered. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1548 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1548*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旺鬼持世，乃困围之象，卦爻又无财，子孙又弱，世又生合应爻，乃兵少食尽降敌之兆。 | 旺相的官鬼持世，是被围困的象，卦爻又没有妻财，子孙又衰弱，世爻又生合应爻，是兵少粮尽、向敌人投降的征兆。 | A thriving Pressure line holding the Self is the image of being besieged; if the lines also have no Wealth, Output is weak, and the Self also generates or combines with the Other, it is a sign of few troops, food run out and surrender to the enemy. |
| 宋吕文焕守襄阳，元兵围久，贾似道隐蔽不援，城中食尽遂降。 | 宋朝的吕文焕守襄阳，元兵围了很久，贾似道隐瞒不发援兵，城中粮食吃尽，于是投降。 | Lü Wenhuan of Song held Xiangyang while the Yuan troops besieged it for long, Jia Sidao concealed the matter and sent no relief, and when the food in the city ran out he surrendered. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1548 行:世鬼兴隆生合应，吕文焕无援而降
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 429 行:世鬼兴隆生合应，吕文焕无援而降

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.103、LY.CAREER.HJC.105

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.105 征战·外宫子动化绝爻，李陵所以降虏（旧注：子在外卦动、应克世，是我军远征，初虽见胜终必有败，又化绝爻不免降虏，如汉武帝时李陵）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙在外卦发动、应爻克制世爻，是我军远征的象，起初虽然能得胜，最终必定失败；子孙又变成绝爻，免不了投降敌虏，就像汉武帝时李陵的往事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Output moves in the outer trigram and the Other controls the Self, it is the image of our army on a distant campaign: at first it may win, but in the end it must lose, and if Output also turns into an extinct line it cannot avoid surrendering to the enemy, like Li Ling in the time of Emperor Wu of Han.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5961 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5961*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 外宫子动化绝爻，李陵所以降虏。 | 外宫的子孙发动又变成绝爻，这就是李陵投降敌虏的缘故。 | If Output moves in the outer palace and turns into an extinct line, this is why Li Ling surrendered to the enemy. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5962 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5962*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子在外宫动，而被应克世，是我军远征。 | 子孙在外宫发动，而应爻克世爻，是我军远征。 | If Output moves in the outer palace and the Other controls the Self, it is our army on a distant campaign. |
| 初虽见胜，终必有败。 | 起初虽然见到胜利，最终必定失败。 | At first a victory may be seen, but in the end there must be defeat. |
| 又化绝爻，不免降虏。 | 又变成绝爻，免不了投降敌虏。 | If it also turns into an extinct line, surrender to the enemy cannot be avoided. |
| 如汉武时李陵往事也。 | 就像汉武帝时李陵的往事。 | It is like what happened to Li Ling in the time of Emperor Wu of Han. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1550 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1550*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子在外宫动，世被应克，终必有败，又化绝爻，不免降虏，如汉武帝时李陵之事也。 | 子孙在外宫发动，世爻被应爻克，最终必定失败，又变成绝爻，免不了投降敌虏，就像汉武帝时李陵的事。 | If Output moves in the outer palace and the Self is controlled by the Other, there will in the end be defeat, and if it also turns into an extinct line, surrender to the enemy cannot be avoided, like the affair of Li Ling in the time of Emperor Wu of Han. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1550 行:外宫子动化绝爻，李陵所以降虏
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 432 行:外宫子动化绝爻，李陵所以降虏

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.104、LY.CAREER.HJC.106

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.106 征战·内卦福兴生合应，乐毅所以背燕（旧注：内卦子孙发动反去生合应爻、伤克世身，是我将卒背主降敌之兆，如燕将乐毅背燕投赵）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:内卦的子孙发动，反而去生合应爻、伤克世身，是我方将领士兵背叛主人、投降敌人的征兆，就像燕国的将领乐毅背弃燕国、投奔赵国。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Output in the inner trigram moves and, instead, generates or combines with the Other and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is a sign that our generals and soldiers will betray their lord and surrender to the enemy, like Yue Yi, general of Yan, who turned his back on Yan and went over to Zhao.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5964 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5964*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内卦福兴生合应，乐毅所以背燕。 | 内卦的福神兴旺、又生合应爻，这就是乐毅背弃燕国的缘故。 | If the Output line in the inner trigram is thriving and generates or combines with the Other, this is why Yue Yi turned his back on Yan. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5965 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5965*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内卦子孙动，而反生合应爻，伤克身世，是我将卒有背主降敌之兆。 | 内卦的子孙发动，反而去生合应爻，伤克世身，是我方将领士兵背叛主人、投降敌人的征兆。 | If Output in the inner trigram moves and, instead, generates or combines with the Other and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is a sign that our generals and soldiers will betray their lord and surrender to the enemy. |
| 如燕将乐毅背燕投赵是也。 | 就像燕国的将领乐毅背弃燕国、投奔赵国。 | It is like Yue Yi, general of Yan, who turned his back on Yan and went over to Zhao. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1552 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1552*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙发动反去生合应爻，伤克身世，是我将卒有背主降敌之兆，如燕将乐毅背燕投赵是也。 | 子孙发动，反而去生合应爻，伤克世身，是我方将领士兵背叛主人、投降敌人的征兆，就像燕国的将领乐毅背弃燕国、投奔赵国。 | If Output moves and, instead, generates or combines with the Other and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is a sign that our generals and soldiers will betray their lord and surrender to the enemy, like Yue Yi, general of Yan, who turned his back on Yan and went over to Zhao. |

> **按**:旧注说「内卦子孙动」，卜筮正宗只说「子孙发动」，没有限定在内卦。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary says 'Output moves in the inner trigram'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says only 'Output moves' and does not limit it to the inner trigram.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1552 行:内卦福兴生合应，乐毅所以背燕
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 435 行:内卦福兴生合应，乐毅所以背燕

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.105、LY.CAREER.HJC.107

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.107 征战·鬼虽衰而遇生扶，勿追穷寇（旧注：官爻虽衰，若遇动爻日辰生扶拱合，是敌兵虽少必有救援）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻虽然衰弱，但如果遇到动爻、日辰来生、扶、拱、合它，那就是敌兵虽然少，也一定有救援，不要去追击陷入穷途的敌人。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Though a Pressure line is weak, if a moving line or the Day comes to generate, support, hold up or combine with it, then the enemy troops, though few, will surely have relief, and one should not chase a desperate foe.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5967 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5967*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼虽衰而遇生扶，勿追穷寇。 | 官鬼虽然衰弱，但遇到生扶，就不要追击走投无路的敌人。 | Though Pressure is weak, if it meets generation and support, do not chase a desperate foe. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5968 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5968*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻虽衰，若遇动爻日辰，生扶拱合，是敌兵虽少，必有救援。 | 官爻虽然衰弱，如果遇到动爻、日辰来生、扶、拱、合，就是敌兵虽然少，也必定有救援。 | Though a Pressure line is weak, if it meets a moving line or the Day generating, supporting, holding up or combining with it, the enemy troops, though few, will surely have relief. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1554 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1554*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻虽衰，若遇动爻日辰生扶拱合，是敌兵虽少，必有救援。 | 官爻虽然衰弱，如果遇到动爻、日辰来生、扶、拱、合，就是敌兵虽然少，也必定有救援。 | Though a Pressure line is weak, if it meets a moving line or the Day generating, supporting, holding up or combining with it, the enemy troops, though few, will surely have relief. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1554 行:鬼虽衰而遇生扶，勿追穷寇
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 438 行:鬼虽衰而遇生扶，勿追穷寇

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.106、LY.CAREER.HJC.108

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.108 征战·子虽旺而遭克制，毋急兴师（旧注：子孙虽旺，若被父母日辰动爻克害，彼必有计，不可急攻；攻之必被摧折，虽不大败亦损军威，宜缓图之）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙虽然旺相，但如果被父母、日辰、动爻克害，对方一定有计谋，不可急着进攻；进攻就必定受挫，即使不至于大败，也会损伤军威，应当缓缓图谋。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Though Output is thriving, if it is controlled and harmed by Resource, the Day or a moving line, the enemy surely has a stratagem and one must not attack in haste; an attack will be broken, and even if it is not a great defeat it will damage the army's prestige, so one should plan it slowly.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5970 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5970*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子虽旺而遭克制，毋急兴师。 | 子孙虽然旺相却遭到克制，不要急着出兵。 | Though Output is thriving, if it is controlled, do not send out troops in haste. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5971 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5971*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙虽旺，若被父母日辰动爻克害，彼必有计，不可急攻。 | 子孙虽然旺相，如果被父母、日辰、动爻克害，对方一定有计谋，不可急着进攻。 | Though Output is thriving, if it is controlled and harmed by Resource, the Day or a moving line, the enemy surely has a stratagem and one must not attack in haste. |
| 攻之，必被摧折； | 进攻的话，必定受到摧折； | If one attacks, one will be broken; |
| 虽不大败，亦损军威。 | 即使不至于大败，也会损伤军威。 | even if it is not a great defeat, it will damage the army's prestige. |
| 宜缓图之。 | 应当缓缓图谋。 | One should plan it slowly. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1554–1555 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, lines 1554–1555*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙虽旺，若被日辰动爻克害，彼必有计，不可急攻，攻之必被摧折，虽不大败，亦损军威。 | 子孙虽然旺相，如果被日辰、动爻克害，对方一定有计谋，不可急着进攻，进攻必定受到摧折，即使不至于大败，也会损伤军威。 | Though Output is thriving, if it is controlled and harmed by the Day or a moving line, the enemy surely has a stratagem and one must not attack in haste; an attack will be broken, and even if it is not a great defeat it will damage the army's prestige. |
| 宜缓图之。 | 应当缓缓图谋。 | One should plan it slowly. |

> **按**:旧注说克害子孙的是「父母日辰动爻」，卜筮正宗只说「日辰动爻」，没有父母。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary names the controllers of Output as 'Resource, the Day or a moving line'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong says only 'the Day or a moving line', without Resource.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1554 行:子虽旺而遭克制，毋急兴师
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 441 行:子虽旺而遭克制，毋急兴师

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.107、LY.CAREER.HJC.109

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.109 征战·鬼爻暗动伤身，吴王被专诸之刺（旧注：官爻虽静而被日辰冲动旺相，克害身世，必若吴王被专诸之刺；鬼暗动而被世克，或子动来救，则如荆轲刺秦王不中反被诛）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻本来安静，却被日辰冲动、又旺相，克害世爻或世身，必定像吴王被专诸刺杀；如果鬼暗动却被世爻克住，或者子孙发动来救，就像荆轲刺秦王没有刺中，反而自己被杀。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If a Pressure line is still but the Day clashes it into motion and it is thriving and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is surely like the King of Wu stabbed by Zhuanzhu; if the Pressure line moves in secret but is controlled by the Self, or Output moves to rescue, it is like Jing Ke, who failed to stab the King of Qin and was himself put to death instead.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5973 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5973*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼爻暗动伤身，吴王被专诸之刺。 | 官鬼暗中发动伤克世身，就像吴王被专诸刺杀。 | If a Pressure line moves in secret and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is like the King of Wu stabbed by Zhuanzhu. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5974 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5974*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官爻虽静，而被日辰冲动旺相，克害身世，必若吴王被专诸之刺。 | 官爻虽然安静，却被日辰冲动、又旺相，克害世爻或世身，必定像吴王被专诸刺杀。 | If a Pressure line is still but is clashed into motion by the Day and is thriving, and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is surely like the King of Wu stabbed by Zhuanzhu. |
| 若鬼暗动，而被世克，或子动来救，则如荆何刺秦王不中，而反自被诛也。 | 如果鬼暗中发动却被世爻克住，或者子孙发动来救，就像荆轲（这里原文作「荆何」）刺秦王没有刺中，反而自己被杀。 | If the Pressure line moves in secret but is controlled by the Self, or Output moves to rescue, it is like Jing Ke (written Jing He here), who failed to stab the King of Qin and was himself put to death instead. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1557 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1557*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旺官暗动克害世身，如吴王被专诸之刺； | 旺相的官鬼暗中发动，克害世爻或世身，就像吴王被专诸刺杀； | If a thriving Pressure line moves in secret and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is like the King of Wu stabbed by Zhuanzhu; |
| 世克暗动之鬼或子动来救，如荆轲刺秦王不中，自反被诛也。 | 世爻克住暗动的鬼，或者子孙发动来救，就像荆轲刺秦王没有刺中，反而自己被杀。 | if the Self controls the secretly moving Pressure line, or Output moves to rescue, it is like Jing Ke, who failed to stab the King of Qin and was himself put to death instead. |

> **按**:旧注把荆轲写成「荆何」，卜筮正宗写「荆轲」。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary writes Jing Ke as 'Jing He'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes 'Jing Ke'.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 1557 行:鬼爻暗动伤身，吴王被专诸之刺
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 444 行:鬼爻暗动伤身，吴王被专诸之刺

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.108、LY.CAREER.HJC.110

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.110 征战·子化官爻克世，张飞遭范张之诛（旧注：子孙化官生合应而克害身世，是我兵卒谋杀主帅而欲降敌之象，如后汉张翼德素不恤士卒，被小卒范强、张达枭首帐下，顺流投孙权）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙变出官鬼，去生合应爻、克害世爻或世身，是我方的士兵谋杀主帅、想要投降敌人的象，就像后汉的张翼德，平时不体恤士兵，有一天被小兵范强、张达在帐下砍了头，顺流而下投奔孙权。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Output turns into Pressure that generates or combines with the Other and controls and harms the Self or Body, it is the image of our soldiers murdering their commander in order to surrender to the enemy, like Zhang Yide of the Later Han, who never cared for his soldiers and one day had his head cut off in his tent by the common soldiers Fan Qiang and Zhang Da, who went down the river to Sun Quan.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5976 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5976*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子化官爻克世，张飞遭范张之诛。 | 子孙变出官鬼来克世爻，就像张飞遭到范强、张达的诛杀。 | If Output turns into Pressure that controls the Self, it is like Zhang Fei put to death by Fan and Zhang. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5977 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5977*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙化官生合应，而克害身世，乃是我兵卒谋杀主帅，而欲降敌之象。 | 子孙变出官鬼、去生合应爻，而克害世爻或世身，就是我方的士兵谋杀主帅、想要投降敌人的象。 | If Output turns into Pressure and generates or combines with the Other and controls and harms the Self or Body, it is the image of our soldiers murdering their commander in order to surrender to the enemy. |
| 如后汉张翼德，素不恤士卒，一日被小卒范强、张达枭首帐下，顺流而投孙权也。 | 就像后汉的张翼德，平时不体恤士兵，有一天被小兵范强、张达在帐下砍了头，顺流而下投奔孙权。 | It is like Zhang Yide of the Later Han, who never cared for his soldiers and one day had his head cut off in his tent by the common soldiers Fan Qiang and Zhang Da, who went down the river to Sun Quan. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1559 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1559*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙化官鬼生合应爻，反来克害身世者，是我兵卒杀主降敌，如后汉张翼德被部卒范疆、张达之刺帐下，因之而投孙权也。 | 子孙变出官鬼、去生合应爻，反过来克害世爻或世身的，是我方的士兵杀主帅投降敌人，就像后汉的张翼德被部下范疆、张达在帐下刺杀，他们因此投奔孙权。 | If Output turns into Pressure that generates or combines with the Other and in turn controls and harms the Self or Body, it is our soldiers killing their commander to surrender to the enemy, like Zhang Yide of the Later Han, stabbed in his tent by his men Fan Jiang and Zhang Da, who therefore went over to Sun Quan. |

> **按**:范强、范疆是同一个人的两种写法：旧注作「范强」，卜筮正宗作「范疆」。
>
> **Note:** 'Fan Qiang' and 'Fan Jiang' are two ways of writing the same man's name: the old commentary has the first and the Bu Shi Zheng Zong the second.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.92) 第 1559 行:子化官爻持世，张飞遭范张之诛
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 447 行:子化官爻克世，张飞遭范张之诛

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.109、LY.CAREER.HJC.111

---

## LY.CAREER.HJC.111 征战·要识用兵之利器，五行卦象并推详；仁智勇器之将，岂越于此（旧注：土为炮石，金为刀箭，水木为舟，火为营寨；乾兑为刀，震巽为弓马，坤为野战；有克应之神宜用此器克敌，应爻官父在此象克世则反防敌用此器）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:要知道用兵该用什么器械，要把五行和卦象一起推究：土是炮石，金是刀箭，水、木是舟船，火是营寨；乾、兑是刀，震、巽是弓马，坤是野战之类。哪个象里有能克应爻的神，就适合用这种器械去克敌；应爻的官鬼或父母在这个象里克世爻，就要反过来防备敌人用这种器械。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To know the sharp tools for war, work out the five elements and the hexagram images together: Earth is cannon-stones, Metal is blades and arrows, Water and Wood are boats, Fire is the camp; Qian and Dui are blades, Zhen and Xun are bows and horses, Kun is open-field fighting and the like. Where an image holds a spirit that controls the Other, it is fit to use that tool against the enemy; where the Other's Pressure or Resource in that image controls the Self, one must instead guard against the enemy using that tool.

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5979 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5979*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 要识用兵之利器，五行卦象并推详。 | 要认识用兵的利器，要把五行和卦象一起推究。 | To know the sharp tools for war, work out the five elements and the hexagram images together. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5980 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5980*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土为炮石，金为刀箭，水木为舟，火为营寨。 | 土是炮石，金是刀箭，水、木是舟船，火是营寨。 | Earth is cannon-stones, Metal is blades and arrows, Water and Wood are boats, Fire is the camp. |
| 又乾兑为刀，震巽为弓马，火枪，坤为野战类。 | 又有乾、兑是刀，震、巽是弓马，火是枪，坤是野战之类。 | Also Qian and Dui are blades, Zhen and Xun are bows and horses, Fire is the lance, and Kun is open-field fighting and the like. |
| 若此象有克应之神，宜用此器以克敌； | 如果这个象里有克应爻的神，适合用这种器械去克敌； | If this image holds a spirit that controls the Other, it is fit to use that tool against the enemy; |
| 应爻官父在此象克世，宜反防敌人用此器也。 | 应爻的官鬼、父母在这个象里克世爻，就要反过来防备敌人用这种器械。 | if the Other's Pressure or Resource in this image controls the Self, one should instead guard against the enemy using that tool. |

**王洪绪注(《卜筮正宗》)** — 《卜筮正宗》第 1561 行 · *Wang Hongxu's commentary (Bushi Zhengzong); Bushi Zhengzong, line 1561*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 土为炮石，金为刀箭，水木为舟，火为营寨； | 土是炮石，金是刀箭，水、木是舟船，火是营寨； | Earth is cannon-stones, Metal is blades and arrows, Water and Wood are boats, Fire is the camp; |
| 又乾兑为刀，震巽为弓马，火为枪，坤为野战类。 | 又有乾、兑是刀，震、巽是弓马，火是枪，坤是野战之类。 | also Qian and Dui are blades, Zhen and Xun are bows and horses, Fire is the lance, and Kun is open-field fighting and the like. |
| 若有克应之神，宜用此器敌之，如应爻克世，须防敌人用此器也。 | 如果有克应爻的神，适合用这种器械去对付敌人，如果应爻克世爻，就要防备敌人用这种器械。 | If there is a spirit that controls the Other, it is fit to use that tool against the enemy; if the Other controls the Self, one must guard against the enemy using that tool. |

**《黄金策·征战》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 5982 行 · *Huangjin Ce (The Golden Strategy), "Campaigns and war", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5982*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 仁智勇器之将，岂越于此？ | 仁、智、勇和器械，作为一个将领所要的这些，哪里能越出这些象之外？ | Benevolence, wisdom, courage and the tools of war, which a general needs, how could they go beyond these? |
| 攻守克敌用兵，当审于时。 | 是攻是守、克敌用兵，都应当看准时机。 | Whether to attack or defend, to overcome the enemy and deploy troops, one must judge the moment. |

> **按**:旧注「震巽为弓马，火枪」，卜筮正宗写成「火为枪」，译文按卜筮正宗读作「火是枪」。
>
> **Note:** The old commentary has 'Zhen and Xun are bows and horses, fire lance'; the Bu Shi Zheng Zong writes 'Fire is the lance', and the translation reads it so.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 1561 行:要试用兵之利器，五行卦象并推详
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 450 行:要识用兵之利器，五行卦象并推详
- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.9) 第 1563 行:仁智勇严之将岂越于此;异文(相似 0.8) 第 1563 行:攻守克敌当审于时
- 《黄金策》:同文 第 453 行:仁智勇器之将岂越于此;同文 第 453 行:攻守克敌用兵当审于时

相关:LY.CAREER.HJC.110、LY.CAREER.HJC.86

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.50 征战占总述（游南子）：兵凶战危，观八卦知方向，观世应知彼己，观六亲知师徒，观五行知器具，观日辰知我兵之淑慝

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:征战占总述：用兵凶险，打仗危险，关系人命生死和国家安危，所以要慎重。看八卦来知道方向；看世爻、应爻来知道彼此；看六亲来知道将帅和士卒；看五行来知道器械用具；看日辰来知道我方兵士的好坏。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Overview of divining war. Arms are ill-omened and war is perilous, bearing on life and death and the safety of the state, so one must be careful. Look at the eight trigrams to know the direction; at the Self and Other to know them and us; at the six kin to know the commanders and the troops; at the five elements to know the arms and gear; and at the Day to know whether my soldiers are good or bad.

**游南子(《易隐》引)** — 《易隐》第 2013 行 · *Master Younan (quoted in the Yi Yin); Yi Yin, line 2013*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 游南子曰，兵凶战危，古人慎之，谓其关人命之生死，系宗稷之安危也。 | 游南子说：兵事凶险，战争危险，古人对它很慎重，是因为它关系到人命的生死，牵系着宗庙社稷的安危。 | Master Younan says: arms are ill-omened and war is perilous, and the ancients were careful of it, because it bears on the life and death of men and hangs on the safety of the ancestral temple and the altars of the state. |
| 故观八卦，以知方向。 | 所以看八卦，来知道方向。 | So look at the eight trigrams to know the direction. |
| 观世应以知彼己，观六亲以知师徒，观五行以知器具，观日辰，以知我兵之淑慝。 | 看世爻、应爻来知道彼此，看六亲来知道将帅和士卒，看五行来知道器械用具，看日辰来知道我方兵士的善恶。 | Look at the Self and Other to know them and us, at the six kin to know the commanders and the troops, at the five elements to know the arms and gear, and at the Day to know whether my soldiers are good or bad. |
| 盖绳绳乎其慎之也。 | 大概是要时时谨慎地对待它。 | It is, after all, to be treated with constant care. |

> **按**:这一节是征战占的提纲，后面五节（方向、彼己、六亲、五行、日辰）就是按这个顺序排的。「绳绳」书里写作「盖绳绳乎其慎之也」，字面不很清楚，译文取「时时、连绵不断」的意思。
>
> **Note:** This section is the outline of the divination of war, and the five sections after it (direction, them and us, the six kin, the five elements and the Day) follow this order. In 'sheng sheng hu qi shen zhi ye', the sense of 'sheng sheng' is not clear from the letter, and the translation takes the sense of 'constantly, without a break'.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.51、LY.CAREER.HJC.86、LY.CAREER.HJC.111

## LY.CAREER.YY.51 征战占·方向（一）：内卦为我寨外卦为彼营，内卦旺相吉、胎没次之、死囚休废凶；附八卦旺相胎没死囚休废随八节的排列

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:方向：内卦是我方的营寨，外卦是对方的军营。内卦旺相的，吉；胎、没次一等；死、囚、休、废的，凶。八卦的旺、相、胎、没、死、囚、休、废随八个节气排列：立春后艮旺、震相、巽胎、离没、坤死、兑囚、乾休、坎废；春分后震旺、巽相、离胎、坤没、兑死、乾囚、坎休、艮废；立夏后巽旺、离相、坤胎、兑没、乾死、坎囚、艮休、震废；夏至后离旺、坤相、兑胎、乾没、坎死、艮囚、震休、巽废；立秋后坤旺、兑相、乾胎、坎没、艮死、震囚、巽休、离废；秋分后兑旺、乾相、坎胎、艮没、震死、巽囚、离休、坤废；立冬后乾旺、坎相、艮胎、震没、巽死、离囚、坤休、兑废；冬至后坎旺、艮相、震胎、巽没、离死、坤囚、兑休、乾废。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Direction. The inner trigram is my camp and the outer trigram is the enemy's. If the inner trigram is thriving or assisting it is auspicious; Tai and Mo come next; if dead, imprisoned, resting or abandoned it is ominous. The eight states of the trigrams run through the eight solar terms: after the Start of Spring Gen thrives, Zhen assists, Xun is Tai, Li is Mo, Kun is dead, Dui imprisoned, Qian resting and Kan abandoned; after the Spring Equinox Zhen thrives, Xun assists, Li is Tai, Kun is Mo, Dui is dead, Qian imprisoned, Kan resting and Gen abandoned; after the Start of Summer Xun thrives, Li assists, Kun is Tai, Dui is Mo, Qian is dead, Kan imprisoned, Gen resting and Zhen abandoned; after the Summer Solstice Li thrives, Kun assists, Dui is Tai, Qian is Mo, Kan is dead, Gen imprisoned, Zhen resting and Xun abandoned; after the Start of Autumn Kun thrives, Dui assists, Qian is Tai, Kan is Mo, Gen is dead, Zhen imprisoned, Xun resting and Li abandoned; after the Autumn Equinox Dui thrives, Qian assists, Kan is Tai, Gen is Mo, Zhen is dead, Xun imprisoned, Li resting and Kun abandoned; after the Start of Winter Qian thrives, Kan assists, Gen is Tai, Zhen is Mo, Xun is dead, Li imprisoned, Kun resting and Dui abandoned; after the Winter Solstice Kan thrives, Gen assists, Zhen is Tai, Xun is Mo, Li is dead, Kun imprisoned, Dui resting and Qian abandoned.

**《易隐·征战占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2017 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining war", base text; Yi Yin, line 2017*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内卦为我寨，外卦为彼营也。 | 内卦是我方的营寨，外卦是对方的军营。 | The inner trigram is my camp and the outer trigram is the enemy's. |
| 内卦，旺相者，吉也，胎没次之，死囚伏废者，凶也。 | 内卦旺相的，吉；胎、没次一等；死、囚、伏、废的，凶。 | If the inner trigram is thriving or assisting, it is auspicious; Tai and Mo come next; if it is dead, imprisoned, hidden or abandoned, it is ominous. |
| 立春后艮旺震相巽胎离没坤死兑囚乾休坎废也。 | 立春以后：艮旺、震相、巽胎、离没、坤死、兑囚、乾休、坎废。 | After the Start of Spring: Gen thrives, Zhen assists, Xun is Tai, Li is Mo, Kun is dead, Dui imprisoned, Qian resting, Kan abandoned. |
| 春分后震旺巽相离胎坤没兑死乾囚坎休艮废也。 | 春分以后：震旺、巽相、离胎、坤没、兑死、乾囚、坎休、艮废。 | After the Spring Equinox: Zhen thrives, Xun assists, Li is Tai, Kun is Mo, Dui is dead, Qian imprisoned, Kan resting, Gen abandoned. |
| 立夏后巽旺离相坤胎兑没乾死坎囚艮休震废也。 | 立夏以后：巽旺、离相、坤胎、兑没、乾死、坎囚、艮休、震废。 | After the Start of Summer: Xun thrives, Li assists, Kun is Tai, Dui is Mo, Qian is dead, Kan imprisoned, Gen resting, Zhen abandoned. |
| 夏至后离旺坤相兑胎乾没坎死艮囚震休巽废也。 | 夏至以后：离旺、坤相、兑胎、乾没、坎死、艮囚、震休、巽废。 | After the Summer Solstice: Li thrives, Kun assists, Dui is Tai, Qian is Mo, Kan is dead, Gen imprisoned, Zhen resting, Xun abandoned. |
| 立秋后坤旺兑相乾胎坎没艮死震囚巽休离废也。 | 立秋以后：坤旺、兑相、乾胎、坎没、艮死、震囚、巽休、离废。 | After the Start of Autumn: Kun thrives, Dui assists, Qian is Tai, Kan is Mo, Gen is dead, Zhen imprisoned, Xun resting, Li abandoned. |
| 秋分后兑旺乾相坎胎艮没震死巽囚离休坤废也。 | 秋分以后：兑旺、乾相、坎胎、艮没、震死、巽囚、离休、坤废。 | After the Autumn Equinox: Dui thrives, Qian assists, Kan is Tai, Gen is Mo, Zhen is dead, Xun imprisoned, Li resting, Kun abandoned. |
| 立冬后乾旺坎相艮胎震没巽死离囚坤休兑废也。 | 立冬以后：乾旺、坎相、艮胎、震没、巽死、离囚、坤休、兑废。 | After the Start of Winter: Qian thrives, Kan assists, Gen is Tai, Zhen is Mo, Xun is dead, Li imprisoned, Kun resting, Dui abandoned. |
| 冬至后坎旺艮相震胎巽没离死坤囚兑休乾废也。 | 冬至以后：坎旺、艮相、震胎、巽没、离死、坤囚、兑休、乾废。 | After the Winter Solstice: Kan thrives, Gen assists, Zhen is Tai, Xun is Mo, Li is dead, Kun imprisoned, Dui resting, Qian abandoned. |

> **按**:第二句「死囚伏废」的「伏」，对照后面八张表里的「休」，应是「休」字抄错；译文照字面写成 hidden，没有改字。八种状态在朝廷占「大象」里也用到（见 YY.32），这里是书里唯一把它们按节气逐项排出来的地方。表里每一行的八个字，都是那一节气之后八个卦各自的状态，每一行里八个卦各出现一次。
>
> **Note:** In the second sentence the character 'fu', hidden, in 'dead, imprisoned, hidden, abandoned' is compared with 'xiu', resting, in the eight tables after it, and is presumably a miscopy for 'xiu'; the translation writes 'hidden' by the letter and does not change the character. The eight states are also used in the 'great image' of the court divination (see YY.32), and this is the only place in the book that sets them out one by one by solar term. In each row, the eight characters give the state of each of the eight trigrams after that solar term, and each trigram appears once in every row.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:异文(相似 0.9) 第 4728 行:立春：艮旺、震相、巽胎、离没、坤死、兑囚、乾休、坎废;异文(相似 0.9) 第 4728 行:春分：震旺、巽相、离胎、坤没、兑死、乾囚、坎休、艮废;异文(相似 0.9) 第 4729 行:立夏：巽旺、离相、坤胎、兑没、乾死、坎囚、艮休、震废;异文(相似 0.9) 第 4729 行:夏至：离旺、坤相、兑胎、乾没、坎死、艮囚、震休、巽废;异文(相似 0.9) 第 4730 行:立秋：坤旺、兑相、乾胎、坎没、艮死、震囚、巽休、离废;异文(相似 0.9) 第 4730 行:秋分：兑旺、乾相、坎胎、艮没、震死、巽囚、离休、坤废;异文(相似 0.9) 第 4731 行:立冬：乾旺、坎相、艮胎、震没、巽死、离囚、坤休、兑废;异文(相似 0.9) 第 4731 行:冬至：坎旺、艮相、震胎、巽没、离死、坤囚、兑休、乾废
- 《卜筮正宗》:异文(相似 0.95) 第 1107 行:立春后艮旺震相，巽胎离没，坤死兑囚，乾休坎废
- 《易林补遗》:异文(相似 0.95) 第 928 行:立春后，艮旺、震相、巽胎、离没、坤死、兑囚、乾休、坎废;异文(相似 0.95) 第 928 行:春分后，震旺、巽相、离胎、坤没、兑死、乾囚、坎休、艮废;异文(相似 0.95) 第 928 行:立夏后，巽旺、离相、坤胎、兑没、乾死、坎囚、艮休、震废;异文(相似 0.95) 第 928 行:夏至后，离旺，坤相、兑胎、乾没、坎死、艮囚、震休、巽废;异文(相似 0.95) 第 928 行:立秋后，坤旺、兑相、乾胎、坎没、艮死、震囚、巽休、离废;异文(相似 0.95) 第 928 行:秋分后，兑旺、乾相、坎胎、艮没、震死、巽囚、离休，坤废;异文(相似 0.95) 第 928 行:立冬后，乾旺、坎相、艮胎、震没、巽死、离囚、坤休、兑废;异文(相似 0.95) 第 928–930 行:冬至后，坎旺、艮相、震胎、巽没、离死、坤囚、兑休、乾废
